- **Fix:** In db.js saveCredential, distinguish an absent field from an explicit null: `const setLoginEnc = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec,'login_encrypted'); const setNotesEnc = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec,'notes_encrypted');` and in the UPDATE use `login_encrypted = CASE WHEN ? THEN ? ELSE login_encrypted END`, binding (setLoginEnc?1:0, loginEnc), and do the same for notes_encrypted. credentials-save always sets both keys, so an empty field clears them to NULL, while the auto-capture path (967) omits them and keeps the stored values.

#### A-100 🟡 Captured credentials: de-duplication uses the empty plaintext login, login is stored in plaintext, and updates reset folder and type _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:957` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Each login on a site followed by 'Save' creates another duplicate entry. The captured usernames sit in plaintext in cupnet.db until the next unlock's migration. Updating an item moves it out of its folder.
- **Fix:** In _saveCapturedCredential, compare `_plainLogin(ctx.db.getCredential(r.id)).trim() === login`, or decrypt the logins once per domain. On both paths, pass `login: ''` and `login_encrypted: login ? encryptCredentialFieldWithKey(login, vaultCrypto.requireUnlockedMasterKey()) : null`. On update, also pass `item_type: full.item_type` and `folder_id: full.folder_id`, and keep `notes_encrypted` through COALESCE by not changing it.

#### A-101 🟡 Import stores login and notes in plaintext; plaintext migration skips trashed rows and rows beyond 2000 _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:108` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user imports 5000 Bitwarden items. 5000 usernames and notes are written in plaintext. After unlock, only 2000 are migrated and 3000 stay readable in cupnet.db and backups. Trashed items are never migrated.
- **Fix:** In _insertCredentialFromImport, get `const key = vaultCrypto.requireUnlockedMasterKey()` and set `login: ''`, `login_encrypted: login ? encryptCredentialFieldWithKey(login, key) : null`, `notes: ''`, `notes_encrypted: notes ? encryptCredentialFieldWithKey(notes, key) : null`. In migratePlaintextCredentialFields, replace listCredentials with a direct query, for example a new db helper `SELECT id FROM credentials WHERE vault_id = ? AND ((login <> '' AND login_encrypted IS NULL) OR (notes <> '' AND notes_encrypted IS NULL))` that covers all rows, trashed ones included, with no limit.

#### A-102 🟡 Unlock without an explicit vaultId targets vaults[0], not the current profile's vault
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:272` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user is signed into profile B (id 2) and the screen locks. The Credentials list falls back to the unlock screen, and the user types B's password. The result is 'Invalid password', and each attempt counts toward vault A's rate limit, locking A for 30s/5min.
- **Fix:** In both handlers (and in credentials-vault-status), add a fallback to settingsStore.getActiveProfileVaultId() after _activeVaultId() and before vaults[0]. This works the same way _resolveCaptureVaultId does at credentials-ipc.js:49-56. For the renderers: when vaultCount>1, route credentials-renderer.js:544 to showScreen('picker') instead of 'unlock'. Pass the vaultId from profileSessionStatus or _activeProfileVaultId in doUnlock and submitCredPopupUnlock.

#### A-103 🟡 Unlocking or creating a vault from the Credentials window or toolbar silently switches the active profile, inconsistently
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:179` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** In profile A, the user creates vault 'Work' in the Credentials window, or unlocks vault B from the picker. Settings now save into profiles/B/, ui-prefs still come from A, notes are scoped to B, the main window still shows 'A', and A's key stays in memory.
- **Fix:** Decide between two designs. Option (a): in credentials-vault-switch/create/unlock, when the target id differs from profileSession.getActiveVaultId(), call vaultSession.lockAll('profile-switch') and then go through finishProfileLogin (export it from profile-ipc.js, or share a helper that calls applyProfileScopedStores + ensureProfileDir + broadcastProfileSessionChanged + onProfileLoggedIn). Option (b): stop treating vaults as profiles in these paths. Remove settingsStore.setActiveProfileVaultId from _unlockVaultRecord and make activateProfile in unlockVault opt-in (e.g. opts.activateProfile === true, set only by finishProfileLogin).
- _Reported 2× (also: x-ipc-contract)_

#### A-104 🟡 credentials-vault-delete: no auth for idle vaults, and deleting the active profile leaves dangling session/settings and orphaned data
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:311` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** After lock-screen, anyone at the main window opens Credentials, picks a rarely used profile and deletes it (confirm only). All of its credentials are gone. When the current profile's vault is deleted, settings keep writing into the deleted profile's folder.
- **Fix:** In credentials-vault-delete, always require the password and verify it through the rate limiter: call vaultSession._checkRateLimit/_recordUnlockFail, or expose a vaultSession.verifyPasswordRateLimited(vaultId, blob, pw). If vid === profileSession.getActiveVaultId() or vid === settingsStore.getActiveProfileVaultId(), run the profile-logout sequence before returning: lockAll, clearCredentialsVaultSession, setActiveProfileVaultId(null) on both stores, reload, broadcastProfileSessionChanged, onProfileSwitchGate. Extend db.deleteVault to DELETE FROM user_notes WHERE profile_id = ? (or reassign those notes), and remove profileSession.getProfileDir(vid) with fs.rmSync(..., {recursive:true, force:true}).

#### A-105 🟡 Encrypted export silently truncates to 2000 items and drops item type, URIs, custom fields and folders
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:1148` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user with 3000 items exports a backup and later restores it. 1000 items are missing, cards and identities come back as logins, and custom fields and URIs are gone.
- **Fix:** Replace line 1148 with a query that is not limited, for example a new db helper listAllCredentialIds(vaultId) that runs SELECT id FROM credentials WHERE vault_id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL. Add item_type, the folder name, the credential_uris rows and the decrypted custom fields to each exported item. Add a credentials-import-vault-export handler that decrypts the envelope and feeds its items to _insertCredentialFromImport, and make that function write item_type and the URIs as well.

#### A-111 🟡 Notes are unscoped whenever no profile is active: every profile's notes are listed and readable
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/notes-ipc.js:150` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The OS screen locks, lockAll runs and the main window stays. Opening Notes now lists the titles and plaintext bodies of every profile's notes. A note written during this time is invisible after the next sign-in.
- **Fix:** In notes-ipc.js add `function _requireProfileId(){ const pid = profileSession.getActiveVaultId(); if (pid == null) throw new Error('Profile locked'); return pid; }`. notes-list: return [] when there is no active profile, else pass profileId: pid. notes-get/notes-export/notes-delete/notes-pin/notes-save (when p.id is set): load the row and reject unless Number(row.profile_id) === pid. notes-save: use profile_id: _requireProfileId() instead of `|| 0`. Also apply the check to any other notes-* handlers that take a note id (link/unlink).
- _Reported 2× (also: tool-windows-b)_

#### A-112 🟡 migrateNotesToMasterKey is a no-op: listUserNotes never returns body_encrypted _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/notes-ipc.js:183` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user signs into a profile whose password matches their per-note password. Encrypted notes stay CN01 and still ask for the note password every time.
- **Fix:** In migrateNotesToMasterKey, iterate over full rows: add db.listUserNoteIdsForProfile(pid) (SELECT id FROM user_notes WHERE is_encrypted=1 AND profile_id=?) with no limit, or call listUserNotes({...filter, limit: 2000}) and then `const full = db.getUserNote(row.id)` and use full.body_encrypted. Pass page_url: full.page_url and profile_id to saveUserNote. Optionally have notes-save encrypt with encryptNotePayloadWithKey when a master key is present, so migrated notes do not revert to CN01.

#### A-117 🟡 profile-login (and vault delete / change-master) bypass the unlock rate limiter
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/profile-ipc.js:261` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A script or person at the gate can try unlimited passwords against any profile, limited only by Argon2 time. The rate-limit check in the manual checklist (item 8) fails for the gate.
- **Fix:** In profile-login, remove the verifyVaultPassword pre-check. Call vaultSession.unlockVault first (via finishProfileLogin), and run lockAll/clearCredentialsVaultSession only after unlock succeeds. The simplest way is to export a `verifyWithRateLimit(vaultId, verifyBlob, pw)` from vault-session-service that wraps _checkRateLimit, verifyVaultPassword and _recordUnlockFail/_recordUnlockSuccess. Use it in profile-login before lockAll, and in credentials-vault-delete and credentials-change-master.
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-135 🟡 Paste-unlock blocks page copy/paste/contextmenu handlers for every site and is fingerprintable
- **Where:** `main-process/services/paste-unlock.js:10` · **Category:** compat · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Pasting an image or formatted text into a web editor inserts raw DOM and desyncs the editor model. Right-click opens the browser menu instead of the app's menu. Cloudflare-type scripts can flag the browser via the global.
- **Fix:** Default pasteUnlock to false in settings-store.js:25, or make it a per-site allowlist. Change the script so it does not stop propagation. For example, patch Event.prototype.preventDefault so that it becomes a no-op only for clipboard events whose target is not contenteditable or a rich editor, or only on sites the user enabled. Remove the window.__cupnetPasteUnlocked global and use a closure-scoped guard, or inject the script into an isolated world.

#### A-137 🟡 The profile switch gate can be bypassed: dismissing it or using app-menu accelerators gives full access without a profile
- **Where:** `main-process/services/profile-gate-window.js:95` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** User A clicks 'Switch profile' and walks away. Someone presses Alt+F4 on the gate, or Cmd+Shift+N on macOS. The main window with A's tabs and cookies comes back, and the Notes window lists every profile's notes (see the unscoped notes finding).
- **Fix:** In profile-gate-window 'closed': if !profileSession.isProfileActive() and !forceAppQuit, either reopen the gate or call app.quit(), instead of mainWindow.show(). In openProfileSwitchGate call Menu.setApplicationMenu(null), or disable the tool items, and restore the menu in openMainAfterLogin. Add a guard in _spawnTrustedToolWindow/the create*Window functions that returns early when !profileSession.isProfileActive() && !shouldSkipProfileGate(). In the vaultSession.onLock callback, call profileGateApi.openProfileSwitchGate() when the gate is enabled.

#### A-150 🟡 Idle auto-lock never fires while a profile is active, contradicting SECURITY.md
- **Where:** `main-process/services/vault-session-service.js:55` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user leaves the app unattended for hours with vaultAutoLockMinutes=15. The vault key stays in memory, and autofill and credential reads keep working.
- **Fix:** Choose one. Option (a): make the code match the docs. Drop the isProfileActive() early return and have the idle callback run the logout path: lockAll('idle'), then trigger the profile gate through an onLock/onIdle hook in cupnet-runtime that calls profileGateApi.openProfileSwitchGate. Call vaultSession.configure({autoLockMinutes: settingsStore.getCached()?.vaultAutoLockMinutes ?? 15}) at the end of applyProfileScopedStores, and flip the first test in vault-auto-lock.test.mjs. Option (b): if the behaviour is intended, update SECURITY.md lines 12/32-33/42/54 to say idle lock does not apply while a profile is signed in, and hide or relabel the vaultAutoLockMinutes setting.

#### A-229 ⚪ Change-master modal always overwrites the vault hint, wiping it when the hint field is left empty _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `credentials-renderer.js:1429` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user changes the master password without typing a new hint, and the existing hint is erased.
- **Fix:** Line 1429: `if (hint) await api.credentialsVaultSetHint(hint);`. Alternatively, pre-fill cm-hint with the current hint when the modal opens so that clearing it is deliberate.

#### A-230 ⚪ DOMContentLoaded handler throws ReferenceError: setAutolockSelectFromStorage is not defined _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `credentials-renderer.js:1485` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Each time the Credentials window opens, sysLog gets an error entry. Any code added after this line would never run.
- **Fix:** Delete line 1485 (`setAutolockSelectFromStorage();`).

#### A-231 ⚪ Clipboard auto-clear lives only in the Credentials window and can wipe unrelated clipboard content _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `credentials-renderer.js:260` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user copies a password and closes the Credentials window. The password stays on the system clipboard (and in clipboard history managers) forever.
- **Fix:** Add a main-process IPC handler (e.g. 'credentials-clipboard-write') that calls electron clipboard.writeText(value) and starts a main-process timer. When the timer fires, it clears the clipboard only if clipboard.readText() === value. Call it from clipboardWrite through preload instead of navigator.clipboard.

#### A-232 ⚪ CSV import breaks on quoted fields containing newlines
- **Where:** `credentials-renderer.js:166` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** An item with a two-line note is imported as one truncated item plus a bogus item whose login/password come from the note's continuation line.
- **Fix:** Replace parseCsvText with a single-pass character parser over the whole text. It keeps inQ across '\n'/'\r\n', ends a field on ',' and a row on a newline outside quotes, and handles '""' escapes. It then drops rows whose cells are all empty. No split before parsing.

#### A-233 ⚪ Passphrase generator uses a 437-word list mislabelled as EFF; the password shuffle reuses random values
- **Where:** `credentials-renderer.js:63` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Users trust generated 4-word passphrases that can be brute-forced offline in hours.
- **Fix:** Replace EFF_WORDS with the full EFF large wordlist (7776 words), or at least raise the default word count to 6+ and label the list accurately. Pick words with rejection sampling: draw a Uint32 and reject values >= floor(2^32/len)*len. In generatePassword, draw a second Uint32Array(len) for the shuffle indices, and use rejection sampling for character selection as well.

#### A-234 ⚪ UI copy and client checks say 8 characters but the server requires 12
- **Where:** `credentials.html:636` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user enters a 9-character password as instructed, and the server rejects it.
- **Fix:** Change credentials.html:636 and :1017 and profile-gate.html:698 to say 12. Change credentials-renderer.js:1140 to `if (pw.length < 12) { ... 'At least 12 characters' }`, and apply the same bound to the change-master-password client check if it has one. Ideally expose MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH through preload or an IPC call so the value lives in one place.

#### A-270 ⚪ credential-form-captured is unguarded and page-controllable; a single global pending capture can be swapped _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:1006` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Tab 1 shows the save bar for bank.com. A background tab on evil.com calls reportCapture({login:'x',password:'y'}) in a loop. The user clicks Save and stores evil.com's data. The pending real capture (including its password) is overwritten, and save-bar prompts can be spammed. The global is also fingerprintable by anti-bot scripts.
- **Fix:** Give each capture a random id, store captures in a Map keyed by id, and send the id in show-credential-save-bar. Require that id in credential-capture-confirm, dismiss and unlock-and-save. In the credential-form-captured handler, drop reports unless the sender's tab is the active tab (ctx.tabManager.getActiveTab()?.id === tabId), and rate-limit per webContents (for example, 1 report per 2s). Longer term, run the capture script in an isolated world (webFrame.executeJavaScriptInIsolatedWorld) so that cupnetCredentials is not in the main world.

#### A-271 ⚪ Switching to an already-unlocked but non-active vault still requires a password
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:291` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Vaults A and B are both unlocked and B is active. The user selects A in the switcher, nothing happens (error 'Password required'), and the dropdown shows the wrong vault.
- **Fix:** In credentials-vault-switch, change the early return to `if (vaultSession.isVaultUnlocked(vid)) { vaultSession.setActiveVault(vid); settingsStore.setActiveProfileVaultId(vid); _toolbarRefresh(); return { success: true, vaultId: vid }; }`. setActiveVault is a new small export in vault-session-service.js: it sets _activeVaultId = vid if _vaultKeys has vid, calls profileSession.activateProfile({ vaultId: vid, name: _vaultKeys.get(vid).name }), then calls _scheduleIdleLock(). Also call refreshVaultSwitcher() in the renderer's unlocked branch when the switch fails.

#### A-272 ⚪ Item and folder handlers don't check the item belongs to the active vault (cross-vault IDOR)
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/credentials-ipc.js:361` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A compromised trusted renderer, or a stale id after a vault switch, reads another profile's labels, domains and legacy plaintext login/notes. It can also delete or move that profile's items without unlocking it.
- **Fix:** Add a helper in credentials-ipc.js: function _ownedCredential(id){ const r = ctx.db.getCredential(id); if (!r || Number(r.vault_id) !== Number(_activeVaultId())) throw new Error('Not found'); return r; }. Call it at the top of credentials-get, credentials-save when p.id is set, and credentials-delete, -restore, -favorite, -move-to-folder, -uris-get/save and -fields-get/save. Add the same check for folders (credential_folders.vault_id must equal the active vault) in folder-rename, folder-delete and move-to-folder, where the folderId must also belong to the vault. In credentials-vault-rename, require Number(vaultId) === _activeVaultId().

#### A-308 ⚪ Migration state file is written non-atomically; losing it forces setup mode (password reset path) _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/services/profile-data-migration.js:26` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The app is force-killed while marking setup complete. On the next start the gate asks for 'first-time setup', and completing it destroys access to the vault's data.
- **Fix:** In profile-data-migration.js, replace fs.writeFileSync(getMigrationStatePath(), ...) in writeMigrationState with require('./secure-fs.js').writeFileSecure(getMigrationStatePath(), JSON.stringify(state, null, 2)), which does an atomic tmp+rename. For defence in depth, make readMigrationState tell 'file missing' apart from 'file corrupt'. On corrupt, or when needsProfileSetup would otherwise return true, treat setup as complete if the primary vault's verify_blob is already CV02 and its name is not 'Default'. That way a lost state file never routes an established vault into profile-complete-setup.

#### A-321 ⚪ Profile login runs up to 5 Argon2id (64 MiB) derivations
- **Where:** `main-process/services/vault-session-service.js:127` · **Category:** perf · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** On slower machines, sign-in takes several seconds and allocates about 320 MiB of transient memory.
- **Fix:** Add deriveAndVerify(verifyBlob, password) in credentials-crypto.js. For CV02 it derives the key once from blob[5..21], decrypts the marker with it, and returns the key on success or null on failure. For CV01 it verifies with scrypt and then derives the Argon2 key. Use it in unlockVault in place of the separate verifyVaultPassword and deriveSessionKeyFromVerifyBlob calls. Drop the pre-check in profile-login (profile-ipc.js:263) and rely on unlockVault's result. For the upgrade, build the new CV02 blob with the same salt that produced the session key (blob[5..21]), or re-encrypt all CV02 fields after swapping the blob, so the key stays stable across logins.

#### A-388 ⚪ profile-avatars unit test uses node:test, so `npm run test:unit` (vitest) fails
- **Where:** `tests/unit/profile-avatars.test.mjs:3` · **Category:** test-infra · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — same node:test→vitest fix (see A-058).
- **Impact:** `npm run test:unit` (vitest run) exits non-zero, which breaks CI and hides the avatar tests.
- **Fix:** Change line 3 to `import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';`. Either keep node:assert/strict (vitest accepts thrown assertion errors) or convert assert.equal(a,b) to expect(a).toBe(b).
- _Reported 2× (also: proxy)_

#### A-389 ⚪ TOTP treats otpauth:// URIs as base32 and ignores the per-item algorithm/digits/period
- **Where:** `totp-generator.js:11` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user imports Bitwarden items whose totp is an otpauth URI. The TOTP shown and copied is wrong, and 2FA login fails.
- **Fix:** In totp-generator.js generate(): if the secret starts with /^otpauth:\/\//i, parse it with new URL(). Take secret, algorithm, digits and period from searchParams, with explicit opts taking precedence, then decode only that secret. This fixes every call site at once without changing the storage format.

### Tools B (settings/dns/cookies/notes)

#### A-003 🔴 Any website can read every cookie in its partition, including HttpOnly cookies of other sites, and can wipe any tab's cookie jar _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/cookies-dns-ipc.js:70` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A malicious page open in the active tab runs `await electronAPI.getCookies(null, {})` and gets the HttpOnly session cookies of every other site logged in within the same cookie group (for example the Default group). It can also call `electronAPI.getTabs()` and then `electronAPI.clearCookies(id)` for each tab to log the user out everywhere.
- **Fix:** In get-cookies, when the owned-tab branch matches (sender is a tab view), ignore the caller's filter. Return only non-HttpOnly cookies for sender.getURL(): `cookies.get({url: event.sender.getURL()})` filtered to !c.httpOnly. Better: expose only a count to new-tab.html, or drop getCookies/clearCookies from preload-view.js and guard both with assertTrustedShell. Wrap remove-cookie, clear-cookies and consolidate-cookies in guardIpcHandler, or route them through _cookieSessionTab and allow only a sender clearing its own tab.

#### A-038 🟠 Cookie consolidation is not public-suffix aware and deletes before writing, so it can destroy cookies of unrelated sites
- **Where:** `main-process/services/session-cookie-utils.js:172` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With the Cookie Manager 'Current tab' filter locked to www.bbc.co.uk (or the domain field set to example.co.uk), click Consolidate and confirm. Every same-name cookie from all *.co.uk sites in the first group is expired, the set fails, and the status shows an error; those logins are gone. A __Host- cookie on a subdomain fails the same way.
- **Fix:** In consolidateCookiesToParent, compute parentBare = tldts.getDomain(anchor) (and refuse when it is null or equals tldts.getPublicSuffix(anchor)), or keep computeParentDomain but throw when the result equals the public suffix. Skip cookies whose name starts with '__Host-' (leave them untouched). For each group, call sess.cookies.set(buildSetDetailsForDomain(canonical, parentDomain)) FIRST, inside try/catch; remove the narrower members only after the set succeeds, and on failure record the error and continue with the next group. Restrict the post-sweep (L299-306) to names that were merged successfully.
- _Reported 2× (also: tabs-shell-main)_

#### A-043 🟠 An autosave in flight re-binds the editor to the old note id, so the next edit overwrites a different note
- **Where:** `notes-renderer.js:825` · **Category:** race · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — capture savingId before notesSave; skip currentId re-bind if the user navigated away (notes-renderer.js).
- **Impact:** Type in note A. When autosave fires (1.5s idle), immediately click 'New' and confirm. notes-save resolves and sets currentId=A. The user writes a new note in the blank editor, and autosave sends {id: A, …}, replacing note A's title and body with the new text. The same happens when switching to note B: B's content is later autosaved over A.
- **Fix:** In saveNoteInternal, capture `const gen = ++saveGen; const saveForId = currentId;` at the start. Increment saveGen in newNote, selectNote, selectNoteSkipDirtyCheck and deleteNote. After `await api.notesSave(...)`, if `gen !== saveGen || currentId !== saveForId`, just `await loadList(); return;` without touching currentId, pwdCache, meta or the baseline. Otherwise continue as today, and re-check the generation after each later await before calling updateMeta or scheduleBaselineAfterLoad.
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-052 🟠 Settings page cannot load or save anything: settings.html runs in a tab whose preload-view.js no longer exposes the settings APIs _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `settings-renderer.js:1053` · **Category:** ipc-contract · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Press Ctrl/Cmd+, to open Settings. init() reaches `await api.getSettingsAll()` (L1053), which throws 'api.getSettingsAll is not a function'. The catch shows 'Failed to load settings' and every field stays at its HTML default. Every later edit calls a missing saver, gets a TypeError and shows 'Could not save'. Devices, Performance and CA download silently do nothing because of the `if (!api.x) return` guards. With CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL>=6, window.electronAPI is undefined.
- **Fix:** Do not re-add these APIs to preload-view.js. Open settings.html in a trusted BrowserWindow that loads preload.js and calls registerTrustedShell, following the other tool windows in sub-windows.js. Change open-settings-tab and the cupnet://settings navigation (tabs-ipc.js:22-39, 66) to open or focus that window, and wrap the settings IPC handlers in settings-toolbar-ipc.js with guardIpcHandler.

#### A-069 🟡 Cookie group New/Copy/Rename buttons throw because window.prompt is unsupported in Electron
- **Where:** `cookie-manager-renderer.js:69` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** In Cookie Manager, click '+', 'Cp' or 'Ren'. An uncaught exception is logged and nothing happens, so cookie groups cannot be created, copied or renamed from this window.
- **Fix:** Add a small DOM modal to cookie-manager.html, like notes.html #cn-req-prompt, exposed as `askText(label, defaultValue): Promise<string|null>`. Replace the three prompt() calls with `await askText(...)`.

#### A-070 🟡 Editing a cookie turns host-only cookies into domain cookies and leaves the original entry as a duplicate
- **Where:** `cookie-manager-renderer.js:472` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open a host-only 'sid' cookie on example.com, change its value and save. The jar now has 'sid' on example.com (old value) and on .example.com (new value), and the site keeps sending both.
- **Fix:** In the save handler: `const keepHostOnly = editingCookie?.hostOnly || name.startsWith('__Host-'); if (keepHostOnly) delete details.domain;` Keep `url` built from the domain without its leading dot. Validate the expiry with `const exp = Number(expiresRaw); if (expiresRaw && !Number.isFinite(exp)) { setStatus('Expires must be a Unix timestamp', 'err'); return; }`. After `result.success`, if editingCookie exists and its name, domain, path or hostOnly differs from the new values, call `await api.removeCookie(activeTabId, editingCookie)`. Optionally, force `secure` when sameSite === 'no_restriction'.

#### A-071 🟡 Netscape cookie import skips #HttpOnly_ lines and keeps CR characters; export drops HttpOnly
- **Where:** `cookie-manager-renderer.js:616` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Import a cookies.txt exported by curl or a browser extension: all HttpOnly session cookies are silently skipped, and on Windows-made files every value gains a trailing '\r' (set fails or the site rejects it). Exporting and re-importing from CupNet turns HttpOnly cookies into JS-readable ones.
- **Fix:** Import: `for (let line of text.split(/\r?\n/)) { let httpOnly = false; if (line.startsWith('#HttpOnly_')) { httpOnly = true; line = line.slice(10); } else if (line.startsWith('#') || !line.trim()) continue; ... cookies.push({ domain: parts[1] === 'TRUE' ? parts[0] : undefined, /* url built from parts[0] */ path: parts[2], secure: parts[3]==='TRUE', httpOnly, expirationDate: Number(parts[4]) || undefined, name: parts[5], value: parts[6] }) }`. Build the URL from the raw host in parts[0] rather than from c.domain. Export: `const dom = (c.httpOnly ? '#HttpOnly_' : '') + sanitize(c.domain);`.

#### A-113 🟡 Notes formatting is lost on save: Quill 2 lists, checklists, underline, strike, colors and code blocks do not survive Turndown
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/notes-ipc.js:60` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Create a checklist with one checked item, a bullet list, a struck-through word and a code block, then wait for autosave and reopen the note (or save an encrypted note, which reloads immediately). The lists are numbered, the check state is gone, strike/underline/colors are gone, and the code is plain paragraphs.
- **Fix:** Store the Quill Delta (JSON.stringify(quill.getContents())) or sanitized Quill HTML instead of markdown. Keep markdown only for export, and load with quill.setContents or quill.clipboard.convert. Minimal alternative: add turndown-plugin-gfm (strikethrough) plus custom rules. Map li[data-list=bullet] to '- ', checked/unchecked to '- [x] '/'- [ ] ', and s to ~~ ~~. Keep u and colored spans as raw HTML. Join consecutive div.ql-code-block lines into one fenced block. On load, convert the GFM task and bullet output back to Quill's data-list format.
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-114 🟡 Master-key (CN02) notes cannot be unlocked with a password, exported, duplicated or saved without a downgrade _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/notes-ipc.js:267` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Log in to a profile whose password matches a note's password; the note migrates to CN02. Lock the vault and open the note, then enter the correct password: 'Wrong password', and the note is permanently locked until the vault is unlocked. With the vault unlocked, Export > .md shows 'Unlock the note to export' for an already open note.
- **Fix:** In notes-get's password branch and in notes-export, replace the direct decryptNotePayload call with `_decryptNoteBody(row, pw)`. In notes-export, only require a password when `!vaultSession.getMasterKey()` or the buffer is not CN02. Return `enc_format: 'CN02'|'CN01'` from notes-get. In the renderer, allow export, duplicate and save without a password when enc_format === 'CN02'. In notes-save, when is_encrypted is set, no password was given and a master key exists, encrypt with `encryptNotePayloadWithKey(JSON.stringify({ body: bodyMd }), key)` instead of throwing, so CN02 notes are never downgraded.

#### A-115 🟡 Request cards copy decrypted headers, cookies and bodies into plaintext notes, bypassing traffic-log encryption and redaction
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/notes-ipc.js:110` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With trafficLogEncryption=encrypt, embed a logged-in request into a normal note. The session cookie and bearer token now sit unencrypted in the SQLite user_notes table and in any exported file.
- **Fix:** In normalizeRequestRowForNotesEmbed, run the header objects through the existing redactor before returning: `const { redactHeaders, redactBody } = require('../../../bundle-utils.js');`, then set `requestHeaders: reqH && typeof reqH === 'object' ? redactHeaders(reqH, 'Balanced', {redactedFieldsCount:0,fields:[]}, 'request_headers') : reqH` and do the same for responseHeaders, applying redactBody to the bodies. Optionally, skip redaction when the target note is encrypted, and have db.js:1914 strip ```cupnet fences from the preview.

#### A-162 🟡 Note URL patterns: '*.example.com' never matches, '?' is not escaped, and a path in a host pattern is ignored
- **Where:** `note-domain-utils.js:36` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — scheme-less patterns match subdomains/paths; regression tests added.
- **Impact:** A note bound to '*.example.com' (the most natural wildcard) never appears under 'This site' on a.example.com. A note bound to 'example.com/admin' shows on every example.com page.
- **Fix:** In matchGlobPattern add '?' to the escape class: `/[.+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g`. In noteMatchesUrlMatch, when p has no scheme, split it into hostPart = p.split('/')[0] and pathPart = the rest. Match the host with `hostPart.startsWith('*.') ? (host === base || host.endsWith('.'+base)) : (hostPart.includes('*') ? matchGlobPattern(hostPart, host) : host===hostPart || host.endsWith('.'+hostPart))`. When a pathPart exists, also require matchGlobPattern('/'+pathPart, u.pathname + u.search). Add unit tests for these cases.

#### A-163 🟡 A tab switch or navigation overwrites the draft's Site pattern and resets the dirty baseline, so unsaved draft text and embedded blocks are never saved
- **Where:** `notes-renderer.js:1193` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** In a new draft, type a title, body and pattern '*.example.com/admin*', then switch browser tabs within 1.5s. The pattern is replaced by 'othersite.com', the draft is marked clean and never autosaved, and closing the window loses it. Likewise, 'Embed in note' from the Log Viewer into an open window with an unsaved draft inserts a card that is never persisted.
- **Fix:** In onNotesContextUpdate: const prevDomain = ctxDomain (capture before reassigning). Only when !currentId && !isDirtyNow() && el.match.value === prevDomain, set el.match.value = ctxDomain, update the draft summary, and call captureBaseline() or scheduleBaselineAfterLoad. Otherwise leave match and baseline alone. In onNotesEmbedBlock, if _loading is true, defer: setTimeout(() => markDirty(), 50), or re-run markDirty after the baseline callback, so the embed is counted as a change.

#### A-164 🟡 Duplicate silently discards unsaved edits of the current note
- **Where:** `notes-renderer.js:944` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — duplicateNote flushes unsaved edits first.
- **Impact:** Edit note A, and within 1.5s (or at any time for an encrypted note, which never autosaves) click Duplicate. The copy has the old text, the editor switches to the copy, and the edits made to A are lost.
- **Fix:** At the top of duplicateNote, after the guards: if (isDirtyNow()) { try { await saveNoteInternal(); } catch (e) { setErr(formatNotesIpcError(e)); return; } } then continue as before, so the row fetched from the database includes the edits. Alternatively, keep the autosave timer running and ask for confirmation, as selectNote does.

#### A-165 🟡 beforeunload silently blocks closing the Notes window and quitting the app while a note is dirty
- **Where:** `notes-renderer.js:1213` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open an encrypted note, edit one character and click the window's close button: nothing happens. Pressing Cmd+Q to quit CupNet is also aborted with no message.
- **Fix:** In createNotesWindow (sub-windows.js), after spawning, add: d.notesWindow.webContents.on('will-prevent-unload', (e) => { const c = d.dialog.showMessageBoxSync(d.notesWindow, { type: 'question', buttons: ['Stay', 'Leave'], defaultId: 0, cancelId: 0, message: 'You have unsaved note changes. Leave without saving?' }); if (c === 1) e.preventDefault(); }). For the quit path, before destroying the notes window in destroyAllSecondaryWindows or before-quit, either ask the renderer to save (IPC with a timeout) or warn the user, because destroy() currently drops unsaved notes without a word.

#### A-166 🟡 Turning Encrypt off on an encrypted note auto-saves it as plaintext 1.5s later with no confirmation
- **Where:** `notes-renderer.js:1128` · **Category:** security · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — autosave blocked for enc→plain; manual save confirms.
- **Impact:** On an unlocked encrypted note, accidentally click the lock toggle (btn-lock-toggle). Within 1.5s the note is decrypted to plaintext in SQLite with no prompt, and turning the toggle back on does not restore the ciphertext without a new password.
- **Fix:** In scheduleAutosave and doAutosave, add `|| (baseline && baseline.enc && !el.chkEnc.checked)` to the early-return guards so autosave never runs an encrypted-to-plain transition. In saveNoteInternal's non-encrypted branch, when `baseline?.enc` is true, require `confirm('Remove encryption from this note? It will be stored as plain text.')` and throw or abort if the user cancels.

#### A-193 🟡 Log-filter patterns with regex metacharacters are compiled unescaped, and a failed compile leaves the old filters active
- **Where:** `utils.js:285` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Add the line '*c++*' to the filters: the save reports success, one CDP network event throws (unhandled rejection), and from then on the NEW list is never applied; the previous filters stay in effect until restart or a new save.
- **Fix:** In utils.js shouldFilterUrl, build the regexes safely and assign the cache last: `const compiled=[]; for (const p of patterns) { if (typeof p!=='string'||!p) continue; const src=p.replace(/[.+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&').replace(/\*/g,'.*'); try { compiled.push(new RegExp(src,'i')); } catch {} } _compiledFilters=compiled; _lastFilterPatterns=patterns;`. Optionally, save-filter-patterns can also filter out non-string entries.
- _Reported 2× (also: core-runtime)_

#### A-224 ⚪ The Cookie Manager domain filter has no label boundary, so it shows cookies from unrelated hosts and differs from what 'Clear all' deletes
- **Where:** `cookie-manager-renderer.js:302` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With the Current-tab filter on www.example.com, the table also lists cookies from le.com. With a manual filter 'google', the table shows google.com cookies, but 'Clear all' deletes 0 and reports success.
- **Fix:** In applyFilters, replace the domain check with the same predicate as main: const needle = sDomain.replace(/^\./,''); const hay = cd.replace(/^\./,''); if (!(hay === needle || hay.endsWith('.'+needle) || needle.endsWith('.'+hay))) return false;. If you want to keep substring search, make it a separate mode and turn off Clear-all (or send the exact matched cookies) while it is on.

#### A-225 ⚪ Sorting the Cookie table by Expires does nothing
- **Where:** `cookie-manager-renderer.js:322` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click 'Expires': the order does not change.
- **Fix:** In sortCookies: const key = sortCol === 'expires' ? 'expirationDate' : sortCol; if (key === 'expirationDate') { const na = a.expirationDate ?? Infinity, nb = b.expirationDate ?? Infinity; return sortAsc ? na - nb : nb - na; }. Otherwise keep the string compare. In the click handler, first loop over all th[data-col] and strip /[▲▼]\s*/g from their textContent, then add the arrow to the clicked header.

#### A-226 ⚪ Cookie expiry dates use the Russian locale, breaking the English-UI rule
- **Where:** `cookie-manager-renderer.js:353` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Every expiry in the table shows as dd.mm.yyyy in Russian formatting regardless of the UI language.
- **Fix:** Replace 'ru-RU' with 'en-GB' in both calls, or use undefined/ISO formatting, e.g. d.toISOString().slice(0,16).replace('T',' ').

#### A-227 ⚪ 'Value copied' is shown even when the clipboard write fails
- **Where:** `cookie-manager-renderer.js:435` · **Category:** error-handling · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** When the window is unfocused or clipboard permission is denied, the status says 'Value copied to clipboard' but nothing was copied.
- **Fix:** Make it async: try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text || ''); setStatus('Value copied to clipboard','ok',1500); } catch { setStatus('Copy failed','error',2500); }. Callers do not use the return value.

#### A-236 ⚪ Filtering by a tag that contains a space never matches, and choosing a tag shrinks the tag dropdown
- **Where:** `db.js:1934` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Tag a note 'work items' and pick 'work items' in the tag filter: the list is empty. Pick tag 'a': the dropdown now only offers tags that co-occur with 'a', so switching directly to tag 'b' is impossible.
- **Fix:** db.js: `const tagRaw = filter.tag != null ? String(filter.tag).replace(/\s+/g,'').toLowerCase() : '';`. Alternatively, strip the spaces around commas in SQL instead of removing all spaces. notes-renderer.js: fill _listRowsAllForTags from a separate `api.notesList({ limit: 500 })` call with no tag or search filter, or cache the tag set from an unfiltered load.

#### A-240 ⚪ The DNS IPv4 validator accepts leading zeros, which WHATWG URL reads as octal, so traffic goes to a different IP
- **Where:** `dns-manager-renderer.js:53` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — IPv4 validator rejects leading zeros.
- **Impact:** Save a rule 'api.test' to 010.001.002.003 meaning 10.1.2.3. MITM connects to 8.1.2.3, so requests go to an unrelated public host.
- **Fix:** In both isValidIpv4 implementations, reject leading zeros: `return value.split('.').every(p => /^(0|[1-9]\d{0,2})$/.test(p) && Number(p) <= 255);`. Alternatively, normalize each octet with String(Number(p)) before saving, both in dns-overrides-save and in mitm setDnsOverrides.

#### A-241 ⚪ A 'New rule' for a host that already exists silently overwrites that rule (upsert), including its legacy CORS flag
- **Where:** `dns-manager-renderer.js:370` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A legacy rule api.x.com has CORS injection and IP 1.1.1.1. Clicking '+ New' and saving api.x.com → 2.2.2.2 clears the CORS flag and replaces the IP without asking.
- **Fix:** In the renderer save handler, before calling api.saveDnsOverride, when !editingRule and allRules.some(r => String(r.host).toLowerCase() === host), either refuse with setStatus('Rule for this host already exists — edit it instead','err') or confirm() and then send that rule's id. Alternatively, in dns-overrides-save, when id is null and a row with that host exists, return {success:false, error:'Host already exists'}.

#### A-242 ⚪ The DNS Activity tab re-renders the whole feed once per event
- **Where:** `dns-manager-renderer.js:400` · **Category:** perf · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** On a busy page with DNS rules and the Activity tab open, a batch of 100 events triggers 100 full rebuilds of 300 rows, which visibly janks the window.
- **Fix:** Split addActivityEvent into pushActivityEntry(info) (push, trim to 300, no render) and a render step. In onDnsRuleMatchedBatch, push every item, then call updateActivityCount() once and renderActivity() once if the tab is active. Replace the per-button listeners with one delegated click listener on activityLog that matches e.target.closest('.act-link').

#### A-283 ⚪ Notes md/txt export writes raw ```cupnet JSON (with all headers and bodies) instead of the documented exportText
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/notes-ipc.js:379` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Export a note with a request card as .txt: the file contains a single-line JSON blob with the full Cookie/Authorization headers instead of the readable '=REQ=====' block.
- **Fix:** In notes-ipc.js, `const { cnBlockExportText } = require('../../../notes-cn-block-export.js');` and add `function expandCnBlocks(md){ return String(md).replace(/```cupnet\n([\s\S]*?)\n```/g, (m, j) => { try { return cnBlockExportText(JSON.parse(j)); } catch { return m; } }); }`. In notes-export, apply `bodyMd = expandCnBlocks(bodyMd)` before the md/txt branches. Header redaction (Cookie, Authorization, Set-Cookie) is an optional separate hardening step in exporters.request.

#### A-339 ⚪ Request card render puts unescaped payload fields (timing, requestId) into innerHTML
- **Where:** `notes-quill-custom-blocks.js:266` · **Category:** security · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — note request-card fields escaped.
- **Impact:** A cupnet fence with "timing":"<a href=//evil>click</a>" renders a live link inside the card after reload. CSP blocks scripts, but HTML injection into the editor DOM still happens.
- **Fix:** In normalize: `requestId: v.requestId == null ? null : String(v.requestId)` and `timing: Number.isFinite(Number(v.timing)) ? Number(v.timing) : null` (same for responseSize). In render use `metaParts.push(esc(p.timing) + ' ms')` and `metaParts.push('ID ' + esc(p.requestId))`.

#### A-340 ⚪ The password is trimmed when setting it but not when unlocking, so passwords with leading or trailing spaces can never unlock
- **Where:** `notes-renderer.js:795` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — save no longer trims the password (symmetric with unlock).
- **Impact:** Set the password ' secret ' (with spaces) and save; the note is encrypted with 'secret'. Reopen it, type exactly ' secret ', and get 'Wrong password'.
- **Fix:** Treat the password the same way in both places. The simplest fix is to stop trimming at notes-renderer.js:795 (`const p1 = el.pw1.value;`) and keep rejecting an empty or whitespace-only value, e.g. `if (p1 && p1.trim())`. If you keep the trim, also use `el.unlockPw.value.trim()` at line 717. Either way, existing notes saved with trimmed passwords still open with the trimmed form.

#### A-341 ⚪ Notes find bar highlights the wrong text once the note contains images or request cards
- **Where:** `notes-renderer.js:1018` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** In a note with one request card followed by text, search for a word after the card: the selection is shifted one character left for each embed before it.
- **Fix:** Build text and an index map from the delta: `let text='', map=[]; let idx=0; for (const op of quill.getContents().ops) { if (typeof op.insert === 'string') { for (let k=0;k<op.insert.length;k++){ map.push(idx+k); } text += op.insert; idx += op.insert.length; } else { idx += 1; } }`. Search in text and push `{ index: map[i], length: map[i+q.length-1] - map[i] + 1 }`.

#### A-372 ⚪ The Theme and UI scale settings have no effect
- **Where:** `settings-renderer.js:973` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Choose Light theme or 125% scale: the pref is saved and 'All changes saved' is shown, but nothing changes visually in Settings or anywhere else.
- **Fix:** Either remove/hide the Theme and UI scale controls, or implement them: add a :root[data-settings-theme=light] token block (plus a prefers-color-scheme block for 'system') in settings.css/shared-dark-theme.css, convert settings.css font sizes to rem, and have the other windows read uiTheme through getUiPref and apply the same attribute.

#### A-373 ⚪ Settings search hides matching cards that are in other tabs, so a search from the wrong tab finds nothing
- **Where:** `settings-renderer.js:730` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** On the General tab, search 'certificate': every General card is hidden and the page is empty, although the Certificate tab has a matching card.
- **Fix:** In applySettingsHubFilter, after computing matches, count hits per `.sp-tab-body` and toggle a `has-match` class on the matching `.sp-tab-btn[data-sp-tab]`. If q is non-empty and the active body has zero hits, call switchTab() on the first body with hits. Guard against recursion, because switchTab calls applySettingsHubFilter.

#### A-374 ⚪ Vault auto-lock is silently saved as 'Never' when the stored value isn't one of the dropdown options
- **Where:** `settings-renderer.js:850` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With vaultAutoLockMinutes:120 in settings.json, open Settings > Security and change 'Storage mode'. Auto-lock is now disabled without the user touching it.
- **Fix:** In init (L1091), if no option has value String(mins), append `new Option(mins + ' minutes', String(mins))` before assigning. In scheduleSecuritySave, only include vaultAutoLockMinutes when `spVaultAutolock.selectedIndex >= 0 && spVaultAutolock.value !== ''`. The handler already skips it when the field is null or undefined.

#### A-375 ⚪ After a failed Settings load, blurring a field or pressing Ctrl+S overwrites stored settings with empty defaults
- **Where:** `settings-renderer.js:686` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Once the savers are exposed again, if getSettingsAll throws (for example during a profile switch), clicking into the filter textarea and out again saves [], wiping the user's log filters; the homepage is cleared the same way.
- **Fix:** Add `let settingsLoaded = false;`, set it to true just before setPersistState('saved') at the end of the try in init(). Return early from saveFiltersNow, saveHomepageNow, scheduleFiltersSave and scheduleHomepageSave while it is false. Optionally set spFilters and spHomepageUrl to disabled until it becomes true.

#### A-376 ⚪ The strict Settings CSP silently drops all inline style attributes _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `settings.html:5` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open Devices before the enumeration finishes (or when it fails): 'No cameras detected' is visible even though display:none was intended. The Performance table shows no type colors or CPU highlight.
- **Fix:** Add CSS classes in settings.css (.perf-type-browser/.perf-type-renderer/.perf-type-gpu/.perf-type-cupnet/.perf-type-other, .perf-cpu-high, .perf-empty-row) and emit class= instead of style= in renderPerfRows. Replace style="...display:none" on #sp-devices-empty with the hidden attribute plus a margin-top class, and toggle .hidden in renderCameraRows. Move the other inline style attributes in settings.html into classes.

### Browser UI

#### A-009 🔴 Every website in a tab gets the privileged electronAPI bridge: it can leak the real IP, read HttpOnly cookies, get LAN proxy credentials and navigate the active tab _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `preload-view.js:49` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A malicious page runs `await electronAPI.getDirectIp()` and receives the user's real IP and city while the user is behind a proxy profile. It gets its own id from `electronAPI.getTabs()`, then calls `electronAPI.getCookies(myId)` and receives every other site's session cookies from the shared jar. `electronAPI.extProxyList()` returns the ext-proxy password and LAN IP. From a background tab, `electronAPI.navigateTo('https://phish.example')` redirects the tab the user is looking at.
- **Fix:** In preload-view.js, expose electronAPI only when location.protocol === 'file:' and the pathname ends with /new-tab.html or /cupnet-guide.html. Everything else keeps only cupnetCredentials and cupnetStorageActivity. In main, add an isInternalPageSender(event) helper that checks event.senderFrame.url against getInternalPageUrl('new-tab'/'guide'), and require it (or assertTrustedShell) in get-direct-ip, get-tabs, get-cookies, clear-cookies, remove-cookie, set-cookie, ext-proxy:* , set-ui-pref/get-ui-pref, isolate-tab, set-tab-proxy, set-tab-cookie-group, connect-proxy-template and navigate-to. In navigate-to, resolve the tab from event.sender.id via tabManager.getTabIdByWebContentsId and navigate that tab. Only the trusted shell may pass file:// URLs. Whitelist the ui-pref keys that new-tab uses. In _emitTabListUpdated, send only to tabs whose URL is an internal page.
- _Reported 2× (also: x-security)_

#### A-015 🟠 get-app-metrics blocks the main process with execSync ps or PowerShell, and the shell calls it every 3s and on every tab update
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:1861` · **Category:** perf · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** On Windows, every status refresh blocks the Electron main process for the PowerShell startup time (hundreds of ms). While a page loads (tab-list-updated every ~80ms), the UI, IPC and MITM handling all stall repeatedly, so the app feels frozen. On macOS the block is shorter but still happens several times a second.
- **Fix:** In os-process-metrics.js replace execSync with a promisified execFile (for example `execFile('ps', ['-p', String(n), '-o', '%cpu=,rss='])` and `execFile('powershell', [...])`) and make getPidMetrics async. In diagnostics-ipc, await it, and keep a main-side cache of the child rows for about 3s so concurrent calls reuse one in-flight promise. In browser-renderer, remove `void refreshResourceMetrics()` from refreshBrowserStatusBar and call it only from the 3s interval, or only while _resourcesPopoverOpen for the per-process table.
- _Reported 3× (also: core-runtime, tool-windows-a)_

#### A-016 🟠 Credential fill popup is rendered under the native tab view, so multi-account fill and the empty-state popup are invisible
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:849` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A site has 2 or more saved logins and the vault is unlocked. Click Creds. handleCredentialsToolbarPrimaryClick calls showCredPopupList, but the list is drawn behind the page and nothing appears, so the user cannot pick an account. The same happens with 0 matches ('No saved credentials').
- **Fix:** When the popup is shown, call api.setWindowSwitcherOverlayVisible(true) (or add a generic setShellOverlayVisible), and call it with false in hideCredPopup. Better, render the account list in a small frameless child window like site-info-popover. Remove hideCredPopup from _onActiveTabChanged, or only call it when the active tab id actually changed. Delete showCredPopupUnlock, submitCredPopupUnlock and the unlock markup, or wire them into the locked branch.

#### A-017 🟠 Typed text in the address bar is overwritten whenever ANY tab changes title, favicon or URL
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:688` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A background tab is loading, or the active page is an SPA that updates its title or pushState. The user focuses the URL bar and types 'github.com/org'. About 80ms after the next title change the input is reset to the current URL mid-typing, and pressing Enter navigates to the old URL or a truncated query.
- **Fix:** In _scheduleTabUiUpdate keep a module-level `_lastActiveTabIdForUrl`. If active.id !== _lastActiveTabIdForUrl (a real tab switch), keep the current forced reset (`_urlInputDirty = false; _setToolbarUrl(normalized, { forceInput: true })`) and store the id. Otherwise call `_setToolbarUrlIfChanged(normalized, { respectFocus: true })`.

#### A-036 🟠 Settings button opens a Settings tab that cannot load or save (WIP removed the Settings APIs from preload-view) _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/tabs-ipc.js:22` · **Category:** ipc-contract · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click Settings in the toolbar, press Cmd+, or run the palette command 'Open Settings'. The tab opens, init throws `TypeError: api.getSettingsAll is not a function`, and no setting (search engine, toolbar placement, homepage, CA download) can be read or changed. Settings is the only UI for tool placement and search engine.
- **Fix:** Open settings.html in a trusted tool window that uses preload.js and registerTrustedShell (add an openSettingsWindow in sub-windows.js, similar to the other _spawnTrustedToolWindow callers), and point open-settings-tab / cupnet://settings / the homepage alias at it. Then guard the settings-toolbar-ipc handlers with guardIpcHandler, because they are currently open to any sender that can reach them.
- _Reported 2× (also: tool-windows-b)_

#### A-066 🟡 RAM in the status bar is wrong: Chromium rows are KB but are formatted as bytes
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:1517` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The browser process uses 300 MB (307200 KB). The table shows 'Main process … 0.3 MB', while the Go worker (40 MB in bytes) shows 40 MB, so the 'RAM total' is almost entirely the helper and understates real usage by roughly 1000x.
- **Fix:** In diagnostics-ipc.js map `memWorkingSet: (m.memory?.workingSetSize ?? 0) * 1024`, `memPrivate: (m.memory?.privateBytes ?? 0) * 1024` and `memShared: (m.memory?.sharedBytes ?? 0) * 1024` so every row is in bytes. Change settings-renderer.js fmtMb to `(Number(bytes || 0) / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)`.

#### A-067 🟡 localhost, single-label hosts and IPv6 literals are sent to the search engine instead of being opened
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:179` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A developer types 'localhost:3000' in the omnibox. The row shows 'Search DuckDuckGo for …', Enter sends the local host and port to DuckDuckGo, and the dev server never opens. Internal hostnames leak to a third party.
- **Fix:** In utils.resolveNavigationUrl, before the search fallback, add: `if (/^(localhost|\[[0-9a-f:.]+\])(:\d+)?(\/.*)?$/i.test(s) || /^[a-z0-9-]+:\d{1,5}(\/.*)?$/i.test(s) || /^[a-z0-9-]+\/\S*$/i.test(s)) { try { const u='http://'+s; new URL(u); return u; } catch {} }`. Also make the IPv4 branch accept an optional :port and use http for private/loopback hosts. Mirror the same test in browser-renderer.js _looksLikeUrlOrHost and in new-tab.html doSearch (use navigate('http://'+val)). Add unit tests for localhost, localhost:3000, [::1]:3000 and intranet/path.

#### A-139 🟡 Per-tab proxy is shown as 'Direct', and proxy-status-changed overwrites the correct tab name with the global one
- **Where:** `main-process/services/proxy-notify-broadcast.js:47` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** No global proxy, and the active tab has per-tab proxy profile 'DE-1'. The pill and status bar say 'Direct' and label the per-tab proxy's egress IP (checkIpGeo(tabId) resolves the tab upstream) as the direct IP. After any proxy-status-changed broadcast, even the global-proxy case loses the tab-specific name that get-current-proxy returned.
- **Fix:** In proxy-notify-broadcast.js:47, change the condition to `if (!tab || !tab.proxyProfileId)`. Add a tab-aware flag to both payloads, e.g. `tabActive: !!tab.proxyProfileId || base.active` in _augmentProxyStatusForTab and the equivalent in get-current-proxy. In browser-renderer.js _renderPill (and in the onProxyStatusChanged/getCurrentProxy callbacks that pick _fetchProxyIpGeo vs _fetchDirectIpGeo) and in the new-tab widget, use `info.tabActive ?? info.active` instead of `info.active`.

#### A-186 🟡 Launch Profile modal silently drops tab.isolated/cookieGroupId and proxy.ja3/profileName from imported profiles
- **Where:** `session-profile-modal-renderer.js:157` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — Launch Profile modal preserves tab/proxy fields via baseProfile.
- **Impact:** Export a request's launch profile with a custom JA3 and a cookie group, then open it in Launch Profile and click Launch. The profile's cookies go into the shared cookie jar (group 1), mixing identities, and the connection uses the default JA3 instead of the exported fingerprint.
- **Fix:** In session-profile-modal-renderer.js keep a module-level `baseProfile`, set to the validated profile in fillFormFromProfile. In buildProfileFromForm, set `tab: { ...(baseProfile?.tab || {}), newTab: !!els.newTab?.checked }`. When building profile.proxy, copy `ja3: baseProfile?.proxy?.ja3` and `profileName: baseProfile?.proxy?.profileName` when they exist. Better still, add a visible JA3 input (pf-proxy-ja3) and a cookie group id field, and add them to watchIds.
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-187 🟡 Shell toasts (errors, MITM failure, 'Filled…', rule notifications) are hidden behind the tab view
- **Where:** `shared-dark-theme.css:201` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** MITM fails to start. applyMitmState calls showToast('MITM failed: …', 12s), but the user sees nothing apart from a few pixels above the status bar. The same happens for 'Password saved to vault' and 'Session … loaded'.
- **Fix:** While toasts are visible, reserve bottom chrome: call api.setBottomChromeHeight(currentBottomReserve + toastStackHeight) from showToast in the shell, and restore it on dismiss. Alternatively, render shell toasts in the omnibox-style overlay WebContentsView or in a transparent, non-focusable child window anchored to the content bounds. A cheaper stopgap is to anchor #cn-toast-container and #rule-toast-container at the top of the chrome (top: var(--shell-chrome-top)), but that area is also covered by the view below 95px, so reserving space is the real fix.

#### A-203 ⚪ 'CupNet start page' quick link and 'Open start page' command use dead alias cupnet://newtab and do nothing
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:2787` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Type 'home' in the omnibox and pick 'CupNet start page', or run '> Open start page'. The palette closes and nothing happens.
- **Fix:** In tabs-ipc.js navigate-to, add `|| alias === 'cupnet://newtab' || alias === 'cupnet:newtab'` to the home/new-tab alias check. Alternatively, change both browser-renderer.js call sites to 'cupnet://home'.

#### A-204 ⚪ Window switcher ref-count leak leaves the tab content detached (blank page)
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:2717` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open the switcher with Ctrl+`, then press Cmd+K and run '> Window switcher' (or click the toolbar Switcher button while a hotkey open is still awaiting). The ref count is 2. Pick a window or press Esc and the count drops to 1, so the tab view stays detached and the page area is blank. switchTab also skips re-adding views while _shellOverlayHidesTabView is set, until some navigation calls ensureActiveTabViewVisible.
- **Fix:** Add a module flag `let _winSwitcherOpening = false;`. At the top of showWindowSwitcher, `if (_winSwitcherOpening || winSwitcherOverlay.classList.contains('win-switcher-overlay--open')) return;`. Set it to true before the first await and to false after the class is added (use try/finally). Also set `_winSwitcherShellHidden = true` only after setWindowSwitcherOverlayVisible(true) resolves. In hideWindowSwitcher, send false only when `_winSwitcherShellHidden` is true, then reset it. Toggle handlers should treat `_winSwitcherOpening` as open.

#### A-205 ⚪ mod/Alt+Enter navigates the CURRENT tab when the user cancels the 'too many tabs' confirmation
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:3290` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user has maxTabsBeforeWarning tabs open, types a URL and presses Cmd+Enter, then clicks Cancel in the confirmation dialog. The current tab navigates away and its page state is lost.
- **Fix:** At both sites: `const id = await api.newTab(); if (id != null) api.navigateTo(raw);`
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-206 ⚪ Enter in the omnibox leaves _urlInputDirty=true (masked only by the tab-list clobber bug)
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:3304` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** After fixing the tab-list clobber: type 'example.com' and press Enter. The page loads https://example.com/ but the address bar keeps showing the raw 'example.com' until blur.
- **Fix:** Set `_urlInputDirty = false;` before each `api.navigateTo(...)` in onCommandPaletteGlobalKeydown (shift, modEnter and plain raw branches) and in runOmniboxIndex for `row.kind === 'url'`. Optionally call urlInput.blur() as well.

#### A-207 ⚪ 'Show my public IP' toasts '[object Object]'; 'Check IP / Geo' gives no feedback
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:2859` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Run '> Show my public IP'. The toast text is '[object Object]', or 'unknown' is never shown on failure.
- **Fix:** `const r = await api.getDirectIp?.(); showToast(r?.ip ? `${r.ip} · ${[r.city, r.country].filter(Boolean).join(', ')}` : 'Could not determine public IP', { type: r?.ip ? 'info' : 'error' });` For Check IP / Geo: `api.checkIpGeo(activeTabId).then(g => showToast(g?.ip && g.ip !== 'unknown' ? `${g.ip} · ${[g.city, g.country].filter(Boolean).join(', ')}` : 'IP check failed', { type: g?.ip ? 'info' : 'error' })).catch(() => showToast('IP check failed', { type: 'error' }));`

#### A-208 ⚪ Omnibox 'Search <engine> for' label is stale: only set by the one-shot init-settings
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:162` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user sets Google in Settings. The omnibox still shows 'Search DuckDuckGo for …' while Enter searches Google. Right after startup the label is DuckDuckGo even when Google is configured.
- **Fix:** In initToolbarToolsPlacement, add `if (data?.searchEngine) _cupnetSearchEngineKey = String(data.searchEngine);`. Add `searchEngine: ctx.settingsStore.normalizeSearchEngineSettings(s).searchEngine` to the broadcastBrowserChromeSettings payload, and call broadcastBrowserChromeSettings(ctx) at the end of the save-search-engine-settings handler. In onBrowserSettingsUpdated (browser-renderer.js:1351), add `if (data?.searchEngine) _cupnetSearchEngineKey = String(data.searchEngine);`.

#### A-209 ⚪ MITM error text is sticky: after recovery the restart button stays visible forever
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:1923` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** MITM fails once, then the user restarts it successfully. Status shows 'MITM: Active', but the red restart button remains permanently, and the old error is kept for later tooltips.
- **Fix:** At :1923, use `const error = (info && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(info, 'error')) ? String(info.error || '').trim() : String(_mitmUiError || '').trim();`, or additionally clear it when state === 'ready' (`if (state === 'ready') error = '';` with error declared via let).

#### A-210 ⚪ A single click on 'MITM: Active' restarts the proxy with no confirmation
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:2006` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user clicks the status bar to read the tooltip or by accident while a long upload or websocket session is running. The connections drop.
- **Fix:** At :2006, use `statusMitm.addEventListener('click', () => { if (_mitmUiState === 'ready' && !_mitmUiError && !confirm('Restart MITM proxy? Active connections will be dropped and the current tab reloaded.')) return; void _requestMitmRestart(); });`, or only restart when _mitmUiState !== 'ready'. Update the ready-state title at :1939 to match.

#### A-211 ⚪ Blurring the URL bar (including switching to another app) discards what the user typed
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:3232` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Type half a URL, Cmd+Tab to another app to copy a path, and come back: the typed text is replaced by the old URL.
- **Fix:** At the start of the dirty-revert part of the blur handler, defer the check and skip it when the window itself lost focus: setTimeout(() => { if (!document.hasFocus()) return; /* existing revert */ }, 0). Or keep the draft in a variable and restore it on window 'focus'. Keep the explicit Esc revert.

#### A-212 ⚪ Omnibox hint and the guide promise Tab-completion and inline autocomplete that do not exist
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:2875` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user presses Tab as instructed. The suggestion list closes and the typed text is reverted.
- **Fix:** Remove ' · Tab complete' from the string at browser-renderer.js:2875, or add a Tab branch in onCommandPaletteGlobalKeydown that fills urlInput with omniboxRows[commandPaletteActiveIdx]'s URL and calls preventDefault. In scripts/guide-i18n/{en,fr,es,ru}.mjs, remove the inline-autocomplete sentence and the Tab (inline) row, replace '+🔒' with Ctrl+Shift+T / File > New Isolated Tab, and say that isolated data persists until the cookie group is deleted. Then run npm run build:guide.

#### A-213 ⚪ Ctrl+1..9, Esc-to-stop and Cmd+, only work while the shell has focus
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:2246` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click into a page and press Ctrl+2: nothing happens. Press Esc during a slow load: loading does not stop.
- **Fix:** In attachWindowSwitcherHotkeyToTabWebContents, handle keyDown with (control||meta) && !shift && !alt && /^[1-9]$/.test(input.key): call event.preventDefault() and mw.webContents.send('switch-tab-index', n), with a matching api.onSwitchTabIndex in preload/browser-renderer that reuses the index logic. Add CmdOrCtrl+, to open settings the same way (or as a menu accelerator 'CmdOrCtrl+,'). For a bare Escape, do not preventDefault, so pages still get it; just call tabManager active tab webContents.stop() when it isLoading().

#### A-214 ⚪ Credential save bar 'Never' behaves exactly like dismiss (no never-save list)
- **Where:** `browser-renderer.js:1413` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user clicks 'Never' on example.com. The next login on example.com shows the save bar again.
- **Fix:** Add handle('credential-capture-never', ...) that records _pendingCapture.domain in a per-profile never-save set (settings store or a small db table), clears _pendingCapture and returns { success: true }. In credential-form-captured, return before sending show-credential-save-bar when the domain is in that set. Expose credentialCaptureNever in preload.js and call it from the #cred-save-never handler. The minimal alternative is to rename or remove the 'Never' button.

#### A-281 ⚪ Site-info popover shows the GLOBAL proxy profile's TLS/JA3, not the tab's per-tab profile
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/misc-ipc.js:45` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The active tab uses per-tab profile 'Firefox-DE' while the global profile is 'Chrome-US'. The popover reports TLS profile chrome and 'Proxy profile Chrome-US', which is wrong for the fingerprint actually used.
- **Fix:** In buildTabTlsInfo use `const pid = tab.proxyProfileId || ctx.connectedProfileId;`. Set out.connectedProfileId = pid and out.connectedProfileName = prof?.name || (tab.proxyProfileId ? `#${pid}` : ctx.connectedProfileName). Optionally add `out.perTabProfile = !!tab.proxyProfileId`.

#### A-315 ⚪ Site-info button cannot close the popover (blur-close then reopen), and popovers are offset by the title-bar height
- **Where:** `main-process/services/sub-windows.js:812` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Click the lock icon to open site info, then click it again to close: it flickers and reopens. The popover overlaps the address bar instead of sitting below it. The logging modal can end up partly off-screen when REC is near the right edge.
- **Fix:** In both functions use `const cb = d.mainWindow.getContentBounds();` as the origin (x = cb.x + hint.x, y = cb.y + hint.y + hint.h + 8), drop the +200/+56 fudges in createLoggingModalWindow, and clamp to `screen.getDisplayMatching({x,y,width:W,height:H}).workArea`. In the blur handler record `d.siteInfoPopoverBlurClosedAt = Date.now()` and its tabId. In toggle-site-info-popover, return { ok:true, action:'closed' } without reopening when the same tabId was blur-closed less than 300ms ago.

#### A-334 ⚪ Logging modal: after a re-init, hover no longer cancels the 5s auto-confirm
- **Where:** `modal-logging.html:247` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open the modal, hover it (which consumes the listener), then click REC again. The countdown restarts and auto-starts logging in 'continue' mode after 5s while the user is still reading the options.
- **Fix:** Remove {once:true} from the mouseenter listener; cancelTimer is already idempotent through `cancelled`. In startCountdown(), also call progressTrack.classList.remove('hidden'); countdownEl.classList.remove('hidden'); countdownEl.textContent = AUTO_SECS + 's'. Otherwise, skip restarting the countdown when the modal is re-initialised.

#### A-335 ⚪ new-tab: every tab-list-updated from any tab triggers get-proxy-profiles, 2x get-tabs and a full cookie-jar read in every open new-tab page
- **Where:** `new-tab.html:2412` · **Category:** perf · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Five new-tab pages are open while a heavy SPA loads (dozens of title and favicon updates). Main handles 5 x 4 IPCs per broadcast plus repeated full cookie-jar enumerations with thousands of cookies, which slows the main process during page loads.
- **Fix:** In applyTabList, return early for the loadNtProxyProfiles/getTabs/cookie-bar block when document.hidden, since visibilitychange at 2486-2492 already re-syncs on show. Also wrap that block in `if (tabSigChanged)`, and use `active` from the broadcast payload instead of calling getTabs again. Keep the cookie-bar dispatch only on tabSigChanged or visibilitychange.

#### A-336 ⚪ new-tab: startup splash timing reads _startupSplashDelay before the async consumeStartupSplash resolves
- **Where:** `new-tab.html:2313` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** On a cold start where the IPC round-trip finishes after window load, the splash flashes or never appears. The IP and ext-proxy checks meant to wait for the splash run during it.
- **Fix:** Keep the promise, e.g. `const _splashReady = Promise.resolve(window.electronAPI?.consumeStartupSplash?.()).then(...)...`. Then replace the two timers with `_splashReady.then(() => setTimeout(fn, _startupSplashDelay))` (lines 2313 and 2876). Drop the dead `if (_startupSplashDelay === 0)` guard at 2331, or move it inside the chain.

#### A-337 ⚪ new-tab: the External proxy LAN/This PC selection resets to 'This PC' every 5 seconds
- **Where:** `new-tab.html:2826` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user selects 'LAN' to copy the LAN proxy URL. Within 5 seconds the URL and firewall hint switch back to 127.0.0.1, so the user may copy the wrong address.
- **Fix:** In showOn, remember the previous port and hasLan before overwriting epEndpoints. Replace lines 2826-2827 with `const keep = epActiveTarget === 'lan' && epEndpoints.hasLan && prevPort === port; setEpTarget(keep ? 'lan' : 'local');`.

#### A-338 ⚪ new-tab: MITM mode badge is hardcoded, and the search engine is separate from Settings
- **Where:** `new-tab.html:2209` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** In direct traffic mode the widget still says 'MITM'. A user who set Google or a custom engine in Settings gets DuckDuckGo from the start-page search box, and a different engine again in each isolated tab.
- **Fix:** Leave the badge as is, and optionally remove the dead eMode !== 'mitm' branch in the ext-proxy refresh. For search, expose a narrow read-only 'get-search-engine' IPC in preload-view returning {searchEngine, searchEngineCustomUrl}. Use it to pick the initial engine, and build the URL the same way the omnibox does (supporting brave and custom). Persist the per-click choice with setUiPref (already exposed at preload-view.js:88-89) instead of partition localStorage.

#### A-377 ⚪ Site-info 'Clear cookies' misses parent-domain cookies and gives no feedback
- **Where:** `site-info-popover.html:131` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** On www.example.com, where the login cookie is set on .example.com, clicking 'Clear cookies' leaves the session cookie, and the user believes the site's cookies were cleared.
- **Fix:** In misc-ipc.js site-info-popover-clear-cookies: call cookies.get({}), then filter with cookieMatchesDomainFilter(c, host), plus a check that host equals the cookie domain or ends with '.'+cookie domain (so parent domains like .example.com match). Remove each match with removeSessionCookiePrecise(sess, {...c, url: buildCookieRequestUrl(c)}). In site-info-popover.html, await api.clearCookies(...) and show 'Removed N cookies', or the error, in the popover body.

#### A-380 ⚪ Omnibox overlay race: a hide during the setProxy await leaves an orphan full-screen overlay (dark veil, swallowed clicks)
- **Where:** `tab-manager.js:1255` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Press Ctrl+T (switchTab destroys the overlay), click the URL bar and immediately press Esc, or paste a URL and press Enter within a few ms. The page is dimmed by a 42% black layer with no dropdown until the user clicks, and the click is swallowed. With the double-open race the veil never goes away.
- **Fix:** In tab-manager.js, add `let _omniboxWanted = false; let _omniboxShowPromise = null;`. showOmniboxOverlay sets `_omniboxWanted = true` and, if `_omniboxShowPromise` is set, returns it. Otherwise it wraps the creation in `_omniboxShowPromise` and clears it in finally. After the setProxy await, `if (!_omniboxWanted) return false;` comes before creating and attaching. hideOmniboxOverlay sets `_omniboxWanted = false` first. In omnibox-overlay.html, make `#backdrop` transparent by default (or add class="transparent" in the markup). In setWindowSwitcherOverlayVisible(true), after _destroyOmniboxOverlayView(), send 'force-close-omnibox' to mainWindow.webContents, as switchTab does.
- _Reported 2× (also: tabs-shell-main)_

### Data & logging

#### A-001 🔴 Encrypt mode writes plaintext headers/bodies next to the ciphertext
- **Where:** `db.js:1062` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Unlock a profile vault (the default trafficLogEncryption='encrypt'), then log in to a site. Run `SELECT request_headers, request_body FROM requests` on cupnet.db: the Authorization/Cookie headers and form passwords are there in cleartext, and requests_fts shadow tables hold the body tokens.
- **Fix:** In db.js `_prepareLogEntry`, remove the camelCase fields before merging: `const { requestHeaders, responseHeaders, requestBody, responseBody, ...rest } = entry; return { ...rest, ...prepareRequestLogFields(entry) };`. insertRequest then writes only the prepared snake_case values. Add a one-time migration: `UPDATE requests SET request_headers=NULL, response_headers=NULL, request_body=NULL, response_body=NULL WHERE log_encrypted=1 AND (request_headers_enc IS NOT NULL OR ...)` (only null a column when its _enc counterpart is present). Then run `INSERT INTO requests_fts(requests_fts) VALUES('rebuild')`.

#### A-018 🟠 Balanced redaction (the vault-locked/redact fallback) leaves JSON credentials in plaintext
